<file path="public/sw.js">
self.addEventListener('push', (event) => {
  const data = event.data?.json() ?? {}

  const title = data.title ?? 'AD Ceuta FC'
  const options = {
    body: data.body ?? '',
    icon: '/icon-512.png',
    badge: '/escudo.png',
    tag: data.tag ?? 'adceuta',
    renotify: true,
    data: { url: data.url ?? '/' },
  }

  event.waitUntil(self.registration.showNotification(title, options))
})

self.addEventListener('notificationclick', (event) => {
  event.notification.close()
  const targetUrl = event.notification.data?.url ?? '/'

  event.waitUntil(
    clients.matchAll({ type: 'window', includeUncontrolled: true }).then((windowClients) => {
      for (const client of windowClients) {
        if (client.url.includes(self.location.origin) && 'focus' in client) {
          client.focus()
          return client.navigate(targetUrl)
        }
      }
      return clients.openWindow(targetUrl)
    })
  )
})

<file path="scripts/sync.sh">
#!/bin/bash
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."

echo "🚀 Arrancando servidor..."
pnpm dev &> /tmp/funhub-sync.log &
SERVER_PID=$!

# Esperar hasta que el servidor responda
for i in $(seq 1 30); do
  sleep 1
  if curl -s http://localhost:3000/api/standings > /dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "✅ Servidor listo"
    break
  fi
done

echo "🔄 Sincronizando datos..."
RESULT=$(curl -s http://localhost:3000/api/sync)
echo "$RESULT" | python3 -m json.tool 2>/dev/null || echo "$RESULT"

echo "🛑 Cerrando servidor..."
kill $SERVER_PID 2>/dev/null
wait $SERVER_PID 2>/dev/null

echo "✅ Sync completado"
</file>

<file path="src/app/admin/login/page.tsx">
'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'

export default function AdminLoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    setError('')
    const res = await fetch('/api/admin/auth', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ password }),
    })
    if (res.ok) {
      router.replace('/admin')
    } else {
      setError('Contraseña incorrecta')
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        minHeight: '100vh',
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',
        background: 'var(--bg-soft)',
        padding: 16,
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          background: 'var(--white)',
          borderRadius: 20,
          padding: 32,
          width: '100%',
          maxWidth: 360,
          boxShadow: '0 4px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.08)',
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            fontSize: 11,
            fontWeight: 700,
            textTransform: 'uppercase',
            letterSpacing: '0.1em',
            color: 'var(--red)',
            marginBottom: 4,
          }}
        >
          Panel de administración
        </div>
        <div
          style={{
            fontSize: 24,
            fontWeight: 900,
            textTransform: 'uppercase',
            letterSpacing: '-0.02em',
            color: 'var(--black)',
            marginBottom: 24,
          }}
        >
          AD Ceuta FC
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <input
            type="password"
            placeholder="Contraseña"
            value={password}
            onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
            autoFocus
            style={{
              width: '100%',
              padding: '12px 16px',
              borderRadius: 12,
              border: `1.5px solid ${error ? 'var(--red)' : 'var(--border)'}`,
              fontSize: 16,
              outline: 'none',
              marginBottom: 8,
              boxSizing: 'border-box',
              background: 'var(--bg-soft)',
            }}
          />
          {error && (
            <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--red)', margin: '0 0 12px', fontWeight: 600 }}>
              {error}
            </p>
          )}
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading || !password}
            style={{
              width: '100%',
              padding: '13px 0',
              borderRadius: 12,
              border: 'none',
              background: loading ? 'var(--bg-mid)' : 'var(--black)',
              color: 'var(--white)',
              fontSize: 14,
              fontWeight: 700,
              textTransform: 'uppercase',
              letterSpacing: '0.05em',
              cursor: loading ? 'default' : 'pointer',
            }}
          >
            {loading ? 'Entrando...' : 'Entrar'}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

<file path="src/app/alineacion/page.tsx">
'use client'

import { useEffect, useState, Suspense } from 'react'
import { useSearchParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { MatchLineup } from '@/app/api/matches/lineup/route'
import { LineupField } from '@/features/matches/LineupField'

function LineupContent() {
  const params = useSearchParams()
  const router = useRouter()
  const eventId = params.get('eventId')
  const matchLabel = params.get('match') ?? 'Alineación'
  const score = params.get('score') // e.g. "1-0"

  const [lineup, setLineup] = useState<MatchLineup | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!eventId) {
      setError(true)
      setLoading(false)
      return
    }
    fetch(`/api/matches/lineup?eventId=${eventId}`)
      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : null))
      .then((d) => {
        if (d?.available) setLineup(d)
        else setError(true)
      })
      .catch(() => setError(true))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false))
  }, [eventId])

  return (
    <div className="page">
        {/* ── HERO ── */}
        <div className="relative overflow-hidden bg-ceuta-black px-[18px] pb-[52px] pt-[22px] text-white">
          {/* rayas — sólo esquina derecha, no invaden el texto */}
          <div
            className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 right-0 top-0 w-[120px]"
            style={{
              background: 'repeating-linear-gradient(-45deg, white 0 14px, #0A0A0A 14px 28px)',
              clipPath: 'polygon(60px 0, 100% 0, 100% 100%, 0 100%)',
            }}
          />

          {/* todo el contenido con z-10 para quedar por encima de las rayas */}
          <div className="relative z-10">
            <button
              onClick={() => router.back()}
              className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ceuta-red"
            >
              <svg
                width="14"
                height="14"
                viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                fill="none"
                stroke="currentColor"
                strokeWidth="2.5"
                strokeLinecap="round"
                strokeLinejoin="round"
              >
                <path d="M19 12H5M12 5l-7 7 7 7" />
              </svg>
              Volver
            </button>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-ceuta-red">
              <div className="h-0.5 w-[18px] bg-ceuta-red" />
              Once inicial{score && <span className="text-ceuta-gold"> · {score.replace('-', '–')}</span>}
            </div>

            <div className="mt-2 pr-[70px] font-display text-[32px] font-black uppercase leading-[0.95]">
              {matchLabel.includes(' vs ') ? (
                <>
                  {matchLabel.split(' vs ')[0]}
                  <em className="not-italic text-ceuta-gold"> vs </em>
                  {matchLabel.split(' vs ')[1]}
                </>
              ) : (
                matchLabel
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ── CONTENT ── */}
        <div className="relative z-[2] -mt-7 mx-3">
          {loading && (
            <div className="rounded-[14px] border-2 border-ceuta-black bg-white p-8 text-center">
              <div className="font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ceuta-muted">
                Cargando alineación…
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && error && (
            <div className="rounded-[14px] border-2 border-ceuta-black bg-white p-8 text-center">
              <div className="text-4xl mb-3">📋</div>
              <div className="font-display text-[16px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.04em] text-ceuta-black">
                Alineación no disponible
              </div>
              <div className="mt-1.5 font-display text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-ceuta-muted">
                Se publicará ~30 min antes del partido
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && lineup && <LineupField lineup={lineup} />}
        </div>

        <div className="h-6" />
      </div>
  )
}

export default function AlineacionPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense>
      <LineupContent />
    </Suspense>
  )
}

<file path="src/app/api/admin/auth/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { createHash } from 'crypto'

function deriveToken(password: string): string {
  return createHash('sha256').update(password + 'funhub-admin-v1').digest('hex')
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const { password } = await request.json()

  if (!process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || password !== process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Contraseña incorrecta' }, { status: 401 })
  }

  const token = deriveToken(process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD)
  const res = NextResponse.json({ ok: true })
  res.cookies.set('admin_session', token, {
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',
    sameSite: 'lax',
    maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 30,
    path: '/',
  })
  return res
}
</file>

<file path="src/app/api/admin/logout/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'

export async function POST() {
  const res = NextResponse.json({ ok: true })
  res.cookies.set('admin_session', '', { maxAge: 0, path: '/' })
  return res
}

<file path="src/app/api/admin/migrate-images/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { createHash } from 'crypto'
import { cookies } from 'next/headers'
import { supabase } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/client'
import {
  downloadAndUploadImage,
  isStoredNewsImage,
  newsImageFilename,
} from '@/infrastructure/storage/imageUploader'
import { fetchOgImage } from '@/infrastructure/rss/fetchOgImage'

function deriveToken(password: string): string {
  return createHash('sha256').update(password + 'funhub-admin-v1').digest('hex')
}

async function checkAuth(): Promise<boolean> {
  const cookieStore = await cookies()
  const session = cookieStore.get('admin_session')?.value
  const password = process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD
  if (!session || !password) return false
  return session === deriveToken(password)
}

export const maxDuration = 60

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  if (!(await checkAuth())) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })
  }

  const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}))
  const batchSize = 10
  const offset = (body.offset as number) ?? 0

  // Fetch articles without a stored image (null or external URL)
  const { data: rows, error } = await supabase
    .from('news')
    .select('id, external_id, source, url, image_url')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .range(offset, offset + batchSize - 1)

  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })

  let migrated = 0
  let failed = 0
  let skipped = 0

  for (const row of rows ?? []) {
    // Already in Supabase Storage — skip
    if (isStoredNewsImage(row.image_url)) { skipped++; continue }

    // Determine source URL: use existing image_url or fetch OG from article URL
    let sourceUrl: string | null = row.image_url ?? null
    if (!sourceUrl && row.url) {
      sourceUrl = await fetchOgImage(row.url)
    }

    if (!sourceUrl) { failed++; continue }

    const filename = newsImageFilename(row.source, row.external_id)
    const storedUrl = await downloadAndUploadImage(sourceUrl, filename)
    if (storedUrl) {
      await supabase.from('news').update({ image_url: storedUrl }).eq('id', row.id)
      migrated++
    } else {
      failed++
    }
  }

  const hasMore = (rows ?? []).length === batchSize
  return NextResponse.json({
    ok: true,
    offset,
    migrated,
    failed,
    skipped,
    hasMore,
    nextOffset: offset + batchSize,
  })
}
</file>

<file path="src/app/api/admin/transfers/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { createHash } from 'crypto'
import { cookies } from 'next/headers'
import {
  createTransfer,
  deleteTransfer,
  updateTransfer,
  getTransfers,
} from '@/infrastructure/supabase/TransfersRepository'

function deriveToken(password: string): string {
  return createHash('sha256').update(password + 'funhub-admin-v1').digest('hex')
}

async function checkAuth(): Promise<boolean> {
  const cookieStore = await cookies()
  const session = cookieStore.get('admin_session')?.value
  const password = process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD
  if (!session || !password) return false
  return session === deriveToken(password)
}

export async function GET() {
  if (!(await checkAuth())) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })
  const transfers = await getTransfers()
  return NextResponse.json({ transfers })
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  if (!(await checkAuth())) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })
  const body = await request.json()
  const transfer = await createTransfer(body)
  if (!transfer) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to create' }, { status: 500 })
  return NextResponse.json({ transfer })
}

export async function PATCH(request: Request) {
  if (!(await checkAuth())) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })
  const body = await request.json()
  const { id, ...data } = body
  if (!id) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Missing id' }, { status: 400 })
  await updateTransfer(id, data)
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true })
}

export async function DELETE(request: Request) {
  if (!(await checkAuth())) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })
  const { id } = await request.json()
  if (!id) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Missing id' }, { status: 400 })
  await deleteTransfer(id)
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true })
}

<file path="src/app/api/admin/trigger/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { createHash } from 'crypto'
import { cookies } from 'next/headers'

function deriveToken(password: string): string {
  return createHash('sha256').update(password + 'funhub-admin-v1').digest('hex')
}

async function checkAuth(): Promise<boolean> {
  const cookieStore = await cookies()
  const session = cookieStore.get('admin_session')?.value
  const password = process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD
  if (!session || !password) return false
  return session === deriveToken(password)
}

// Enqueue via QStash so Vercel timeout doesn't cut the response
async function enqueueViaQStash(path: string): Promise<{ queued: boolean }> {
  const base = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL ?? 'https://adceutainfo.vercel.app'
  const token = process.env.QSTASH_TOKEN
  if (!token) return { queued: false }

  const res = await fetch(`https://qstash.upstash.io/v2/publish/${base}${path}`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    },
    body: '{}',
  })
  return { queued: res.ok }
}

const ACTIONS: Record<string, () => Promise<Record<string, unknown>>> = {
  news: async () => enqueueViaQStash('/api/cron/news'),
  matches: async () => enqueueViaQStash('/api/cron/matches'),
  'match-day': async () => enqueueViaQStash('/api/cron/match-day'),
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  if (!(await checkAuth())) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })
  }

  const body = await request.json()
  const { action, payload } = body as {
    action: string
    payload?: { title: string; body: string; tag: string; url: string }
  }

  // Custom push notification
  if (action === 'push' && payload) {
    const base = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL ?? 'http://localhost:3000'
    const res = await fetch(`${base}/api/push/send`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      cache: 'no-store',
    })
    return NextResponse.json(await res.json())
  }

  const handler = ACTIONS[action]
  if (!handler) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: `Unknown action: ${action}` }, { status: 400 })
  }

  const result = await handler()
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, action, result })
}

<file path="src/app/api/admin/upload-photo/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
import { cookies } from 'next/headers'
import { createHash } from 'crypto'

const supabaseAdmin = createClient(
  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!
)

function deriveToken(password: string): string {
  return createHash('sha256').update(password + 'funhub-admin-v1').digest('hex')
}

async function isAuthed(): Promise<boolean> {
  const cookieStore = await cookies()
  const token = cookieStore.get('admin_session')?.value
  if (!token || !process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD) return false
  return token === deriveToken(process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD)
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  if (!(await isAuthed())) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })
  }

  const formData = await request.formData()
  const file = formData.get('file') as File | null
  const name = (formData.get('name') as string | null) ?? 'player'

  if (!file) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'No file' }, { status: 400 })

  const buffer = Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer())

  // Convert to WebP
  const sharp = (await import('sharp')).default
  const webpBuffer = await sharp(buffer)
    .resize(400, 400, { fit: 'cover', position: 'top' })
    .webp({ quality: 80 })
    .toBuffer()

  const slug = name.toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, '').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, '')
  const path = `transfers/${slug}-${Date.now()}.webp`

  const { error } = await supabaseAdmin.storage.from('news-images').upload(path, webpBuffer, {
    contentType: 'image/webp',
    cacheControl: '31536000',
    upsert: true,
  })

  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })

  const { data } = supabaseAdmin.storage.from('news-images').getPublicUrl(path)
  return NextResponse.json({ url: data.publicUrl })
}

<file path="src/app/api/cron/backfill-images/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { Receiver } from '@upstash/qstash'
import { NewsSupabaseRepository } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/NewsSupabaseRepository'
import { downloadAndUploadImage, isStoredNewsImage, newsImageFilename } from '@/infrastructure/storage/imageUploader'

export const maxDuration = 60

const repo = new NewsSupabaseRepository()

async function run() {
  // Fetch articles that still have external image URLs (not yet on Supabase Storage)
  const rows = await repo.getRowsWithExternalImages(15)
  let optimized = 0

  await Promise.all(
    rows.map(async (row) => {
      const storedUrl = await downloadAndUploadImage(
        row.imageUrl,
        newsImageFilename(row.source, row.externalId ?? row.id)
      )
      if (!storedUrl) return
      await repo.updateImageUrlById(row.id, storedUrl)
      optimized++
    })
  )

  return { optimized, pending: Math.max(0, rows.length - optimized) }
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
  const secret = searchParams.get('secret')

  if (secret === process.env.TEST_SECRET) {
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, ...(await run()) })
  }

  const receiver = new Receiver({
    currentSigningKey: process.env.QSTASH_CURRENT_SIGNING_KEY!,
    nextSigningKey: process.env.QSTASH_NEXT_SIGNING_KEY!,
  })

  const body = await request.text()
  const valid = await receiver
    .verify({ signature: request.headers.get('Upstash-Signature') ?? '', body })
    .catch(() => false)

  if (!valid) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })

  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, ...(await run()) })
}

<file path="src/app/api/cron/live/__tests__/detectEvents.test.ts">
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'
import { detectEvents } from '../detectEvents'
import type { MatchState } from '../detectEvents'
import type { LiveMatch } from '@/app/api/live/route'

function makeMatch(overrides: Partial<LiveMatch> = {}): LiveMatch {
  return {
    espnId: '1',
    state: 'in',
    status: 'En juego',
    clock: "45'",
    homeTeam: 'AD Ceuta FC',
    awayTeam: 'Albacete BP',
    homeScore: 0,
    awayScore: 0,
    events: [],
    venue: 'Estadio Alfonso Murube',
    startDate: '2026-05-25T17:00:00Z',
    ...overrides,
  }
}

function makeState(overrides: Partial<MatchState> = {}): MatchState {
  return {
    espnId: '1',
    homeScore: 0,
    awayScore: 0,
    state: 'in',
    eventCount: 0,
    ...overrides,
  }
}

describe('detectEvents — kickoff', () => {
  it('notifies kickoff when match transitions from pre to in', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({ state: 'in' })
    const prev = makeState({ state: 'pre' })
    const result = detectEvents(match, prev)
    expect(result).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(result[0].tag).toBe('kickoff')
    expect(result[0].title).toContain('en marcha')
  })

  it('notifies kickoff when there is no previous state', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({ state: 'in' })
    const result = detectEvents(match, null)
    expect(result[0].tag).toBe('kickoff')
  })

  it('does not notify kickoff if match was already in', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({ state: 'in' })
    const prev = makeState({ state: 'in' })
    const result = detectEvents(match, prev)
    expect(result.find((n) => n.tag === 'kickoff')).toBeUndefined()
  })
})

describe('detectEvents — goals', () => {
  it('notifies Ceuta goal when Ceuta is home and home score increases', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({ homeScore: 1, awayScore: 0 })
    const prev = makeState({ homeScore: 0, awayScore: 0 })
    const result = detectEvents(match, prev)
    expect(result).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(result[0].title).toContain('GOOOOOL DE CEUTA')
    expect(result[0].tag).toBe('goal-home-1')
  })

  it('notifies goal against when rival scores and Ceuta is home', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({ homeScore: 0, awayScore: 1 })
    const prev = makeState({ homeScore: 0, awayScore: 0 })
    const result = detectEvents(match, prev)
    expect(result[0].title).toContain('Gol en contra')
  })

  it('notifies Ceuta goal when Ceuta is away and away score increases', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({
      homeTeam: 'Albacete BP',
      awayTeam: 'AD Ceuta FC',
      homeScore: 0,
      awayScore: 1,
    })
    const prev = makeState({ homeScore: 0, awayScore: 0 })
    const result = detectEvents(match, prev)
    expect(result[0].title).toContain('GOOOOOL DE CEUTA')
  })

  it('notifies goal against when rival scores and Ceuta is away', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({
      homeTeam: 'Albacete BP',
      awayTeam: 'AD Ceuta FC',
      homeScore: 1,
      awayScore: 0,
    })
    const prev = makeState({ homeScore: 0, awayScore: 0 })
    const result = detectEvents(match, prev)
    expect(result[0].title).toContain('Gol en contra')
  })

  it('includes scorer name when available', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({
      homeScore: 1,
      events: [{ type: 'goal', team: 'home', minute: "23'", playerName: 'Juanito' }],
    })
    const prev = makeState({ homeScore: 0 })
    const result = detectEvents(match, prev)
    expect(result[0].body).toContain('Juanito')
  })

  it('notifies multiple goals in one polling cycle', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({ homeScore: 2, awayScore: 0 })
    const prev = makeState({ homeScore: 0, awayScore: 0 })
    const result = detectEvents(match, prev)
    expect(result).toHaveLength(2)
    expect(result.every((n) => n.title.includes('GOOOOOL'))).toBe(true)
  })

  it('does not notify goals when match is pre', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({ state: 'pre', homeScore: 0, awayScore: 0 })
    const prev = makeState({ state: 'pre' })
    const result = detectEvents(match, prev)
    expect(result.find((n) => n.tag.startsWith('goal'))).toBeUndefined()
  })
})

describe('detectEvents — red cards', () => {
  it('notifies red card for Ceuta player', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({
      events: [{ type: 'redCard', team: 'home', minute: "55'", playerName: 'López' }],
    })
    const prev = makeState({ eventCount: 0 })
    const result = detectEvents(match, prev)
    expect(result.find((n) => n.title.includes('quedamos con 10'))).toBeDefined()
  })

  it('notifies red card for rival player', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({
      events: [{ type: 'redCard', team: 'away', minute: "60'", playerName: 'Martínez' }],
    })
    const prev = makeState({ eventCount: 0 })
    const result = detectEvents(match, prev)
    expect(result.find((n) => n.title.includes('Roja para el rival'))).toBeDefined()
  })

  it('only notifies new red cards (not already seen events)', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({
      events: [
        { type: 'redCard', team: 'away', minute: "30'", playerName: 'Old' },
        { type: 'redCard', team: 'away', minute: "60'", playerName: 'New' },
      ],
    })
    const prev = makeState({ eventCount: 1 }) // first card already seen
    const result = detectEvents(match, prev)
    const redCards = result.filter((n) => n.tag.startsWith('red-'))
    expect(redCards).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(redCards[0].body).toContain('New')
  })
})

describe('detectEvents — fulltime', () => {
  it('notifies victory when Ceuta wins', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({ state: 'post', homeScore: 2, awayScore: 1 })
    const prev = makeState({ state: 'in', homeScore: 2, awayScore: 1 })
    const result = detectEvents(match, prev)
    expect(result[0].title).toContain('VICTORIA')
    expect(result[0].tag).toBe('fulltime')
  })

  it('notifies defeat when Ceuta loses', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({ state: 'post', homeScore: 0, awayScore: 2 })
    const prev = makeState({ state: 'in', homeScore: 0, awayScore: 2 })
    const result = detectEvents(match, prev)
    expect(result[0].title).toContain('Derrota')
  })

  it('notifies draw', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({ state: 'post', homeScore: 1, awayScore: 1 })
    const prev = makeState({ state: 'in', homeScore: 1, awayScore: 1 })
    const result = detectEvents(match, prev)
    expect(result[0].title).toContain('Empate')
  })

  it('does not notify fulltime if previous state was not in', () => {
    const match = makeMatch({ state: 'post', homeScore: 1, awayScore: 0 })
    const prev = makeState({ state: 'pre' })
    const result = detectEvents(match, prev)
    expect(result.find((n) => n.tag === 'fulltime')).toBeUndefined()
  })
})

<file path="src/app/api/cron/live/detectEvents.ts">
import type { LiveMatch, LiveEvent } from '@/app/api/live/route'

export interface MatchState {
  espnId: string
  homeScore: number
  awayScore: number
  state: string
  eventCount: number
  events?: import('@/app/api/live/route').LiveEvent[]
}

export interface PushNotification {
  title: string
  body: string
  tag: string
}

export function detectEvents(match: LiveMatch, prev: MatchState | null): PushNotification[] {
  const notifications: PushNotification[] = []
  const ceutaIsHome = match.homeTeam.toLowerCase().includes('ceuta')
  const rival = ceutaIsHome ? match.awayTeam : match.homeTeam
  const score = `${match.homeScore}-${match.awayScore}`

  // Match just started
  if ((!prev || prev.state === 'pre') && match.state === 'in') {
    notifications.push({
      title: '⚽ ¡Ya estamos en marcha, caballas!',
      body: `Hoy nos enfrentamos al ${rival}. 90 minutos de lucha. ¡Vamos Ceuta! 🖤⚪`,
      tag: 'kickoff',
    })
  }

  // Goals
  if (prev && match.state === 'in') {
    const homeGoals = match.homeScore - prev.homeScore
    const awayGoals = match.awayScore - prev.awayScore

    for (let i = 0; i < homeGoals; i++) {
      const ceutaScored = ceutaIsHome
      const scorer = match.events
        .filter((e: LiveEvent) => e.type === 'goal' && e.team === 'home')
        .pop()
      if (ceutaScored) {
        notifications.push({
          title: '🚨 ¡¡GOOOOOL DE CEUTA!! 🔥',
          body: `¡${scorer?.playerName ?? 'GOOOL'}! ¡Qué locura! Marcador: ${score} (${scorer?.minute ?? ''}) ¡VAMOS CABALLAS! 🖤⚪`,
          tag: `goal-home-${match.homeScore}`,
        })
      } else {
        notifications.push({
          title: `😤 Gol en contra... (${score})`,
          body: `Nos han marcado${scorer ? ` ${scorer.playerName}` : ''}. Pero esto no ha terminado. ¡A por ellos! 💪`,
          tag: `goal-home-${match.homeScore}`,
        })
      }
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < awayGoals; i++) {
      const ceutaScored = !ceutaIsHome
      const scorer = match.events
        .filter((e: LiveEvent) => e.type === 'goal' && e.team === 'away')
        .pop()
      if (ceutaScored) {
        notifications.push({
          title: '🚨 ¡¡GOOOOOL DE CEUTA!! 🔥',
          body: `¡${scorer?.playerName ?? 'GOOOL'}! ¡Qué locura! Marcador: ${score} (${scorer?.minute ?? ''}) ¡VAMOS CABALLAS! 🖤⚪`,
          tag: `goal-away-${match.awayScore}`,
        })
      } else {
        notifications.push({
          title: `😤 Gol en contra... (${score})`,
          body: `Nos han marcado${scorer ? ` ${scorer.playerName}` : ''}. Pero esto no ha terminado. ¡A por ellos! 💪`,
          tag: `goal-away-${match.awayScore}`,
        })
      }
    }

    // Red cards
    const newEvents = match.events.slice(prev.eventCount)
    const redCards = newEvents.filter((e: LiveEvent) => e.type === 'redCard')
    for (const card of redCards) {
      const isCeuta = card.team === (ceutaIsHome ? 'home' : 'away')
      if (isCeuta) {
        notifications.push({
          title: '🟥 Nos quedamos con 10...',
          body: `Roja para ${card.playerName} en el ${card.minute}. Ahora más unidos que nunca. ¡El Ceuta no se rinde! 🖤⚪`,
          tag: `red-${card.minute}`,
        })
      } else {
        notifications.push({
          title: '🟥 ¡Roja para el rival!',
          body: `Expulsado ${card.playerName} (${card.minute}). ¡Con uno más, a aprovechar! Vamos Ceuta 💪🔥`,
          tag: `red-${card.minute}`,
        })
      }
    }
  }

  // Match finished
  if (prev?.state === 'in' && match.state === 'post') {
    const ceutaScore = ceutaIsHome ? match.homeScore : match.awayScore
    const rivalScore = ceutaIsHome ? match.awayScore : match.homeScore

    if (ceutaScore > rivalScore) {
      notifications.push({
        title: `🏆 ¡¡VICTORIA CABALLAS!! (${score})`,
        body: '¡Lo hemos conseguido! Tres puntos de oro. Gracias por estar siempre con nosotros. ¡Hasta la siguiente! 🖤⚪🔥',
        tag: 'fulltime',
      })
    } else if (ceutaScore < rivalScore) {
      notifications.push({
        title: `😔 Derrota... pero seguimos juntos (${score})`,
        body: 'No era el día. Esto es el fútbol. Pero el Ceuta siempre se levanta. ¡Gracias por el apoyo! 🖤⚪',
        tag: 'fulltime',
      })
    } else {
      notifications.push({
        title: `🤝 Empate final (${score})`,
        body: 'Un punto que suma. El equipo lo ha dado todo. ¡Gracias caballas por el apoyo! 🖤⚪',
        tag: 'fulltime',
      })
    }
  }

  return notifications
}
</file>

<file path="src/app/api/cron/live/messages.ts">
function pick<T>(arr: T[]): T {
  return arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)]
}

export const MSG = {

    pick([
      {
        title: '⚽ ¡Arranca el partido, caballas!',
        body: `AD Ceuta vs ${rival}. 90 minutos de corazón blanquinegro. ¡Vamos! 🖤⚪`,

        title: `🟥 Expulsado ${player} (${minute})`,
        body: `Con 10, pero con más corazón que nunca. ¡Vamos Ceuta! 🖤⚪💪`,

      {
        title: '🟥 Roja en el Ceuta...',
        body: `${player} se va al vestuario en el ${minute}. 10 guerreros quedan en el campo. ¡A defenderlo! 🖤⚪`,
      },
    ]),

  redRival: (player: string, minute: string) =>
    pick([
      {
        title: '🟥 ¡Roja para el rival!',
        body: `Expulsado ${player} (${minute}). ¡Con uno más, a aprovechar! Vamos Ceuta 💪🔥`,
      },
      {
        title: `🟥 ¡Se queda con 10 el rival!`,
        body: `${player} ve la roja en el ${minute}. ¡Momento de atacar, caballas! 🖤⚪🔥`,
      },
      {
        title: '🟥 ¡Superioridad numérica!',
        body: `Fuera ${player} (${minute}). ¡Ahora hay que aprovechar! ¡Vamos Ceuta! 💥🖤⚪`,
      },
    ]),

  winCeuta: (score: string) =>
    pick([
      {
        title: `🏆 ¡¡VICTORIA CABALLAS!! (${score})`,
        body: `¡Lo hemos conseguido! Tres puntos de oro. ¡Gracias por estar siempre con nosotros! 🖤⚪🔥`,
      },
      {
        title: `🏆 ¡Tres puntos! (${score})`,
        body: `¡Partido fantástico! El Ceuta gana y sigue adelante. ¡Arriba los caballas! 🖤⚪💪`,
      },
      {
        title: `⚽ ¡Ganamoooos! (${score})`,
        body: `¡Qué tarde tan grande! Tres puntos que saben a gloria. ¡Hasta la siguiente, caballas! 🔥🖤⚪`,
      },
      {
        title: `🖤⚪ ¡Victoria del Ceuta! (${score})`,
        body: `¡Enorme trabajo del equipo! Puntos en el bolsillo. ¡La ciudad está de fiesta! 🎉`,
      },
    ]),

  lossCeuta: (score: string) =>
    pick([
      {
        title: `😔 Derrota... pero seguimos juntos (${score})`,
        body: `No era el día. Esto es el fútbol. El Ceuta siempre se levanta. ¡Gracias por el apoyo! 🖤⚪`,
      },
      {
        title: `😞 No pudo ser (${score})`,
        body: `Derrota dura, pero el equipo lo ha intentado. ¡Seguimos, caballas! 🖤⚪💪`,
      },
      {
        title: `⚫ Derrota (${score})`,
        body: `Un mal resultado que olvidar. El Ceuta se levantará. ¡La afición siempre está ahí! 🖤⚪`,
      },
    ]),

  drawCeuta: (score: string) =>
    pick([
      {
        title: `🤝 Empate final (${score})`,
        body: `Un punto que suma. El equipo lo ha dado todo. ¡Gracias caballas! 🖤⚪`,
      },
      {
        title: `➖ Tablas (${score})`,
        body: `Se reparte el botín. El Ceuta no se rindió. ¡Un punto es un punto! 🖤⚪`,
      },
      {
        title: `🤝 Empate justo (${score})`,
        body: `Ni para unos ni para otros. El Ceuta peleó hasta el final. ¡Gracias afición! 🖤⚪`,
      },
    ]),

  matchDay: (rival: string, time: string | null, isHome: boolean) =>
    pick([
      {
        title: '📅 ¡Hoy jugamos, caballas!',
        body: `AD Ceuta vs ${rival}${time ? ` a las ${time}` : ''}. ${isHome ? '🏠 En casa' : '✈️ Fuera'}. ¡A por los tres puntos! 🖤⚪`,
      },
      {
        title: '⚽ ¡Día de partido!',
        body: `Hoy frente al ${rival}${time ? ` (${time})` : ''}. ${isHome ? 'El Murube estará con vosotros' : 'Vamos a por ello fuera de casa'}. ¡Vamos Ceuta! 🖤⚪🔥`,
      },
      {
        title: `🖤⚪ Esta tarde, Ceuta vs ${rival}`,
        body: `${time ? `Arranca a las ${time}. ` : ''}${isHome ? '¡El Murube como fortín!' : '¡A ganar lejos de casa!'} ¡Ánimo caballas! 💪`,
      },
      {
        title: '🏟️ ¡Jornada de fútbol!',
        body: `AD Ceuta se mide al ${rival}${time ? ` a las ${time}` : ''}. ¡La ciudad entera apoya! 🖤⚪`,
      },
    ]),
}

<file path="src/app/api/cron/live/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { Receiver } from '@upstash/qstash'
import webpush from 'web-push'
import type { LiveMatch, LiveEvent } from '@/app/api/live/route'
import { setConfig, deleteConfig } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/AppConfigRepository'
import { deleteSubscription } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/PushSubscriptionsRepository'
import { ESPN_BASE as ESPN } from '@/lib/constants'
import { MSG } from './messages'

// ── Fetch live match from ESPN (same logic as /api/live) ──────────────────

async function fetchLiveMatch(): Promise<LiveMatch | null> {
  const res = await fetch(`${ESPN}/scoreboard`, { cache: 'no-store' })
  if (!res.ok) return null

  const sb = await res.json()
  const event = (sb.events ?? []).find((e: unknown) =>
    JSON.stringify(e).toLowerCase().includes('ceuta')
  )
  if (!event) return null

  const comp = event.competitions?.[0]
  const statusType = comp?.status?.type
  const home = comp?.competitors?.find((c: Record<string, unknown>) => c.homeAway === 'home')
  const away = comp?.competitors?.find((c: Record<string, unknown>) => c.homeAway === 'away')
  const state: 'pre' | 'in' | 'post' = statusType?.state ?? 'pre'

  let events: LiveEvent[] = []
  if (state === 'in' || state === 'post') {
    const sum = await fetch(`${ESPN}/summary?event=${event.id}`, { cache: 'no-store' })
    if (sum.ok) {
      const sumData = await sum.json()
      events = (sumData.plays ?? [])
        .filter((p: Record<string, unknown>) => {
          const t = (((p.type as Record<string, unknown>)?.text as string) ?? '').toLowerCase()
          return t.includes('goal') || t.includes('yellow') || t.includes('red')
        })
        .map((p: Record<string, unknown>) => {
          const t = (((p.type as Record<string, unknown>)?.text as string) ?? '').toLowerCase()
          const teamId = (p.team as Record<string, unknown>)?.id
          const athlete = (p.participants as Record<string, unknown>[])?.[0]?.athlete as Record<
            string,
            unknown
          >
          return {
            type: t.includes('yellow') ? 'yellowCard' : t.includes('red') ? 'redCard' : 'goal',
            minute: ((p.clock as Record<string, unknown>)?.displayValue as string) ?? '',
            team: teamId === home?.team?.id ? 'home' : 'away',
            playerName: (athlete?.displayName as string) ?? '',
          } satisfies LiveEvent
        })
    }
  }

  return {
    espnId: event.id,
    state,
    status: statusType?.description ?? '',
    clock: state === 'in' ? (comp?.status?.displayClock ?? '') : '',
    homeTeam: home?.team?.displayName ?? '',
    awayTeam: away?.team?.displayName ?? '',
    homeScore: parseInt(home?.score ?? '0'),
    awayScore: parseInt(away?.score ?? '0'),
    events,
    venue: comp?.venue?.fullName ?? '',
    startDate: event.date ?? '',
  }
}

// ── Send push to all subscribers ──────────────────────────────────────────
async function sendPush(title: string, body: string, tag: string, url = '/partidos') {
  webpush.setVapidDetails(
    'mailto:angelhpascual@gmail.com',
    process.env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY!,
    process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY!
  )

  const subs = await import('@/infrastructure/supabase/PushSubscriptionsRepository').then((m) =>
    m.getAllSubscriptions()
  )

  if (!subs.length) return 0

  const payload = JSON.stringify({ title, body, tag, url })
  let sent = 0

  await Promise.allSettled(
    subs.map(async (sub) => {
      try {
        await webpush.sendNotification(
          { endpoint: sub.endpoint, keys: { p256dh: sub.p256dh, auth: sub.auth } },
          payload
        )
        sent++
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        const status = (err as Record<string, unknown>)?.statusCode
        if (status === 410 || status === 404) {
          await deleteSubscription(sub.endpoint)
        }
      }
    })
  )

  return sent
}


// ── Cached state helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
interface MatchState {
  espnId: string
  homeScore: number
  awayScore: number
  state: string
  eventCount: number
}

async function getStoredState(): Promise<MatchState | null> {
  const { getConfig } = await import('@/infrastructure/supabase/AppConfigRepository')
  const raw = await getConfig('live_match_state')
  if (!raw) return null
  try {
    return JSON.parse(raw)
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

async function saveState(state: MatchState) {
  await setConfig('live_match_state', JSON.stringify(state))
}

// ── Main logic ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function checkAndNotify() {
  const match = await fetchLiveMatch()

  if (!match) {
    // ESPN returned no Ceuta match — could be an API hiccup during a live game.
    // Don't delete stored state here; only clear it after the match reaches 'post'.
    // If we cleared on every miss we'd reset the goal baseline mid-game.
    const prev = await getStoredState()
    if (prev?.state === 'post') {
      await deleteConfig('live_match_state')
    }
    return { match: null, notifications: [] }
  }

  const prev = await getStoredState()
  const notifications: string[] = []

  const newState: MatchState = {
    espnId: match.espnId,
    homeScore: match.homeScore,
    awayScore: match.awayScore,
    state: match.state,
    eventCount: match.events.length,
  }

  const rival = match.homeTeam.toLowerCase().includes('ceuta') ? match.awayTeam : match.homeTeam
  const ceutaIsHome = match.homeTeam.toLowerCase().includes('ceuta')
  const score = `${match.homeScore}-${match.awayScore}`

  // ── Match just started ────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if ((!prev || prev.state === 'pre') && match.state === 'in') {
    const m = MSG.kickoff(rival)
    await sendPush(m.title, m.body, 'kickoff')
    notifications.push('kickoff')
  }

  // ── Goal detected ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (prev && match.state === 'in') {
    const homeGoals = match.homeScore - prev.homeScore
    const awayGoals = match.awayScore - prev.awayScore

    for (let i = 0; i < homeGoals; i++) {
      const ceutaScored = ceutaIsHome
      const scorer = match.events.filter((e) => e.type === 'goal' && e.team === 'home').pop()
      const m = ceutaScored
        ? MSG.goalCeuta(scorer?.playerName ?? 'GOOOL', score, scorer?.minute ?? '')
        : MSG.goalRival(scorer?.playerName ?? '', score)
      await sendPush(m.title, m.body, `goal-home-${match.homeScore}`)
      notifications.push(`goal-home-${match.homeScore}`)
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < awayGoals; i++) {
      const ceutaScored = !ceutaIsHome
      const scorer = match.events.filter((e) => e.type === 'goal' && e.team === 'away').pop()
      const m = ceutaScored
        ? MSG.goalCeuta(scorer?.playerName ?? 'GOOOL', score, scorer?.minute ?? '')
        : MSG.goalRival(scorer?.playerName ?? '', score)
      await sendPush(m.title, m.body, `goal-away-${match.awayScore}`)
      notifications.push(`goal-away-${match.awayScore}`)
    }

    // ── Red card ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const newEvents = match.events.slice(prev.eventCount)
    const redCards = newEvents.filter((e) => e.type === 'redCard')
    for (const card of redCards) {
      const isCeuta = card.team === (ceutaIsHome ? 'home' : 'away')
      const m = isCeuta
        ? MSG.redCeuta(card.playerName, card.minute)
        : MSG.redRival(card.playerName, card.minute)
      await sendPush(m.title, m.body, `red-${card.minute}`)
      notifications.push(`red-${card.playerName}`)
    }
  }

  // ── Match finished ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (prev?.state === 'in' && match.state === 'post') {
    const ceutaScore = ceutaIsHome ? match.homeScore : match.awayScore
    const rivalScore = ceutaIsHome ? match.awayScore : match.homeScore
    const m =
      ceutaScore > rivalScore
        ? MSG.winCeuta(score)
        : ceutaScore < rivalScore
          ? MSG.lossCeuta(score)
          : MSG.drawCeuta(score)
    await sendPush(m.title, m.body, 'fulltime')
    notifications.push('fulltime')
  }

  await saveState(newState)
  return {
    match: { state: match.state, score: `${match.homeScore}-${match.awayScore}` },
    notifications,
  }
}

// ── Routes ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const receiver = new Receiver({
    currentSigningKey: process.env.QSTASH_CURRENT_SIGNING_KEY!,
    nextSigningKey: process.env.QSTASH_NEXT_SIGNING_KEY!,
  })
  const body = await request.text()
  const valid = await receiver
    .verify({
      signature: request.headers.get('Upstash-Signature') ?? '',
      body,
    })
    .catch(() => false)

  if (!valid) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })

  const result = await checkAndNotify()
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, ...result })
}

export async function GET() {
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Use POST in production' }, { status: 405 })
  }
  const result = await checkAndNotify()
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, ...result })
}
</file>

<file path="src/app/api/cron/match-day/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { Receiver } from '@upstash/qstash'
import webpush from 'web-push'
import { ESPN_BASE as ESPN } from '@/lib/constants'
import {
  getAllSubscriptions,
  deleteSubscription,
} from '@/infrastructure/supabase/PushSubscriptionsRepository'
import { MSG } from '../live/messages'

async function getTodayMatch() {
  const res = await fetch(`${ESPN}/scoreboard`, { cache: 'no-store' })
  if (!res.ok) return null

  const sb = await res.json()
  const event = (sb.events ?? []).find((e: unknown) =>
    JSON.stringify(e).toLowerCase().includes('ceuta')
  )
  if (!event) return null

  const comp = event.competitions?.[0]
  const home = comp?.competitors?.find((c: Record<string, unknown>) => c.homeAway === 'home')
  const away = comp?.competitors?.find((c: Record<string, unknown>) => c.homeAway === 'away')
  const state = comp?.status?.type?.state ?? 'pre'

  // Solo notificar si el partido no ha empezado aún
  if (state !== 'pre') return null

  const startDate: string = event.date ?? ''
  const kickoffTime = startDate
    ? new Date(startDate).toLocaleTimeString('es-ES', {
        hour: '2-digit',
        minute: '2-digit',
        timeZone: 'Europe/Madrid',
      })
    : null

  return {
    homeTeam: home?.team?.displayName ?? '',
    awayTeam: away?.team?.displayName ?? '',
    venue: comp?.venue?.fullName ?? 'Alfonso Murube',
    kickoffTime,
  }
}

async function sendMatchDayPush(match: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getTodayMatch>>) {
  if (!match) return 0

  webpush.setVapidDetails(
    'mailto:angelhpascual@gmail.com',
    process.env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY!,
    process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY!
  )

  const subs = await getAllSubscriptions()
  if (!subs.length) return 0

  const ceutaIsHome = match.homeTeam.toLowerCase().includes('ceuta')
  const rival = ceutaIsHome ? match.awayTeam : match.homeTeam
  const msg = MSG.matchDay(rival, match.kickoffTime, ceutaIsHome)

  const payload = JSON.stringify({
    ...msg,
    tag: 'match-day',
    url: '/partidos',
  })

  let sent = 0
  await Promise.allSettled(
    subs.map(async (sub) => {
      try {
        await webpush.sendNotification(
          { endpoint: sub.endpoint, keys: { p256dh: sub.p256dh, auth: sub.auth } },
          payload
        )
        sent++
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        const status = (err as Record<string, unknown>)?.statusCode
        if (status === 410 || status === 404) await deleteSubscription(sub.endpoint)
      }
    })
  )

  return sent
}

// ── Routes ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const receiver = new Receiver({
    currentSigningKey: process.env.QSTASH_CURRENT_SIGNING_KEY!,
    nextSigningKey: process.env.QSTASH_NEXT_SIGNING_KEY!,
  })
  const body = await request.text()
  const valid = await receiver
    .verify({ signature: request.headers.get('Upstash-Signature') ?? '', body })
    .catch(() => false)

  if (!valid) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })

  const match = await getTodayMatch()
  const sent = await sendMatchDayPush(match)
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, match: match ? 'found' : 'none', sent })
}

export async function GET() {
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Use POST in production' }, { status: 405 })
  }
  const match = await getTodayMatch()
  const sent = await sendMatchDayPush(match)
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, match: match ? 'found' : 'none', sent })
}

<file path="src/app/api/cron/matches/route.ts">
export const maxDuration = 60

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { Receiver } from '@upstash/qstash'
import {
  scrapeNextMatches,
  scrapeRecentForm,
  scrapeRecentResults,
} from '@/infrastructure/scrapers/adceutaScraper'
import { MatchesSupabaseRepository } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/MatchesSupabaseRepository'
import { supabase } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/client'
import { setConfig } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/AppConfigRepository'
import { CURRENT_SEASON, COMPETITION } from '@/lib/constants'

const repo = new MatchesSupabaseRepository()

async function syncMatches() {
  const [matches, form, results] = await Promise.all([
    scrapeNextMatches(),
    scrapeRecentForm(),
    scrapeRecentResults(10),
  ])

  await repo.upsertMatches(matches)

  // Store finished results in matches table
  if (results.length > 0) {
    await supabase.from('matches').upsert(
      results.map((r) => ({
        external_id: `result-${r.date}-${r.homeTeam}-${r.awayTeam}`
          .toLowerCase()
          .replace(/\s+/g, '-'),
        home_team: r.homeTeam,
        away_team: r.awayTeam,
        date: r.date,
        time: '',
        weekday: r.weekday,
        is_home: r.isHome,
        tickets_url: null,
        status: 'finished',
        season: CURRENT_SEASON,
        competition: COMPETITION,
        home_score: r.homeScore,
        away_score: r.awayScore,
        result: r.result,
        espn_event_id: r.espnEventId,
      })),
      { onConflict: 'external_id', ignoreDuplicates: false }
    )
  }

  if (form.length > 0) {
    await setConfig('ceuta_form', form.join(','))
  }

  return { matches: matches.length, form }
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
  const secret = searchParams.get('secret')

  // Permitir TEST_SECRET como alternativa a la firma QStash
  if (secret === process.env.TEST_SECRET) {
    const count = await syncMatches()
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, count })
  }

  // Verificación firma QStash
  const receiver = new Receiver({
    currentSigningKey: process.env.QSTASH_CURRENT_SIGNING_KEY!,
    nextSigningKey: process.env.QSTASH_NEXT_SIGNING_KEY!,
  })

  const body = await request.text()
  const valid = await receiver
    .verify({
      signature: request.headers.get('Upstash-Signature') ?? '',
      body,
    })
    .catch(() => false)

  if (!valid) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })

  const count = await syncMatches()
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, count })
}

// Manual en local/dev
export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
  const secret = searchParams.get('secret')
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' && secret !== process.env.TEST_SECRET) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Use POST via QStash in production' }, { status: 405 })
  }
  const count = await syncMatches()
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, count })
}

<file path="src/app/api/cron/news/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'

export const maxDuration = 60
import { Receiver } from '@upstash/qstash'
import { RSS_SOURCES } from '@/infrastructure/rss/sources'
import { fetchSource, ParsedArticle } from '@/infrastructure/rss/RssFetcher'
import { fetchAdceutaOfficialNews } from '@/infrastructure/rss/adceutaFetcher'
import { fetchCeutaDeportiva } from '@/infrastructure/rss/ceutadeportivaFetcher'
import { fetchOgImage } from '@/infrastructure/rss/fetchOgImage'
import { NewsSupabaseRepository } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/NewsSupabaseRepository'
import { sendNewsNotification } from '@/infrastructure/push/sendNewsNotification'
import { logCron, logNotification } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/logCron'
import { supabase } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/client'
import {
  downloadAndUploadImage,
  isStoredNewsImage,
  newsImageFilename,
} from '@/infrastructure/storage/imageUploader'

const repo = new NewsSupabaseRepository()

// Source priority for picking the most relevant new article to notify about
const SOURCE_PRIORITY: Record<string, number> = {
  oficial: 0,
  faro: 1,
  ceutadeportiva: 2,
  elpueblo: 2,
  marca: 3,
  mundodeportivo: 4,
  '20minutos': 5,
  sport: 6,
  as: 7,
  confidencial: 8,
  abc: 9,
  lavanguardia: 10,
  cope: 11,
  ser: 12,
  onefootball: 13,
  ceutatv: 14,
  rtvc: 15,
  laverdad: 16,
  ceutaactualidad: 17,
  ceutaaldia: 18,
  teleceuta: 19,
}

function pickMostRelevant(articles: ParsedArticle[]): ParsedArticle | null {
  if (articles.length === 0) return null
  return articles.slice().sort((a, b) => {
    const pa = SOURCE_PRIORITY[a.source] ?? 99
    const pb = SOURCE_PRIORITY[b.source] ?? 99
    if (pa !== pb) return pa - pb
    // Same source — prefer most recently published
    return (b.publishedAt ?? '').localeCompare(a.publishedAt ?? '')
  })[0]
}

const MAX_ARTICLE_AGE_MS = 5 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 // 5 days

function isRecent(publishedAt: string | null): boolean {
  if (!publishedAt) return true // no date → don't discard
  return Date.now() - new Date(publishedAt).getTime() < MAX_ARTICLE_AGE_MS
}

async function storeArticleImage(article: ParsedArticle): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!article.imageUrl || isStoredNewsImage(article.imageUrl)) return false

  const storedUrl = await downloadAndUploadImage(
    article.imageUrl,
    newsImageFilename(article.source, article.externalId)
  )

  if (!storedUrl) return false
  article.imageUrl = storedUrl
  await repo.updateImageUrl(article.externalId, storedUrl)
  return true
}

async function backfillLatestRemoteImages(limit = 20): Promise<number> {
  const rows = await repo.getLatestRowsWithImages(limit)
  let optimized = 0

  await Promise.all(
    rows
      .filter((row) => !isStoredNewsImage(row.imageUrl))
      .slice(0, 6)
      .map(async (row) => {
        const storedUrl = await downloadAndUploadImage(
          row.imageUrl,
          newsImageFilename(row.source, row.externalId)
        )
        if (!storedUrl) return
        await repo.updateImageUrlById(row.id, storedUrl)
        optimized += 1
      })
  )

  return optimized
}

async function syncNews() {
  const t0 = Date.now()

  const results = await Promise.allSettled([
    (async () => {
      const articles = (await fetchAdceutaOfficialNews(15)).filter((a) => isRecent(a.publishedAt))
      const newArticles = await repo.upsertArticles(articles)
      return { source: 'oficial', count: articles.length, newArticles }
    })(),
    (async () => {
      const articles = (await fetchCeutaDeportiva(8)).filter((a) => isRecent(a.publishedAt))
      const newArticles = await repo.upsertArticles(articles)
      return { source: 'ceutadeportiva', count: articles.length, newArticles }
    })(),
    ...RSS_SOURCES.filter((s) => s.id !== 'oficial').map(async (source) => {
      const articles = (await fetchSource(source)).filter((a) => isRecent(a.publishedAt))
      const newArticles = await repo.upsertArticles(articles)
      return { source: source.id, count: articles.length, newArticles }
    }),
  ])

  const allNew: ParsedArticle[] = []
  const summary = results.map((r) => {
    if (r.status === 'fulfilled') {
      allNew.push(...r.value.newArticles)
      return { source: r.value.source, count: r.value.count, new: r.value.newArticles.length }
    }
    return { error: (r.reason as Error)?.message }
  })

  // Enrich new articles without images by fetching og:image
  await Promise.all(
    allNew
      .filter((a) => !a.imageUrl && a.url)
      .map(async (a) => {
        const imageUrl = await fetchOgImage(a.url)
        if (imageUrl) {
          a.imageUrl = imageUrl
          await repo.updateImageUrl(a.externalId, imageUrl)
        }
      })
  )

  const optimizedNewImages = (
    await Promise.all(allNew.filter((a) => a.imageUrl).map((a) => storeArticleImage(a)))
  ).filter(Boolean).length

  const backfilledImages = 0 // moved to dedicated /api/cron/backfill-images

  // Only notify about articles published in the last 24 hours.
  const ONE_DAY = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
  const recentNew = allNew.filter((a) => {
    if (!a.publishedAt) return false
    return Date.now() - new Date(a.publishedAt).getTime() < ONE_DAY
  })
  const toNotify = pickMostRelevant(recentNew)
  let notifStats = null
  if (toNotify) {
    // Check if a very similar title was already notified in the last 6 hours
    const SIX_HOURS_AGO = new Date(Date.now() - 6 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString()
    const { data: recentNotifs } = await supabase
      .from('notification_logs')
      .select('article_title')
      .gte('created_at', SIX_HOURS_AGO)

    // Extract meaningful keywords (words > 4 chars, ignore stopwords)
    const STOPWORDS = new Set(['ceuta', 'para', 'como', 'pero', 'este', 'esta', 'desde', 'hasta', 'sobre', 'entre', 'todos', 'tiene', 'tras'])
    const keywords = (s: string): Set<string> => {
      const words = s.toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, '').replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, '').split(/\s+/)
      return new Set(words.filter(w => w.length > 4 && !STOPWORDS.has(w)))
    }
    const titleKw = keywords(toNotify.title)
    const alreadySent = (recentNotifs ?? []).some((n) => {
      const sentKw = keywords(n.article_title)
      if (titleKw.size === 0 || sentKw.size === 0) return false
      // Count how many keywords overlap
      let overlap = 0
      for (const w of titleKw) if (sentKw.has(w)) overlap++
      // Similar if >40% of the smaller set's keywords match
      const minSize = Math.min(titleKw.size, sentKw.size)
      return overlap / minSize >= 0.4
    })

    if (alreadySent) {
      notifStats = null
    } else {
      notifStats = await sendNewsNotification(toNotify).catch(() => null)
    }
    if (notifStats) {
      await logNotification({
        articleTitle: toNotify.title,
        articleSource: toNotify.source,
        articleUrl: toNotify.url,
        recipients: notifStats.recipients,
        sentOk: notifStats.sentOk,
        sentFail: notifStats.sentFail,
      })
    }
  }

  // Get current subscriber count for the log
  const { count: subscribers } = await supabase
    .from('push_subscriptions')
    .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true })

  const articlesFound = results.reduce(
    (acc, r) => acc + (r.status === 'fulfilled' ? r.value.count : 0),
    0
  )
  const newArticles = allNew.length

  await logCron('news', 'success', Date.now() - t0, {
    articlesFound,
    newArticles,
    optimizedNewImages,
    backfilledImages,
    subscribers: subscribers ?? 0,
    notified: toNotify?.title ?? null,
    summary,
  })

  return { summary, notified: toNotify?.title ?? null }
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
  const secret = searchParams.get('secret')

  if (secret === process.env.TEST_SECRET) {
    const result = await syncNews()
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, ...result })
  }

  const receiver = new Receiver({
    currentSigningKey: process.env.QSTASH_CURRENT_SIGNING_KEY!,
    nextSigningKey: process.env.QSTASH_NEXT_SIGNING_KEY!,
  })

  const body = await request.text()
  const valid = await receiver
    .verify({
      signature: request.headers.get('Upstash-Signature') ?? '',
      body,
    })
    .catch(() => false)

  if (!valid) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })

  const result = await syncNews()
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, ...result })
}

// Llamada manual (dev o producción con TEST_SECRET)
export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
  const secret = searchParams.get('secret')
  const testSecret = process.env.TEST_SECRET

  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' && secret !== testSecret) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Use POST via QStash in production' }, { status: 405 })
  }

  const result = await syncNews()
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, ...result })
}

<file path="src/app/api/cron/player-stats/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { Receiver } from '@upstash/qstash'
import { supabase } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/client'
import { logCron } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/logCron'
import { ESPN_ROSTER, CURRENT_SEASON } from '@/lib/constants'

const POSITION_GROUP: Record<string, string> = {
  G: 'Porteros',
  D: 'Defensas',
  M: 'Centrocampistas',
  F: 'Delanteros',
}

interface EspnStat {
  name: string
  value: number
}
interface EspnCategory {
  name: string
  stats: EspnStat[]
}
interface EspnAthlete {
  id: string
  displayName: string
  shortName: string
  position: { abbreviation: string; displayName: string }
  jersey?: string
  headshot?: { href: string }
  statistics?: { splits?: { categories?: EspnCategory[] } }
}

function getStat(categories: EspnCategory[], name: string): number {
  for (const cat of categories) {
    const s = cat.stats.find((s) => s.name === name)
    if (s) return s.value ?? 0
  }
  return 0
}

async function scrapePlayerStats() {
  const t0 = Date.now()
  try {
    const res = await fetch(ESPN_ROSTER, { next: { revalidate: 0 } })
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`ESPN responded ${res.status}`)

    const data = await res.json()
    const athletes: EspnAthlete[] = data.athletes ?? []

    const rows = athletes
      .filter((a) => a.statistics?.splits?.categories)
      .map((a) => {
        const cats = a.statistics!.splits!.categories!
        const posAbbr = a.position?.abbreviation ?? 'M'
        return {
          espn_id: a.id,
          season: CURRENT_SEASON,
          name: a.displayName,
          short_name: a.shortName,
          position: a.position?.displayName ?? '',
          position_group: POSITION_GROUP[posAbbr] ?? 'Centrocampistas',
          photo_url: a.headshot?.href ?? null,
          appearances: Math.round(getStat(cats, 'appearances')),
          goals: Math.round(getStat(cats, 'totalGoals')),
          assists: Math.round(getStat(cats, 'goalAssists')),
          shots: Math.round(getStat(cats, 'totalShots')),
          shots_on_target: Math.round(getStat(cats, 'shotsOnTarget')),
          yellow_cards: Math.round(getStat(cats, 'yellowCards')),
          red_cards: Math.round(getStat(cats, 'redCards')),
          saves: Math.round(getStat(cats, 'saves')),
          goals_conceded: Math.round(getStat(cats, 'goalsConceded')),
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        }
      })

    if (rows.length === 0) throw new Error('No athletes with stats found')

    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('player_stats')
      .upsert(rows, { onConflict: 'espn_id,season' })

    if (error) throw new Error(`Supabase upsert failed: ${error.message}`)

    await logCron('player-stats', 'success', Date.now() - t0, {
      updated: rows.length,
      season: CURRENT_SEASON,
    })
    return rows.length
  } catch (err) {
    await logCron('player-stats', 'error', Date.now() - t0, { error: (err as Error).message })
    throw err
  }
}

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
  const secret = searchParams.get('secret')
  const testSecret = process.env.TEST_SECRET

  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' && secret !== testSecret) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })
  }

  try {
    const count = await scrapePlayerStats()
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, updated: count, season: CURRENT_SEASON })
  } catch (err) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: msg }, { status: 500 })
  }
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
  const secret = searchParams.get('secret')

  if (secret === process.env.TEST_SECRET) {
    const count = await scrapePlayerStats()
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, updated: count, season: CURRENT_SEASON })
  }

  const receiver = new Receiver({
    currentSigningKey: process.env.QSTASH_CURRENT_SIGNING_KEY!,
    nextSigningKey: process.env.QSTASH_NEXT_SIGNING_KEY!,
  })

  const body = await request.text()
  const valid = await receiver
    .verify({
      signature: request.headers.get('Upstash-Signature') ?? '',
      body,
    })
    .catch(() => false)

  if (!valid) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })

  try {
    const count = await scrapePlayerStats()
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, updated: count, season: CURRENT_SEASON })
  } catch (err) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: msg }, { status: 500 })
  }
}

<file path="src/app/api/cron/squad/route.ts">
export const maxDuration = 60

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { scrapeSquad } from '@/infrastructure/scrapers/squadScraper'
import { supabase } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/client'
import { CURRENT_SEASON } from '@/lib/constants'

async function syncSquad() {
  const players = await scrapeSquad()
  if (players.length === 0) return 0

  // Upsert by name+season — preserves career_history and espn_id
  const { error } = await supabase.from('squad').upsert(
    players.map((p) => ({
      dorsal: p.dorsal,
      name: p.name,
      position: p.position,
      position_group: p.positionGroup,
      nationality: p.nationality,
      height: p.height,
      weight: p.weight,
      birth_date: p.birthDate,
      photo_url: p.photoUrl,
      season: CURRENT_SEASON,
    })),
    { onConflict: 'name,season', ignoreDuplicates: false }
  )

  if (error) throw new Error(`Failed to upsert squad: ${error.message}`)
  return players.length
}

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
  const secret = searchParams.get('secret')
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' && secret !== process.env.TEST_SECRET) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Use POST in production' }, { status: 405 })
  }
  const count = await syncSquad()
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, count })
}

export async function POST() {
  const count = await syncSquad()
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, count })
}

<file path="src/app/api/cron/stats/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { setConfig } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/AppConfigRepository'

const LALIGA_URL = 'https://www.laliga.com/en-GB/clubs/ad-ceuta-fc/stats'
const CDN_BASE = 'https://assets.laliga.com/squad/2025/t14705'

const STAT_LABELS: Record<string, { label: string; icon: string }> = {
  total_goals_ranking: { label: 'Goleadores', icon: '⚽' },
  total_assists_ranking: { label: 'Asistencias', icon: '🎯' },
  total_ontarget_attempt_ranking: { label: 'Disparos a puerta', icon: '🥊' },
  total_pass_ranking: { label: 'Pases', icon: '🔵' },
  total_interception_ranking: { label: 'Interceptaciones', icon: '🛡️' },
  total_yellow_card_ranking: { label: 'Tarjetas amarillas', icon: '🟨' },
  total_red_card_ranking: { label: 'Tarjetas rojas', icon: '🟥' },
}

interface LaLigaPlayer {
  opta_player_id: string
  stats: { name: string; stat: number }[]
  extra_info: {
    name: string
    slug: string
    shirtNumber: number
  }
}

async function scrapeLeaders() {
  const res = await fetch(LALIGA_URL, {
    headers: { 'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; FunHubCeuta/1.0)' },
    next: { revalidate: 0 },
  })
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`LaLiga responded ${res.status}`)

  const html = await res.text()
  const match = html.match(
    /<script id="__NEXT_DATA__" type="application\/json">([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/
  )
  if (!match) throw new Error('__NEXT_DATA__ not found in LaLiga page')

  const data = JSON.parse(match[1])
  const rawStats: Record<string, LaLigaPlayer[]> = data?.props?.pageProps?.leadersStats ?? {}

  const result: Record<
    string,
    { label: string; icon: string; leaders: { name: string; value: number; photo: string }[] }
  > = {}

  for (const [key, players] of Object.entries(rawStats)) {
    const statKey = key.replace('stat.', '')
    const meta = STAT_LABELS[statKey]
    if (!meta) continue

    const statName = statKey.replace('_ranking', '').replace('total_', '')

    result[statKey] = {
      label: meta.label,
      icon: meta.icon,
      leaders: players.slice(0, 5).map((p) => {
        const value =
          p.stats.find((s) => s.name === `total_${statName}`)?.stat ??
          p.stats.find((s) => !s.name.includes('ranking'))?.stat ??
          0
        const pid = p.opta_player_id
        return {
          name: p.extra_info.name,
          value,
          photo: `${CDN_BASE}/${pid}/256x256/${pid}_t14705_2025_1_003_000.png`,
        }
      }),
    }
  }

  return result
}

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
  const secret = searchParams.get('secret')
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' && secret !== process.env.TEST_SECRET) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })
  }

  try {
    const leaders = await scrapeLeaders()
    await setConfig('ceuta_leaders', JSON.stringify(leaders))
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, categories: Object.keys(leaders).length })
  } catch (err) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
    console.error('[cron/stats]', msg)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: msg }, { status: 500 })
  }
}

export { GET as POST }

  const body = await request.text()

    .verify({
      signature: request.headers.get('Upstash-Signature') ?? '',

  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, count })
}

  const count = await syncTweets()
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, count })
}

<file path="src/app/api/matches/__tests__/deduplicateMatches.test.ts">
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'
import { deduplicateMatches } from '../deduplicateMatches'

function makeMatch(overrides: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  return {
    date: '2026-05-01',
    home_team: 'AD CEUTA FC',
    away_team: 'MÁLAGA CF',
    result: null,
    status: 'scheduled',
    ...overrides,
  }
}

describe('deduplicateMatches', () => {
  it('returns same rows when no duplicates', () => {
    const rows = [
      makeMatch({ date: '2026-05-01', away_team: 'MÁLAGA CF' }),
      makeMatch({ date: '2026-05-08', away_team: 'ALBACETE' }),
    ]
    expect(deduplicateMatches(rows)).toHaveLength(2)
  })

  it('removes exact duplicate keeping first', () => {
    const rows = [makeMatch({ result: null }), makeMatch({ result: null })]
    expect(deduplicateMatches(rows)).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('keeps the row with result when one has it and the other does not', () => {
    const rows = [makeMatch({ result: null }), makeMatch({ result: 'W' })]
    const deduped = deduplicateMatches(rows)
    expect(deduped).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(deduped[0].result).toBe('W')
  })

  it('keeps the row with result regardless of order', () => {
    const rows = [makeMatch({ result: 'W' }), makeMatch({ result: null })]
    const deduped = deduplicateMatches(rows)
    expect(deduped).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(deduped[0].result).toBe('W')
  })

  it('uses only the date portion (ignores time) for dedup key', () => {
    const rows = [
      makeMatch({ date: '2026-05-01T20:00:00', result: null }),
      makeMatch({ date: '2026-05-01T20:00:00', result: 'D' }),
    ]
    const deduped = deduplicateMatches(rows)
    expect(deduped).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(deduped[0].result).toBe('D')
  })

  it('treats different dates as different matches', () => {
    const rows = [makeMatch({ date: '2026-05-01' }), makeMatch({ date: '2026-05-08' })]
    expect(deduplicateMatches(rows)).toHaveLength(2)
  })

  it('treats different home teams as different matches', () => {
    const rows = [
      makeMatch({ home_team: 'AD CEUTA FC' }),
      makeMatch({ home_team: 'MÁLAGA CF', away_team: 'AD CEUTA FC' }),
    ]
    expect(deduplicateMatches(rows)).toHaveLength(2)
  })

  it('returns empty array for empty input', () => {
    expect(deduplicateMatches([])).toHaveLength(0)
  })
})

<file path="src/app/api/matches/__tests__/parseLineupEvents.test.ts">
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'
import { parseLineupEvents } from '../parseLineupEvents'

const CEUTA_ID = '5404'
const RIVAL_ID = '9999'
const HOME_ID = CEUTA_ID

function makeEvent(
  overrides: {
    type?: string
    teamId?: string
    minute?: string
    player0?: string
    player1?: string
  } = {}
) {
  const {
    type = 'goal',
    teamId = CEUTA_ID,
    minute = "17'",
    player0 = 'Marcos Fernández',
    player1,
  } = overrides
  return {
    type: { type },
    team: { id: teamId },
    clock: { displayValue: minute },
    participants: [
      { athlete: { displayName: player0 } },
      ...(player1 ? [{ athlete: { displayName: player1 } }] : []),
    ],
  }
}

describe('parseLineupEvents', () => {
  it('parses a goal event', () => {
    const events = parseLineupEvents([makeEvent({ type: 'goal' })], HOME_ID)
    expect(events).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(events[0].type).toBe('goal')
    expect(events[0].playerName).toBe('Marcos Fernández')
    expect(events[0].minute).toBe("17'")
  })

  it('parses yellow-card event', () => {
    const events = parseLineupEvents([makeEvent({ type: 'yellow-card' })], HOME_ID)
    expect(events[0].type).toBe('yellow-card')
  })

  it('parses red-card event', () => {
    const events = parseLineupEvents([makeEvent({ type: 'red-card' })], HOME_ID)
    expect(events[0].type).toBe('red-card')
  })

  it('parses substitution with playerOut', () => {
    const events = parseLineupEvents(
      [makeEvent({ type: 'substitution', player0: 'Salvi', player1: 'Rubén Díez' })],
      HOME_ID
    )
    expect(events[0].type).toBe('substitution')
    expect(events[0].playerName).toBe('Salvi')
    expect(events[0].playerOut).toBe('Rubén Díez')
  })

  it('marks event as home when teamId matches homeTeamId', () => {
    const events = parseLineupEvents([makeEvent({ teamId: HOME_ID })], HOME_ID)
    expect(events[0].team).toBe('home')
  })

  it('marks event as away when teamId does not match homeTeamId', () => {
    const events = parseLineupEvents([makeEvent({ teamId: CEUTA_ID })], RIVAL_ID)
    expect(events[0].team).toBe('away')
  })

  it('filters out events from rival team', () => {
    const events = parseLineupEvents(
      [makeEvent({ teamId: CEUTA_ID }), makeEvent({ teamId: RIVAL_ID })],
      HOME_ID
    )
    expect(events).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('filters out unknown event types', () => {
    const events = parseLineupEvents(
      [makeEvent({ type: 'corner-kick' }), makeEvent({ type: 'goal' })],
      HOME_ID
    )
    expect(events).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(events[0].type).toBe('goal')
  })

  it('returns empty array for no events', () => {
    expect(parseLineupEvents([], HOME_ID)).toEqual([])
  })

  it('handles multiple events in order', () => {
    const events = parseLineupEvents(
      [
        makeEvent({ type: 'goal', minute: "17'" }),
        makeEvent({ type: 'yellow-card', minute: "45'" }),
        makeEvent({ type: 'substitution', minute: "67'", player0: 'Salvi', player1: 'Lachhab' }),
      ],
      HOME_ID
    )
    expect(events).toHaveLength(3)
    expect(events.map((e) => e.type)).toEqual(['goal', 'yellow-card', 'substitution'])
  })
})

<file path="src/app/api/matches/all/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { supabase } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/client'
import { deduplicateMatches } from '../deduplicateMatches'

export async function GET() {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('matches')
    .select('*')
    .order('date', { ascending: false })
    .limit(60)

  const rows = data ?? []

  const deduped = deduplicateMatches(rows)

  const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]

  const upcoming = deduped
    .filter((r) => r.status === 'scheduled' && String(r.date).slice(0, 10) >= today)
    .sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date))

  const results = deduped
    .filter(
      (r) =>
        r.status === 'finished' || (r.status === 'scheduled' && String(r.date).slice(0, 10) < today)
    )
    .sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date))

  return NextResponse.json({ upcoming, results })
}
