<file path="src/lib/__tests__/serverFetches.test.ts">
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest'

const { mockFrom, mockGetAllTeams } = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  mockFrom: vi.fn(),
  mockGetAllTeams: vi.fn(),
}))

vi.mock('@/infrastructure/supabase/client', () => ({
  supabase: { from: mockFrom },
}))

vi.mock('@/infrastructure/supabase/TeamsSupabaseRepository', () => ({
  TeamsSupabaseRepository: class {
    getAllTeams() {
      return mockGetAllTeams()
    }
  },
}))

import { fetchCeutaForm, fetchTeamLogos } from '../serverFetches'

// Helper to build a chainable Supabase query mock
function makeQuery(result: unknown) {
  const chain: Record<string, unknown> = {}
  const methods = ['select', 'eq', 'not', 'order', 'limit', 'single']
  for (const m of methods) {
    chain[m] = vi.fn().mockReturnValue(chain)
  }
  chain['limit'] = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(result)
  chain['single'] = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(result)
  return chain
}

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.clearAllMocks()
})

describe('fetchCeutaForm', () => {
  it('returns reversed match results when finished matches exist', async () => {
    const query = makeQuery({ data: [{ result: 'W' }, { result: 'D' }, { result: 'L' }] })
    mockFrom.mockReturnValue(query)

    const form = await fetchCeutaForm()
    expect(form).toEqual(['L', 'D', 'W'])
  })

  it('falls back to app_config when no finished matches', async () => {
    // First query (matches) returns empty
    const matchQuery = makeQuery({ data: [] })
    // Second query (app_config) returns config value
    const configQuery = makeQuery({ data: { value: 'W,D,L,W,W' } })

    mockFrom.mockReturnValueOnce(matchQuery).mockReturnValueOnce(configQuery)

    const form = await fetchCeutaForm()
    expect(form).toEqual(['W', 'D', 'L', 'W', 'W'])
  })

  it('returns empty array when both sources are empty', async () => {
    const matchQuery = makeQuery({ data: [] })
    const configQuery = makeQuery({ data: null })

    mockFrom.mockReturnValueOnce(matchQuery).mockReturnValueOnce(configQuery)

    const form = await fetchCeutaForm()
    expect(form).toEqual([])
  })

  it('filters empty strings from app_config value', async () => {
    const matchQuery = makeQuery({ data: [] })
    const configQuery = makeQuery({ data: { value: 'W,,D,,L' } })

    mockFrom.mockReturnValueOnce(matchQuery).mockReturnValueOnce(configQuery)

    const form = await fetchCeutaForm()
    expect(form).toEqual(['W', 'D', 'L'])
  })

  it('returns at most 5 results from matches', async () => {
    // The limit(5) is applied in the query, not in the function itself,
    // so we trust the mock returns exactly what the DB would
    const query = makeQuery({
      data: [{ result: 'W' }, { result: 'W' }, { result: 'D' }, { result: 'L' }, { result: 'W' }],
    })
    mockFrom.mockReturnValue(query)

    const form = await fetchCeutaForm()
    expect(form).toHaveLength(5)
  })
})

describe('fetchTeamLogos', () => {
  it('returns a name→logoUrl map for teams with logos', async () => {
    mockGetAllTeams.mockResolvedValue([
      { name: 'AD Ceuta FC', logoUrl: '/logos/ceuta.png' },
      { name: 'Málaga CF', logoUrl: '/logos/malaga.png' },
    ])

    const logos = await fetchTeamLogos()
    expect(logos).toEqual({
      'AD Ceuta FC': '/logos/ceuta.png',
      'Málaga CF': '/logos/malaga.png',
    })
  })

  it('omits teams without a logoUrl', async () => {
    mockGetAllTeams.mockResolvedValue([
      { name: 'AD Ceuta FC', logoUrl: '/logos/ceuta.png' },
      { name: 'Unknown FC', logoUrl: null },
    ])

    const logos = await fetchTeamLogos()
    expect(Object.keys(logos)).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(logos['Unknown FC']).toBeUndefined()
  })

  it('returns empty object when no teams have logos', async () => {
    mockGetAllTeams.mockResolvedValue([
      { name: 'Team A', logoUrl: null },
      { name: 'Team B', logoUrl: undefined },
    ])

    const logos = await fetchTeamLogos()
    expect(logos).toEqual({})
  })

  it('returns empty object when team list is empty', async () => {
    mockGetAllTeams.mockResolvedValue([])
    const logos = await fetchTeamLogos()
    expect(logos).toEqual({})
  })
})
</file>

<file path="src/lib/findLogo.ts">
const STOPWORDS = new Set([
  'fc',
  'cf',
  'ad',
  'sd',
  'ud',
  'cd',
  'rc',
  'real',
  'atletico',
  'deportivo',
  'balompie',
  'bp',
  'club',
])

function norm(s: string): string {
  return s
    .toLowerCase()
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, '') // remove accents
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, '')
    .trim()
}

function keywords(name: string): string[] {
  return norm(name)
    .split(/\s+/)
    .filter((w) => w.length > 2 && !STOPWORDS.has(w))
}

export function findLogo(logos: Record<string, string>, name: string): string | null {
  if (!name) return null

  const target = norm(name)
  const targetKws = keywords(name)

  for (const [teamName, logo] of Object.entries(logos)) {
    const n = norm(teamName)
    const nKws = keywords(teamName)

    // Exact normalized match
    if (n === target) return logo

    // Substring match
    if (target.includes(n) || n.includes(target)) return logo

    // Keyword overlap — if any significant keyword matches
    if (targetKws.some((kw) => nKws.includes(kw))) return logo
  }

  return null
}
</file>

<file path="src/lib/proxyImage.ts">
const DIRECT_DOMAINS = ['supabase.co']

export function proxyImage(url: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (!url) return null
  if (url.startsWith('/')) return url

  try {
    const hostname = new URL(url).hostname
    if (DIRECT_DOMAINS.some((d) => hostname === d || hostname.endsWith(`.${d}`))) return url
  } catch {}

  return `/api/proxy/image?url=${encodeURIComponent(url)}`
}
</file>

<file path="src/middleware.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import type { NextRequest } from 'next/server'

async function deriveToken(password: string): Promise<string> {
  const enc = new TextEncoder()
  const data = enc.encode(password + 'funhub-admin-v1')
  const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', data)
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer))
    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
    .join('')
}

export async function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl

  if (!pathname.startsWith('/admin')) return NextResponse.next()
  if (pathname === '/admin/login') return NextResponse.next()

  const session = request.cookies.get('admin_session')?.value
  const password = process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD

  if (!session || !password || session !== (await deriveToken(password))) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/admin/login', request.url))
  }

  return NextResponse.next()
}

export const config = {
  matcher: ['/admin/:path*'],
}
</file>

# This is NOT the Next.js you know

This version has breaking changes — APIs, conventions, and file structure may all differ from your training data. Read the relevant guide in `node_modules/next/dist/docs/` before writing any code. Heed deprecation notices.

<!-- END:nextjs-agent-rules -->

# Design System

Before writing any UI code, read @DESIGN.md. All components must follow the design system defined there. Key rules:

- Use CSS variables (`--red`, `--black`, `--bg-soft`, etc.) — never hex codes directly
- Use existing CSS classes (`stat-tile`, `stat-tile red`, `stat-tile dark`, `stats-grid`, `sec-title`, `chip`, etc.)
- 2-column grid with `stats-grid` class for stat tiles
- Tabs use `chips` / `chip` / `chip active` classes
- Section headers use `sec-header`, `sec-title`, `sec-title-small`

<file path="DESIGN.md">
# AD Ceuta FanHub — Sistema de Diseño v1.0

> Fuente de verdad oficial extraída del Sistema de Diseño AD Ceuta FanHub (Temporada 2025–26).
> **Toda nueva pantalla o componente debe seguir estas reglas.** No uses colores, tamaños ni estilos fuera de este sistema sin justificación explícita.

---

## Paleta de colores

Usa siempre las variables CSS — **nunca valores hexadecimales hardcodeados**.

### Colores de marca

| Variable      | Valor     | Uso                                                    |
| ------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------------ |
| `--red`       | `#DA1A22` | Color primario. Acciones, acentos, victorias, play-off |
| `--red-dark`  | `#A8141B` | Estado hover / pressed del rojo                        |
| `--red-soft`  | `#FCE9EA` | Fila destacada del AD Ceuta en la tabla                |
| `--gold`      | `#F4C300` | **SOLO logros y ascenso directo. No decorativo.**      |
| `--gold-soft` | `#FFF6CC` | Fondo suave de elementos dorados                       |
| `--black`     | `#0E0E0E` | Texto principal, superficies oscuras, descenso         |
| `--white`     | `#FFFFFF` | Lienzo de la app                                       |

### Neutros y superficies

| Variable       | Valor     | Uso                                |
| -------------- | --------- | ---------------------------------- |
| `--bg`         | `#FFFFFF` | Fondo general de página            |
| `--bone`       | `#F4F2EE` | Tarjetas suaves, fondos de sección |
| `--bone-2`     | `#ECEAE4` | Bordes suaves, badges neutros      |
| `--muted`      | `#7A786F` | Texto secundario, metadatos        |
| `--muted-2`    | `#A8A69D` | Texto terciario                    |
| `--border`     | `#E6E3DD` | Separadores y bordes de tarjeta    |
| `--border-ink` | `#0E0E0E` | Bordes de énfasis                  |

### Variables semánticas (fútbol)

| Variable       | Color | Significado     |
| -------------- | ----- | --------------- |
| `--win`        | rojo  | Victoria        |
| `--draw`       | gris  | Empate          |
| `--loss`       | negro | Derrota         |
| `--promo`      | oro   | Ascenso directo |
| `--playoff`    | rojo  | Play-off        |
| `--relegation` | negro | Descenso        |

---

## Tipografía

Dos familias exclusivamente:

- **Barlow Condensed** — todo lo display (títulos, números, labels, chips). Siempre en mayúsculas.
- **Barlow** — texto corrido y datos densos.

| Variable         | Fuente           | Uso                                       |
| ---------------- | ---------------- | ----------------------------------------- |
| `--font-display` | Barlow Condensed | Títulos, números, labels, chips, eyebrows |
| `--font-body`    | Barlow           | Párrafos de texto corrido                 |

### Jerarquía tipográfica

| Nombre       | Fuente           | Tamaño | Weight | Notas                                          |
| ------------ | ---------------- | ------ | ------ | ---------------------------------------------- |
| Display XL   | Barlow Condensed | 56px   | 900    | Uppercase                                      |
| Display L    | Barlow Condensed | 40px   | 800    | Uppercase                                      |
| Título       | Barlow Condensed | 28px   | 800    | `.sec-title`                                   |
| Encabezado   | Barlow Condensed | 20px   | 700    |                                                |
| Eyebrow      | Barlow Condensed | 12px   | 700    | Uppercase, tracking 0.18em. `.sec-title-small` |
| Cuerpo       | Barlow           | 16px   | 400    |                                                |
| Small / meta | Barlow           | 13px   | 500    |                                                |

---

## Espaciado

Escala base de **4px**. Márgenes de pantalla: 12–16px.

| Variable | Valor |
| -------- | ----- |
| `--s-1`  | 4px   |
| `--s-2`  | 8px   |
| `--s-3`  | 12px  |
| `--s-4`  | 16px  |
| `--s-5`  | 20px  |
| `--s-6`  | 24px  |
| `--s-8`  | 32px  |
| `--s-10` | 40px  |
| `--s-12` | 48px  |

---

## Radios

| Variable   | Valor | Uso                  |
| ---------- | ----- | -------------------- |
| `--r-sm`   | 8px   | Botones, thumbnails  |
| `--r-md`   | 14px  | Tarjetas             |
| `--r-lg`   | 18px  | Contenedores grandes |
| `--r-pill` | 999px | Chips, avatares      |

---

## Sombras

| Variable        | Valor                          | Uso                          |
| --------------- | ------------------------------ | ---------------------------- |
| `--shadow-card` | `0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.04)`   | Tarjetas sutiles             |
| `--shadow-pop`  | `0 12px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.10)` | Modales, elementos flotantes |
| `--shadow-hard` | `6px 6px 0 var(--red)`         | Tarjeta destacada AD Ceuta   |

---

## Componentes

### Botones y chips

- Etiquetas siempre en **Barlow Condensed, mayúsculas, con tracking**.
- **Rojo** = acción principal. **Negro** = acción secundaria fuerte.
- Clases: `chip`, `chip active`, `match-cta`

```html
<!-- Chip activo -->
<button class="chip active">Resultados</button>
<!-- Chip inactivo -->
<button class="chip">Próximos</button>
```

### Cabeceras de sección

Patrón firma de la app: eyebrow rojo en mayúsculas + título condensado grande.

```html
<div class="sec-header">
  <div>
    <div class="sec-title-small">Temporada 2025–26</div>
    <div class="sec-title">Clasificación</div>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Stat tiles

Grid de 2 columnas con clase `stats-grid`. Variantes: neutro (blanco), `red`, `dark`.

```html
<div class="stats-grid">
  <div class="stat-tile">...</div>
  <div class="stat-tile red">...</div>
  <div class="stat-tile dark">...</div>
</div>
```

### Indicador de forma (Últimos 5)

**Siempre usar iniciales en castellano:**

- **V** (victoria) → fondo negro
- **E** (empate) → fondo gris (`--muted`)
- **D** (derrota) → fondo rojo

**Nunca usar W/L/D en inglés.**

### Badge de resultado

Mismo criterio que el indicador de forma:

- `.badge-w` → negro (Victoria = **V**)
- `.badge-d` → gris (Empate = **E**)
- `.badge-l` → rojo (Derrota = **D**)

### Navegación inferior

Barra fija de 4 destinos: Inicio, Clasificación, Partidos, Club.
La pestaña activa: icono/etiqueta en negro + **indicador rojo centrado pegado al borde superior** (como hijo del `.nav-item` con `position: relative`, no flotando).

---

## Nomenclatura del club

**Fijar un único nombre oficial:**

- ✅ **AD Ceuta FC** — cabeceras y fichas
- ✅ **AD Ceuta** — tablas y resultados
- ❌ Evitar: «Imperio AD Ceuta FC», «Imperio-AD Ceuta», mezclas con/sin «FC»

---

## Patrones visuales

- **Franjas diagonales** sobre superficies oscuras (6% opacidad) — cabeceras
- **Patrón ajedrezado** — inspirado en el escudo, solo en cabeceras

---

## Auditoría de diseño (pendiente de resolver)

| #   | Prioridad   | Problema                                               | Fix                                                                 |
| --- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1   | ~~Alta~~ ✅ | Nombre del club inconsistente                          | Usar `AD Ceuta FC` / `AD Ceuta`                                     |
| 2   | Alta        | Indicador rojo de la nav «flotando»                    | `position:absolute; top:0` dentro de `.nav-item{position:relative}` |
| 3   | Alta        | Empty state «Próximos partidos» sin diseño             | Empty state real con escudo atenuado + mensaje en condensada        |
| 4   | ~~Alta~~ ✅ | Forma reciente mezcla V/E/D con W/L                    | Corregido: V negro · E gris · D rojo                                |
| 5   | Media       | El oro tiene dos significados (logro + partido título) | Reservar oro solo para logros/ascenso                               |
| 6   | Media       | Resalte de fila AD Ceuta vs barra de play-off          | Borde izquierdo grueso o fondo neutro en vez de rojo suave          |
| 7   | Media       | Truncado de nombres a mitad de palabra                 | Nombres cortos oficiales en tablas, 2 líneas con elipsis            |
| 8   | Baja        | Contraste de metadatos                                 | Oscurecer `--muted` a ~`#6B6960` para texto <13px                   |
| 9   | Baja        | Barra superior solo muestra escudo                     | Opcional: escudo + «AD CEUTA» en condensada                         |
| 10  | Baja        | Bordes de resultado sin leyenda                        | Decidir si son redundantes con el badge o se mantienen              |
</file>

<file path="setup-qstash.sh">
#!/bin/bash
# Lee el token de .env.local
TOKEN=$(grep 'QSTASH_TOKEN=' .env.local | cut -d'=' -f2 | tr -d '"')
BASE="https://funhubceuta.vercel.app"

echo "Token: ${TOKEN:0:10}..."
echo "Creando schedules en QStash..."

# Noticias 3x/día (8h, 14h, 20h hora española)
echo -n "Noticias: "
curl -s -X POST "https://qstash.upstash.io/v2/schedules" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d "{\"destination\":\"$BASE/api/cron/news\",\"cron\":\"0 7,13,19 * * *\"}" | grep -o '"scheduleId":"[^"]*"'

# Partidos cada lunes
echo -n "Partidos: "
curl -s -X POST "https://qstash.upstash.io/v2/schedules" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d "{\"destination\":\"$BASE/api/cron/matches\",\"cron\":\"0 8 * * 1\"}" | grep -o '"scheduleId":"[^"]*"'

# Plantilla cada lunes
echo -n "Plantilla: "
curl -s -X POST "https://qstash.upstash.io/v2/schedules" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d "{\"destination\":\"$BASE/api/cron/squad\",\"cron\":\"30 8 * * 1\"}" | grep -o '"scheduleId":"[^"]*"'

# Live match cada minuto
echo -n "Live: "
curl -s -X POST "https://qstash.upstash.io/v2/schedules" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d "{\"destination\":\"$BASE/api/cron/live\",\"cron\":\"* * * * *\"}" | grep -o '"scheduleId":"[^"]*"'

echo ""
echo "Listo."
</file>

      "type": "image/png",
      "purpose": "any maskable"
    }
  ]
}
</file>

<file path="src/app/admin/mercado/page.tsx">
'use client'

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import Image from 'next/image'
import type { Transfer, TransferType } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/TransfersRepository'

const POSITIONS = ['POR', 'DEF', 'MED', 'DEL', 'ENT']
const TYPE_LABELS: Record<TransferType, string> = { in: 'Fichaje', out: 'Salida', rumor: 'Rumor', renew: 'Renovación' }
const TYPE_COLORS: Record<TransferType, string> = {
  in: '#166534',
  out: 'var(--red)',
  rumor: 'var(--muted)',
  renew: '#92700a',
}
const TABS: { id: TransferType; label: string; color: string }[] = [
  { id: 'renew', label: 'Renovados', color: '#166534' },
  { id: 'in',    label: 'Fichados',  color: '#166534' },
  { id: 'out',   label: 'Salidas',   color: 'var(--red)' },
  { id: 'rumor', label: 'Rumores',   color: 'var(--muted)' },
]

const EMPTY_FORM = {
  name: '',
  position: 'DEL',
  type: 'in' as TransferType,
  clubFrom: '',
  clubTo: '',
  reliability: 1 as 1 | 2 | 3,
  photoUrl: '',
  notes: '',
}

export default function AdminMercadoPage() {
  const [transfers, setTransfers] = useState<Transfer[]>([])
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY_FORM)
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false)
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('')
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TransferType>('renew')
  const formRef = useRef<HTMLFormElement>(null)
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)

  async function handlePhotoUpload(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0]
    if (!file) return
    setUploading(true)
    const fd = new FormData()
    fd.append('file', file)
    fd.append('name', form.name || 'player')
    const res = await fetch('/api/admin/upload-photo', { method: 'POST', body: fd })
    const data = await res.json()
    if (data.url) setForm((f) => ({ ...f, photoUrl: data.url }))
    else setMsg('Error subiendo foto')
    setUploading(false)
    if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = ''
  }

  async function load() {
    const res = await fetch('/api/admin/transfers')
    const data = await res.json()
    setTransfers(data.transfers ?? [])
  }

  useEffect(() => { load() }, [])

  function startEdit(t: Transfer) {
    setEditingId(t.id)
    setActiveTab(t.type)
    setForm({
      name: t.name,
      position: t.position,
      type: t.type,
      clubFrom: t.clubFrom ?? '',
      clubTo: t.clubTo ?? '',
      reliability: t.reliability,
      photoUrl: t.photoUrl ?? '',
      notes: t.notes ?? '',
    })
    formRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' })
  }

  function cancelEdit() {
    setEditingId(null)
    setForm(EMPTY_FORM)
  }

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setSaving(true)
    setMsg('')

    const body = {
      ...form,
      clubFrom: form.clubFrom || null,
      clubTo: form.clubTo || null,
      photoUrl: form.photoUrl || null,
      notes: form.notes || null,
    }

    let res: Response
    if (editingId) {
      res = await fetch('/api/admin/transfers', {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ id: editingId, ...body }),
      })
    } else {
      res = await fetch('/api/admin/transfers', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(body),
      })
    }

    if (res.ok) {
      setForm(EMPTY_FORM)
      setEditingId(null)
      setMsg(editingId ? '✓ Actualizado' : '✓ Añadido')
      await load()
    } else {
      setMsg('Error al guardar')
    }
    setSaving(false)
    setTimeout(() => setMsg(''), 3000)
  }

  async function handleDelete(id: string) {
    if (!confirm('¿Eliminar este movimiento?')) return
    await fetch('/api/admin/transfers', {
      method: 'DELETE',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ id }),
    })
    await load()
  }

  const C = {
    card: {
      background: 'var(--white)', borderRadius: 14, padding: 20,
      marginBottom: 16, boxShadow: 'var(--shadow-card)',
    } as React.CSSProperties,
    label: {
      fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 800, textTransform: 'uppercase' as const,
      letterSpacing: '0.1em', color: 'var(--muted)', marginBottom: 6,
      display: 'block', fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)',
    },
    input: {
      width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', borderRadius: 10,
      border: '1.5px solid var(--border)', fontSize: 14,
      background: 'var(--bone)', boxSizing: 'border-box' as const,
      fontFamily: 'var(--font-body)', outline: 'none',
    },
    select: {
      width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', borderRadius: 10,
      border: '1.5px solid var(--border)', fontSize: 14,
      background: 'var(--bone)', boxSizing: 'border-box' as const,
      fontFamily: 'var(--font-body)',
    },
    row: { marginBottom: 14 } as React.CSSProperties,
    grid2: { display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 12, marginBottom: 14 } as React.CSSProperties,
  }

  const secTitle = {
    fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 800, textTransform: 'uppercase' as const,
    letterSpacing: '0.1em', color: 'var(--muted)', marginBottom: 14,
    fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)',
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)' }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 900, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '-0.01em', marginBottom: 20 }}>
          Mercado de Fichajes
        </div>

        {/* Form */}
        <form ref={formRef} onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={{
          ...C.card,
          borderTop: editingId ? '3px solid var(--gold)' : '3px solid var(--black)',
        }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 14 }}>
            <div style={secTitle}>{editingId ? '✏️ Editando jugador' : 'Añadir movimiento'}</div>
            {editingId && (
              <button type="button" onClick={cancelEdit} style={{
                background: 'none', border: '1px solid var(--border)', borderRadius: 8,
                padding: '4px 12px', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: 12,
                color: 'var(--muted)', fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', fontWeight: 700,
              }}>
                Cancelar
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          <div style={C.row}>
            <label style={C.label}>Nombre del jugador</label>
            <input style={C.input} value={form.name} required
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })}
              placeholder="ej. Marcos Acuña" />
          </div>

          <div style={C.grid2}>
            <div>
              <label style={C.label}>Posición</label>
              <select style={C.select} value={form.position}
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, position: e.target.value })}>
                {POSITIONS.map((p) => <option key={p} value={p}>{p}</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label style={C.label}>Tipo</label>
              <select style={C.select} value={form.type}
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, type: e.target.value as TransferType })}>
                <option value="in">✅ Fichaje</option>
                <option value="renew">🔄 Renovación</option>
                <option value="out">🚪 Salida</option>
                <option value="rumor">🔥 Rumor</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={C.grid2}>
            <div>
              <label style={C.label}>Club de origen</label>
              <input style={C.input} value={form.clubFrom}
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, clubFrom: e.target.value })}
                placeholder="ej. Sevilla FC" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label style={C.label}>Club de destino</label>
              <input style={C.input} value={form.clubTo}
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, clubTo: e.target.value })}
                placeholder="Solo para salidas" />
            </div>
          </div>

          {form.type === 'rumor' && (
            <div style={C.row}>
              <label style={C.label}>Fiabilidad del rumor</label>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
                {([1, 2, 3] as const).map((v) => (
                  <button key={v} type="button"
                    onClick={() => setForm({ ...form, reliability: v })}
                    style={{
                      flex: 1, padding: '10px 0', borderRadius: 10, border: 'none',
                      background: form.reliability >= v ? 'var(--black)' : 'var(--bone-2)',
                      color: form.reliability >= v ? 'var(--white)' : 'var(--muted)',
                      cursor: 'pointer', fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)',
                      fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 800,
                    }}>
                    {'🔥'.repeat(v)}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div style={C.row}>
            <label style={C.label}>Foto del jugador</label>
            {/* Upload button */}
            <input
              ref={fileInputRef}
              type="file"
              accept="image/*"
              style={{ display: 'none' }}
              onChange={handlePhotoUpload}
            />
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, marginBottom: 8 }}>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()} disabled={uploading}
                style={{
                  flex: 1, padding: '10px 0', borderRadius: 10, border: '1.5px dashed var(--border)',
                  background: 'var(--bone)', cursor: uploading ? 'default' : 'pointer',
                  fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700,
                  color: uploading ? 'var(--muted)' : 'var(--black)',
                  textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em',
                }}>
                {uploading ? 'Subiendo...' : '📁 Subir desde dispositivo'}
              </button>
            </div>
            {/* Manual URL fallback */}
            <input style={{ ...C.input, fontSize: 12 }} value={form.photoUrl}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, photoUrl: e.target.value })}
              placeholder="O pega una URL directamente..." type="url" />
            {form.photoUrl && (
              <div style={{ marginTop: 8, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
                <div style={{
                  width: 56, height: 56, borderRadius: 8, overflow: 'hidden',
                  background: 'var(--bone-2)', position: 'relative', flexShrink: 0,
                }}>
                  {/* Serve Supabase URLs directly; proxy external ones */}
                  <Image
                    src={form.photoUrl.includes('supabase.co')
                      ? form.photoUrl
                      : `/api/proxy/image?url=${encodeURIComponent(form.photoUrl)}&w=120`}
                    alt="preview"
                    fill
                    sizes="56px"
                    className="object-cover object-top"
                    unoptimized
                  />
                </div>
                <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--muted)' }}>
                  {form.photoUrl.includes('supabase.co') ? '✅ Guardada en Supabase' : 'Vista previa'}
                </span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div style={C.row}>
            <label style={C.label}>Nota / contexto (opcional)</label>
            <input style={C.input} value={form.notes}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, notes: e.target.value })}
              placeholder="ej. Interés confirmado por El Faro" />
          </div>

          <button type="submit" disabled={saving}
            style={{
              width: '100%', padding: '13px 0', borderRadius: 12, border: 'none',
              background: saving ? 'var(--bone-2)' : editingId ? 'var(--gold)' : 'var(--black)',
              color: editingId ? 'var(--black)' : 'var(--white)',
              fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 800,
              textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.08em', cursor: saving ? 'default' : 'pointer',
            }}>
            {saving ? 'Guardando...' : editingId ? 'Guardar cambios' : 'Añadir movimiento'}
          </button>

          {msg && (
            <p style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 10, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700,
              color: msg.startsWith('✓') ? '#166534' : 'var(--red)' }}>
              {msg}
            </p>
          )}
        </form>

        {/* Tabs */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, marginBottom: 16, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
          {TABS.map((tab) => {
            const count = transfers.filter(t => t.type === tab.id).length
            const active = activeTab === tab.id
            return (
              <button key={tab.id} onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)} style={{
                padding: '8px 16px', borderRadius: 10, border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer',
                background: active ? tab.color : 'var(--bone)',
                color: active ? 'var(--white)' : 'var(--muted)',
                fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 800,
                textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.08em',
                display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6,
              }}>
                {tab.label}
                <span style={{
                  background: active ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.25)' : 'var(--bone-2)',
                  color: active ? 'var(--white)' : 'var(--black)',
                  borderRadius: 'var(--r-pill)', padding: '1px 7px', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 900,
                }}>
                  {count}
                </span>
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>

        {/* List */}
        <div style={secTitle}>
          {TYPE_LABELS[activeTab]} ({transfers.filter(t => t.type === activeTab).length})
        </div>
        {transfers.filter(t => t.type === activeTab).length === 0 && (
          <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '32px 0', color: 'var(--muted)', fontSize: 13 }}>
            Sin movimientos en esta categoría
          </div>
        )}
        {transfers.filter(t => t.type === activeTab).map((t) => (
            <div key={t.id} style={{
              ...C.card,
              display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, padding: '14px 16px',
              outline: editingId === t.id ? '2px solid var(--gold)' : 'none',
            }}>
              {t.photoUrl && (
                <div style={{
                  width: 44, height: 44, borderRadius: 8, overflow: 'hidden',
                  background: 'var(--bone-2)', position: 'relative', flexShrink: 0,
                }}>
                  <Image
                    src={`/api/proxy/image?url=${encodeURIComponent(t.photoUrl)}&w=88`}
                    alt={t.name}
                    fill
                    sizes="44px"
                    className="object-cover object-top"
                    unoptimized
                  />
                </div>
              )}
              <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 800, textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>
                  {t.name}
                </div>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, marginTop: 4, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                  <span style={{
                    fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 800, padding: '2px 8px',
                    borderRadius: 'var(--r-pill)', background: TYPE_COLORS[t.type],
                    color: 'var(--white)',
                  }}>
                    {TYPE_LABELS[t.type]}
                  </span>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--muted)', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                    {t.position}
                  </span>
                  {t.clubFrom && <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--muted)' }}>← {t.clubFrom}</span>}
                  {t.clubTo && <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--muted)' }}>→ {t.clubTo}</span>}
                  {t.type === 'rumor' && <span style={{ fontSize: 11 }}>{'🔥'.repeat(t.reliability)}</span>}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, flexShrink: 0 }}>
                <button onClick={() => startEdit(t)}
                  style={{
                    background: 'var(--bone)', border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                    borderRadius: 8, padding: '6px 12px', cursor: 'pointer',
                    fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--black)', fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)',
                    fontWeight: 700,
                  }}>
                  Editar
                </button>
                <button onClick={() => handleDelete(t.id)}
                  style={{
                    background: 'none', border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                    borderRadius: 8, padding: '6px 12px', cursor: 'pointer',
                    fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--muted)', fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)',
                    fontWeight: 700,
                  }}>
                  Eliminar
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))
        }
    </div>
  )
}

<file path="src/app/api/live/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { ESPN_SCOREBOARD, ESPN_SUMMARY } from '@/lib/constants'
import { getConfig } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/AppConfigRepository'
import { supabase } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/client'

// Cache 60s — multiple users share the same ESPN response
export const revalidate = 0

export interface LiveEvent {
  type: 'goal' | 'yellowCard' | 'redCard' | 'substitution'
  minute: string
  team: 'home' | 'away'
  playerName: string
}

export interface LiveMatch {
  espnId: string
  state: 'pre' | 'in' | 'post'
  status: string // "Scheduled" | "1st Half" | "Half Time" | "2nd Half" | "Full Time"
  clock: string // "23'" | "HT" | ""
  homeTeam: string
  awayTeam: string
  homeScore: number
  awayScore: number
  events: LiveEvent[]
  venue: string
  startDate: string
}

async function fetchEspn(url: string) {
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    headers: { 'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0' },
    next: { revalidate: 60 },
  })
  if (!res.ok) return null
  return res.json()
}

export async function GET() {
  // In dev: use mock if it exists
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {
    const mock = await getConfig('live_match_mock')
    if (mock) return NextResponse.json({ match: JSON.parse(mock) })
  }

  const scoreboard = await fetchEspn(ESPN_SCOREBOARD)
  if (!scoreboard) return NextResponse.json({ match: null })

  const events: unknown[] = scoreboard.events ?? []

  // Find Ceuta match
  const ceutaEvent = events.find((e: unknown) => {
    const ev = e as Record<string, unknown>
    return JSON.stringify(ev).toLowerCase().includes('ceuta')
  }) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined

  if (!ceutaEvent) return NextResponse.json({ match: null })

  const competition = (ceutaEvent.competitions as Record<string, unknown>[])?.[0]
  if (!competition) return NextResponse.json({ match: null })

  const status = competition.status as Record<string, unknown>
  const statusType = status?.type as Record<string, unknown>
  const competitors = competition.competitors as Record<string, unknown>[]

  const home = competitors?.find(
    (c) => (c as Record<string, unknown>).homeAway === 'home'
  ) as Record<string, unknown>
  const away = competitors?.find(
    (c) => (c as Record<string, unknown>).homeAway === 'away'
  ) as Record<string, unknown>
  const homeTeamData = home?.team as Record<string, unknown>
  const awayTeamData = away?.team as Record<string, unknown>
  const venue = competition.venue as Record<string, unknown>

  const state = statusType?.state as string
  const clockDisplay = (status?.displayClock as string) ?? ''
  const statusDesc = statusType?.description as string

  // Guard: cross-check with DB match date. ESPN occasionally seeds upcoming fixtures
  // with corrupt state ('in', clock '1''). A match can only be live if the DB says
  // it's within a ±4h window of its real kickoff time.
  let guardedState = state
  if (state === 'in') {
    const espnId = String(ceutaEvent.id ?? '')
    const { data: dbMatch } = await supabase
      .from('matches')
      .select('date')
      .eq('espn_event_id', espnId)
      .maybeSingle()
    if (dbMatch?.date) {
      const kickoff = new Date(dbMatch.date)
      const now = new Date()
      const diffHours = (now.getTime() - kickoff.getTime()) / 3_600_000
      // Only trust 'in' state if kickoff was between 0 and 4 hours ago
      if (diffHours < 0 || diffHours > 4) guardedState = 'pre'
    }
  }

  // Fetch play-by-play events only during/after match
  let liveEvents: LiveEvent[] = []
  if (guardedState === 'in' || guardedState === 'post') {
    const summary = await fetchEspn(`${ESPN_SUMMARY}?event=${ceutaEvent.id}`)
    const plays: unknown[] = summary?.plays ?? []

    liveEvents = plays
      .filter((p: unknown) => {
        const play = p as Record<string, unknown>
        const type = (((play.type as Record<string, unknown>)?.text as string) ?? '').toLowerCase()
        return type.includes('goal') || type.includes('yellow') || type.includes('red')
      })
      .map((p: unknown) => {
        const play = p as Record<string, unknown>
        const typeText = (
          ((play.type as Record<string, unknown>)?.text as string) ?? ''
        ).toLowerCase()
        const clock = play.clock as Record<string, unknown>
        const participants = (play.participants as Record<string, unknown>[]) ?? []
        const athlete = participants[0]?.athlete as Record<string, unknown>

        let type: LiveEvent['type'] = 'goal'
        if (typeText.includes('yellow')) type = 'yellowCard'
        else if (typeText.includes('red')) type = 'redCard'
        else if (typeText.includes('substitution')) type = 'substitution'

        const playTeamId = (play.team as Record<string, unknown>)?.id
        const team: 'home' | 'away' = playTeamId === homeTeamData?.id ? 'home' : 'away'

        return {
          type,
          minute: (clock?.displayValue as string) ?? '',
          team,
          playerName: (athlete?.displayName as string) ?? '',
        }
      })
  }

  const match: LiveMatch = {
    espnId: ceutaEvent.id as string,
    state: (guardedState as 'pre' | 'in' | 'post') ?? 'pre',
    status: statusDesc ?? '',
    clock: guardedState === 'in' ? clockDisplay || '' : '',
    homeTeam: (homeTeamData?.displayName as string) ?? '',
    awayTeam: (awayTeamData?.displayName as string) ?? '',
    homeScore: parseInt((home?.score as string) ?? '0'),
    awayScore: parseInt((away?.score as string) ?? '0'),
    events: liveEvents,
    venue: ((venue as Record<string, unknown>)?.fullName as string) ?? '',
    startDate: (ceutaEvent.date as string) ?? '',
  }

  // Don't show a finished match that ended more than 3 hours ago
  if (guardedState === 'post') {
    const matchDate = new Date(ceutaEvent.date as string)
    const hoursSinceMatch = (Date.now() - matchDate.getTime()) / 3_600_000
    if (hoursSinceMatch > 3) return NextResponse.json({ match: null })

    // If ESPN didn't return events, try to get them from stored state
    if (liveEvents.length === 0) {
      const storedRaw = await getConfig('live_match_state')
      if (storedRaw) {
        try {
          const storedState = JSON.parse(storedRaw)
          if (storedState.espnId === ceutaEvent.id && storedState.events?.length) {
            liveEvents = storedState.events
          }
        } catch {
          /* ignore */
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ match })
}
</file>

<file path="src/app/api/matches/seed/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { scrapeAllScheduled } from '@/infrastructure/scrapers/adceutaScraper'
import { supabase } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/client'
import { CURRENT_SEASON, COMPETITION } from '@/lib/constants'

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
  const secret = searchParams.get('secret')

  if (secret !== process.env.TEST_SECRET) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })
  }

  const matches = await scrapeAllScheduled()
  if (matches.length === 0) {
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: 'No matches found from ESPN' })
  }

  // Delete all scheduled matches for current season first
  await supabase
    .from('matches')
    .delete()
    .eq('status', 'scheduled')
    .eq('season', CURRENT_SEASON)

  const { error } = await supabase.from('matches').insert(
    matches.map((m) => ({
      external_id: `${m.date}-${m.homeTeam}-${m.awayTeam}`.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '-'),
      home_team: m.homeTeam,
      away_team: m.awayTeam,
      date: m.date,
      time: m.time,
      weekday: m.weekday,
      is_home: m.isHome,
      tickets_url: m.ticketsUrl,
      status: 'scheduled',
      season: CURRENT_SEASON,
      competition: COMPETITION,
      espn_event_id: m.espnEventId,
    }))
  )

  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: error.message }, { status: 500 })

  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, inserted: matches.length, season: CURRENT_SEASON })
}
</file>

<file path="src/app/api/news/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { NewsSupabaseRepository } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/NewsSupabaseRepository'
import { decodeEntities } from '@/lib/decodeEntities'

const repo = new NewsSupabaseRepository()

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
  const limit = Math.min(parseInt(searchParams.get('limit') ?? '10'), 50)
  const offset = Math.max(parseInt(searchParams.get('offset') ?? '0'), 0)

  const raw = await repo.getLatest(limit, offset)
  const news = raw.map(n => ({ ...n, title: decodeEntities(n.title) }))
  return NextResponse.json(
    { news },
    {
      headers: {
        'Cache-Control': 'public, s-maxage=60, stale-while-revalidate=300',
      },
    }
  )
}
</file>

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'
import type { Transfer } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/TransfersRepository'

// ─── Pure logic extracted from MercadoClient ─────────────────────────────────

function isExitRumor(t: Pick<Transfer, 'clubFrom'>): boolean {
  return t.clubFrom === 'AD Ceuta FC'
}

const REL_TEXT: Record<number, string> = { 3: 'Muy fiable', 2: 'Posible', 1: 'Especulación' }

const ROUTE_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  in:    'Procede de',
  out:   'Destino',
  renew: 'Renueva hasta',
  rumor: 'Suena desde',
}

function makeTransfer(overrides: Partial<Transfer> = {}): Transfer {
  return {
    id: '1',
    name: 'Jugador Test',
    position: 'DEL',
    type: 'in',
    clubFrom: 'Club Origen',
    clubTo: null,
    photoUrl: null,
    reliability: 2,
    notes: null,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    ...overrides,
  }
}

// ─── isExitRumor ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe('isExitRumor', () => {
  it('returns true when clubFrom is AD Ceuta FC', () => {
    expect(isExitRumor({ clubFrom: 'AD Ceuta FC' })).toBe(true)
  })

  it('returns false when clubFrom is another club', () => {
    expect(isExitRumor({ clubFrom: 'Real Madrid' })).toBe(false)
  })

  it('returns false when clubFrom is null', () => {
    expect(isExitRumor({ clubFrom: null })).toBe(false)
  })
})

// ─── REL_TEXT ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe('REL_TEXT', () => {
  it('reliability 3 → Muy fiable', () => {
    expect(REL_TEXT[3]).toBe('Muy fiable')
  })

  it('reliability 2 → Posible', () => {
    expect(REL_TEXT[2]).toBe('Posible')
  })

  it('reliability 1 → Especulación', () => {
    expect(REL_TEXT[1]).toBe('Especulación')
  })
})

// ─── ROUTE_LABEL ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe('ROUTE_LABEL', () => {
  it('in → Procede de', () => {
    expect(ROUTE_LABEL['in']).toBe('Procede de')
  })

  it('out → Destino', () => {
    expect(ROUTE_LABEL['out']).toBe('Destino')
  })

  it('renew → Renueva hasta', () => {
    expect(ROUTE_LABEL['renew']).toBe('Renueva hasta')
  })

  it('rumor → Suena desde', () => {
    expect(ROUTE_LABEL['rumor']).toBe('Suena desde')
  })
})

// ─── Transfer counts per tab ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe('transfer counts per tab', () => {
  const transfers: Transfer[] = [
    makeTransfer({ id: '1', type: 'in' }),
    makeTransfer({ id: '2', type: 'in' }),
    makeTransfer({ id: '3', type: 'out' }),
    makeTransfer({ id: '4', type: 'renew' }),
    makeTransfer({ id: '5', type: 'rumor' }),
    makeTransfer({ id: '6', type: 'rumor' }),
  ]

  it('counts in transfers correctly', () => {
    expect(transfers.filter(t => t.type === 'in').length).toBe(2)
  })

  it('counts out transfers correctly', () => {
    expect(transfers.filter(t => t.type === 'out').length).toBe(1)
  })

  it('counts renew transfers correctly', () => {
    expect(transfers.filter(t => t.type === 'renew').length).toBe(1)
  })

  it('counts rumor transfers correctly', () => {
    expect(transfers.filter(t => t.type === 'rumor').length).toBe(2)
  })

  it('filters correctly by active tab', () => {
    const filtered = transfers.filter(t => t.type === 'rumor')
    expect(filtered).toHaveLength(2)
    expect(filtered.every(t => t.type === 'rumor')).toBe(true)
  })
})

// ─── Exit rumor routing ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe('exit rumor routing', () => {
  it('a rumor where clubFrom is AD Ceuta FC is an exit rumor', () => {
    const t = makeTransfer({ type: 'rumor', clubFrom: 'AD Ceuta FC', clubTo: 'Algeciras CF' })
    expect(isExitRumor(t)).toBe(true)
  })

  it('exit rumor uses clubTo as destination', () => {
    const t = makeTransfer({ type: 'rumor', clubFrom: 'AD Ceuta FC', clubTo: 'Algeciras CF' })
    const routeClub = isExitRumor(t) ? (t.clubTo ?? 'Por determinar') : (t.clubFrom ?? null)
    expect(routeClub).toBe('Algeciras CF')
  })

  it('exit rumor with no clubTo falls back to Por determinar', () => {
    const t = makeTransfer({ type: 'rumor', clubFrom: 'AD Ceuta FC', clubTo: null })
    const routeClub = isExitRumor(t) ? (t.clubTo ?? 'Por determinar') : (t.clubFrom ?? null)
    expect(routeClub).toBe('Por determinar')
  })

  it('arrival rumor uses clubFrom as source', () => {
    const t = makeTransfer({ type: 'rumor', clubFrom: 'Cádiz CF', clubTo: null })
    const routeClub = isExitRumor(t) ? (t.clubTo ?? 'Por determinar') : (t.clubFrom ?? null)
    expect(routeClub).toBe('Cádiz CF')
  })
})

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'
import type { Transfer } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/TransfersRepository'

function makeTransfer(overrides: Partial<Transfer> = {}): Transfer {
  return {
    id: '1',
    name: 'Jugador Test',
    position: 'MED',
    type: 'renew',
    clubFrom: null,
    clubTo: null,
    photoUrl: null,
    reliability: 2,
    notes: null,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    ...overrides,
  }
}

// Logic mirrored from MercadoClient line 172:
// type === 'renew' ? (t.clubTo ?? 'AD Ceuta FC') : routeClub
function renewLabel(t: Pick<Transfer, 'type' | 'clubTo'>): string {
  return t.type === 'renew' ? (t.clubTo ?? 'AD Ceuta FC') : '—'
}

describe('renovados — etiqueta de fecha/destino', () => {
  it('muestra clubTo cuando está definido (fecha de contrato)', () => {
    const t = makeTransfer({ clubTo: 'Hasta 2028' })
    expect(renewLabel(t)).toBe('Hasta 2028')
  })

  it('muestra AD Ceuta FC como fallback cuando clubTo es null', () => {
    const t = makeTransfer({ clubTo: null })
    expect(renewLabel(t)).toBe('AD Ceuta FC')
  })

  it('muestra temporada cuando se usa ese formato', () => {
    const t = makeTransfer({ clubTo: 'Temporada 26/27' })
    expect(renewLabel(t)).toBe('Temporada 26/27')
  })

  it('no aplica a otros tipos de movimiento', () => {
    const t = makeTransfer({ type: 'in', clubTo: 'AD Ceuta FC' })
    expect(renewLabel(t)).toBe('—')
  })
})

describe('renovados — jugadores actuales del Ceuta', () => {
  const renovados: Transfer[] = [
    makeTransfer({ id: '1', name: 'Pedro López',     position: 'POR', clubTo: 'Hasta 2028' }),
    makeTransfer({ id: '2', name: 'Yann Bodiger',    position: 'MED', clubTo: 'Hasta 2028' }),
    makeTransfer({ id: '3', name: 'José Juan Romero',position: 'ENT', clubTo: 'Temporada 26/27' }),
    makeTransfer({ id: '4', name: 'Guille Vallejo',  position: 'POR', clubTo: 'Temporada 26/27' }),
  ]

  it('todos son tipo renew', () => {
    expect(renovados.every(t => t.type === 'renew')).toBe(true)
  })

  it('Pedro López renueva hasta 2028', () => {
    const t = renovados.find(t => t.name === 'Pedro López')!
    expect(renewLabel(t)).toBe('Hasta 2028')
  })

  it('Yann Bodiger renueva hasta 2028', () => {
    const t = renovados.find(t => t.name === 'Yann Bodiger')!
    expect(renewLabel(t)).toBe('Hasta 2028')
  })

  it('José Juan Romero — el míster sigue con nosotros 👑', () => {
    const t = renovados.find(t => t.name === 'José Juan Romero')!
    expect(t.position).toBe('ENT')
    expect(renewLabel(t)).toBe('Temporada 26/27')
  })
})

<file path="src/components/ui/__tests__/installModal.test.ts">
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, vi } from 'vitest'

// Logic mirrored from InstallModal.tsx

type Platform = 'ios' | 'android' | 'desktop'

function detectPlatform(ua: string): Platform {
  if (/iphone|ipad|ipod/i.test(ua)) return 'ios'
  if (/android/i.test(ua)) return 'android'
  return 'desktop'
}

function shouldShow(opts: {
  forceShow: boolean
  isStandalone: boolean
  snoozedUntil: number | null
}): boolean {
  if (opts.forceShow) return true
  if (opts.isStandalone) return false
  if (opts.snoozedUntil !== null && Date.now() < opts.snoozedUntil) return false
  return true
}

// dismiss = solo cierra la sesión (sin localStorage)
function dismiss(setVisible: (v: boolean) => void) {
  setVisible(false)
}

// snooze = cierra + guarda 7 días en localStorage
function snooze(setVisible: (v: boolean) => void, storage: Map<string, string>) {
  storage.set('install_snoozed', String(Date.now() + 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000))
  setVisible(false)
}

describe('detectPlatform', () => {
  it('detecta iOS en iPhone', () => {
    expect(detectPlatform('Mozilla/5.0 (iPhone; CPU iPhone OS 17_0)')).toBe('ios')
  })

  it('detecta iOS en iPad', () => {
    expect(detectPlatform('Mozilla/5.0 (iPad; CPU OS 17_0)')).toBe('ios')
  })

  it('detecta Android', () => {
    expect(detectPlatform('Mozilla/5.0 (Linux; Android 14; Pixel 7)')).toBe('android')
  })

  it('detecta desktop en Chrome Mac', () => {
    expect(detectPlatform('Mozilla/5.0 (Macintosh; Intel Mac OS X 10_15_7) Chrome/126')).toBe('desktop')
  })

  it('es case-insensitive para iPhone', () => {
    expect(detectPlatform('...IPHONE...')).toBe('ios')
  })
})

describe('shouldShow', () => {
  it('fuerza mostrar con ?install=1 aunque esté en standalone', () => {
    expect(shouldShow({ forceShow: true, isStandalone: true, snoozedUntil: null })).toBe(true)
  })

  it('fuerza mostrar con ?install=1 aunque esté snoozed', () => {
    const future = Date.now() + 999999
    expect(shouldShow({ forceShow: true, isStandalone: false, snoozedUntil: future })).toBe(true)
  })

  it('no muestra si está en modo standalone (PWA instalada)', () => {
    expect(shouldShow({ forceShow: false, isStandalone: true, snoozedUntil: null })).toBe(false)
  })

  it('no muestra si está dentro del periodo de snooze', () => {
    const future = Date.now() + 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
    expect(shouldShow({ forceShow: false, isStandalone: false, snoozedUntil: future })).toBe(false)
  })

  it('muestra si el snooze ya ha expirado', () => {
    const past = Date.now() - 1000
    expect(shouldShow({ forceShow: false, isStandalone: false, snoozedUntil: past })).toBe(true)
  })

  it('muestra en primera visita sin snooze ni standalone', () => {
    expect(shouldShow({ forceShow: false, isStandalone: false, snoozedUntil: null })).toBe(true)
  })
})

describe('dismiss vs snooze', () => {
  it('dismiss solo cierra el modal sin tocar el storage', () => {
    const storage = new Map<string, string>()
    let visible = true
    dismiss((v) => { visible = v })
    expect(visible).toBe(false)
    expect(storage.has('install_snoozed')).toBe(false)
  })

  it('snooze cierra el modal Y guarda 7 días en storage', () => {
    const storage = new Map<string, string>()
    let visible = true
    const before = Date.now()
    snooze((v) => { visible = v }, storage)
    const after = Date.now()
    expect(visible).toBe(false)
    const snoozedUntil = parseInt(storage.get('install_snoozed')!)
    expect(snoozedUntil).toBeGreaterThan(before + 6 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)
    expect(snoozedUntil).toBeLessThanOrEqual(after + 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 + 100)
  })

  it('tras dismiss, el modal vuelve a mostrarse en la siguiente visita', () => {
    // dismiss no guarda snooze → shouldShow devuelve true en próxima visita
    expect(shouldShow({ forceShow: false, isStandalone: false, snoozedUntil: null })).toBe(true)
  })

  it('tras snooze, el modal NO se muestra en los próximos 7 días', () => {
    const snoozedUntil = Date.now() + 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
    expect(shouldShow({ forceShow: false, isStandalone: false, snoozedUntil })).toBe(false)
  })
})
</file>

<file path="src/features/club/PlayerDetailPage.tsx">
'use client'

import Link from 'next/link'
import Image from 'next/image'
import type { CareerEntry, Player } from './clubTypes'

const NAT_FLAGS: Record<string, string> = {
  españa: '🇪🇸',
  mali: '🇲🇱',
  senegal: '🇸🇳',
  guinea: '🇬🇳',
  'guinea-bisáu': '🇬🇼',
  'guinea bisáu': '🇬🇼',
  argentina: '🇦🇷',
  uruguay: '🇺🇾',
  colombia: '🇨🇴',
  francia: '🇫🇷',
  marruecos: '🇲🇦',
  argelia: '🇩🇿',
  camerún: '🇨🇲',
  brasil: '🇧🇷',
  portugal: '🇵🇹',
  ghana: '🇬🇭',
  nigeria: '🇳🇬',
  'costa de marfil': '🇨🇮',
  'ivory coast': '🇨🇮',
  'estados unidos': '🇺🇸',
  'united states': '🇺🇸',
  usa: '🇺🇸',
  'burkina faso': '🇧🇫',
}

function flag(nat: string): string {
  if (!nat) return ''
  const key = nat.toLowerCase()
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(NAT_FLAGS)) {
    if (key.includes(k)) return v
  }
  return ''
}

function capitalize(s: string): string {
  return s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1).toLowerCase()
}

function splitName(name: string): { first: string; last: string } {
  const parts = name.trim().split(' ')
  if (parts.length === 1) return { first: '', last: parts[0] }
  const last = parts[parts.length - 1]
  const first = parts.slice(0, -1).join(' ')
  return { first, last }
}

interface PlayerStats {
  appearances: number | null
  goals: number | null
  assists: number | null
  shots: number | null
  shots_on_target: number | null
  saves: number | null
  yellow_cards: number | null
  red_cards: number | null
}

interface Props {
  player: Player
  stats: PlayerStats | null
}

export function PlayerDetailPage({ player, stats }: Props) {
  const isGK = player.position.toUpperCase().includes('PORTERO')
  const hasNat = player.nationality && !player.nationality.match(/^\d+/)
  const natFlag = hasNat ? flag(player.nationality) : ''
  const { first, last } = splitName(player.name)

  const heroStat = isGK
    ? { label: 'Paradas', value: stats?.saves ?? null }
    : { label: 'Goles', value: stats?.goals ?? null }

  return (
    <div id="app-root">
        {/* ── TOP BAR ── */}
        <div className="pd-topbar">
          <Link href="/club?tab=plantilla" className="pd-back-btn">
            <svg
              viewBox="0 0 24 24"
              fill="none"
              stroke="currentColor"
              strokeWidth="2.5"
              strokeLinecap="round"
              strokeLinejoin="round"
            >
              <path d="M19 12H5M12 19l-7-7 7-7" />
            </svg>
            Plantilla
          </Link>
        </div>

        <div className="scroll-area">
        {/* ── HERO ── */}
        <div className="pd-hero">
          <div className="pd-hero-bignum">{player.dorsal ?? ''}</div>

          {player.photo_url && (
            <div className="pd-hero-photo">
              <Image
                src={player.photo_url}
                alt={player.name}
                width={220}
                height={220}
                priority
                sizes="220px"
                quality={85}
                className="pd-hero-photo-img"
              />
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="pd-hero-content">
            <div className="pd-hero-eyebrow">AD Ceuta FC</div>
            {first && <div className="pd-hero-firstname">{first}</div>}
            <div className="pd-hero-lastname">{last}</div>
            <span className="pd-pos-badge">{capitalize(player.position)}</span>
          </div>

          {/* franja roja bottom */}
          <div className="pd-hero-redline" />
        </div>

        {/* ── META STRIP ── */}
        <div className="pd-meta-strip">
          <div className="pd-meta-cell">
            <div className="pd-meta-value">#{player.dorsal ?? '—'}</div>
            <div className="pd-meta-label">Dorsal</div>
          </div>
          <div className="pd-meta-cell">
            <div className="pd-meta-value">{capitalize(player.position)}</div>
            <div className="pd-meta-label">Posición</div>
          </div>
          <div className="pd-meta-cell">
            <div className="pd-meta-value">{player.position_group}</div>
            <div className="pd-meta-label">Grupo</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ── BODY ── */}
        <div className="pd-body">
          {/* ── FICHA FÍSICA ── */}
          {(player.height || player.weight || hasNat) && (
            <>
              <div className="pd-sec-eyebrow">Ficha técnica</div>
              <div className="pd-bio-grid">
                {player.height && (
                  <div className="pd-bio-tile">
                    <div className="pd-bio-value">
                      {player.height.replace(/\s*(cm|CM)/i, '')}
                      <span className="pd-bio-unit">cm</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="pd-bio-label">Altura</div>
                  </div>
                )}
                {player.weight && (
                  <div className="pd-bio-tile">
                    <div className="pd-bio-value">
                      {player.weight.replace(/\s*(kg|KG)/i, '')}
                      <span className="pd-bio-unit">kg</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="pd-bio-label">Peso</div>
                  </div>
                )}
                {hasNat && (
                  <div className="pd-bio-tile">
                    <div className="pd-bio-flag">{natFlag}</div>
                    <div className="pd-bio-label">{player.nationality}</div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          {/* ── BIO ── */}
          {player.bio && (
            <>
              <div className="pd-sec-eyebrow">Sobre el jugador</div>
              <div className="mb-6 rounded-[10px] bg-ceuta-bg-soft border-l-[3px] border-ceuta-red px-4 py-3 text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-ceuta-black">
                {player.bio}
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          {/* ── ESTADÍSTICAS ── */}
          {stats && (
            <div className="pd-stats-card">
              <div className="pd-stats-eyebrow">Estadísticas · 2025–26</div>
              <div className="pd-stat-hero">
                <div className="pd-stat-hero-v">{heroStat.value ?? 0}</div>
                <div className="pd-stat-hero-l">{heroStat.label}</div>
              </div>
              <div className="pd-stat-row">
                {stats.appearances != null && (
                  <div className="pd-stat-mini">
                    <div className="pd-stat-mini-v">{stats.appearances}</div>
                    <div className="pd-stat-mini-l">Partidos</div>
                  </div>
                )}
                {!isGK && stats.assists != null && (
                  <div className="pd-stat-mini">
                    <div className="pd-stat-mini-v">{stats.assists}</div>
                    <div className="pd-stat-mini-l">Asistencias</div>
                  </div>
                )}
                {stats.yellow_cards != null && (
                  <div className="pd-stat-mini">
                    <div className="pd-stat-mini-v pd-stat-mini-v--gold">{stats.yellow_cards}</div>
                    <div className="pd-stat-mini-l">Amarillas</div>
                  </div>
                )}
                {stats.red_cards != null && (
                  <div className="pd-stat-mini">
                    <div className="pd-stat-mini-v">{stats.red_cards}</div>
                    <div className="pd-stat-mini-l">Rojas</div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ── TRAYECTORIA ── */}
          {player.career_history && player.career_history.length > 0 && (
            <>
              <div className="pd-sec-eyebrow">Trayectoria · Equipos anteriores</div>
              <div className="pd-timeline">
                {(player.career_history as CareerEntry[]).map((entry, i) => {
                  const isCurrent = i === 0
                  return (
                    <div key={i} className={`pd-tl-item${isCurrent ? ' pd-tl-item--current' : ''}`}>
                      <div className="pd-tl-marker">
                        <div className="pd-tl-dot" />
                        <div className="pd-tl-years">{entry.seasons}</div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="pd-tl-body">
                        {entry.logo ? (
                          <Image
                            src={entry.logo}
                            alt={entry.club}
                            width={36}
                            height={36}
                            sizes="36px"
                            quality={70}
                            className="pd-tl-crest-img"
                          />
                        ) : (
                          <div className="pd-tl-crest">{entry.club.slice(0, 3).toUpperCase()}</div>
                        )}
                        <div className="pd-tl-info">
                          <div className="pd-tl-club">{entry.club}</div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )
                })}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
        </div>
    </div>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/features/matches/__tests__/matchTime.test.ts">
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'

// Logic mirrored from PartidosPage and LiveMatchCard:
// m.time || 'Por confirmar'  →  now hardcoded to 'Por confirmar' always
function displayTime(time: string | null | undefined): string {
  return 'Por confirmar'
}

// Legacy fallback logic (what was before, for regression)
function displayTimeLegacy(time: string | null | undefined): string {
  return time || 'Por confirmar'
}

describe('displayTime — todos los partidos muestran Por confirmar', () => {
  it('siempre devuelve Por confirmar independientemente del valor', () => {
    expect(displayTime('20:00')).toBe('Por confirmar')
  })

  it('devuelve Por confirmar cuando time es null', () => {
    expect(displayTime(null)).toBe('Por confirmar')
  })

  it('devuelve Por confirmar cuando time es undefined', () => {
    expect(displayTime(undefined)).toBe('Por confirmar')
  })

  it('devuelve Por confirmar cuando time es cadena vacía', () => {
    expect(displayTime('')).toBe('Por confirmar')
  })
})

describe('displayTime legacy — fallback cuando time no está definido', () => {
  it('muestra la hora cuando está definida', () => {
    expect(displayTimeLegacy('20:00')).toBe('20:00')
  })

  it('muestra Por confirmar cuando time es null', () => {
    expect(displayTimeLegacy(null)).toBe('Por confirmar')
  })

  it('muestra Por confirmar cuando time es cadena vacía', () => {
    expect(displayTimeLegacy('')).toBe('Por confirmar')
  })
})

<file path="src/infrastructure/push/sendNewsNotification.ts">
import webpush from 'web-push'
import { ParsedArticle } from '@/infrastructure/rss/RssFetcher'
import { decodeEntities } from '@/lib/decodeEntities'
import {
  getAllSubscriptions,
  deleteSubscription,
} from '@/infrastructure/supabase/PushSubscriptionsRepository'

const SOURCE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  oficial: 'AD Ceuta FC',
  faro: 'El Faro de Ceuta',
  elpueblo: 'El Pueblo de Ceuta',
  marca: 'Marca',
  mundodeportivo: 'Mundo Deportivo',
  '20minutos': '20 Minutos',
  sport: 'Sport',
  as: 'AS',
  confidencial: 'El Confidencial',
  abc: 'ABC',
  lavanguardia: 'La Vanguardia',
  cope: 'COPE',
  ser: 'Cadena SER',
  ceutatv: 'Ceuta Televisión',
  rtvc: 'RTV Ceuta',
  laverdad: 'La Verdad de Ceuta',
  ceutaactualidad: 'Ceuta Actualidad',
  onefootball: 'OneFootball',
  ceutaaldia: 'Ceuta al Día',
  elpais: 'El País',
  rtve: 'RTVE',
  goal: 'Goal.com',
  elgoldigital: 'El Gol Digital',
  fondosegunda: 'Fondo Segunda',
  transfermarkt: 'Transfermarkt',
}

export async function sendNewsNotification(
  article: ParsedArticle
): Promise<{ recipients: number; sentOk: number; sentFail: number }> {
  webpush.setVapidDetails(
    'mailto:angelhpascual@gmail.com',
    process.env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY!,
    process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY!
  )

  const subs = await getAllSubscriptions()
  if (subs.length === 0) return { recipients: 0, sentOk: 0, sentFail: 0 }

  const sourceLabel = SOURCE_LABELS[article.source] ?? 'Noticias'
  const payload = JSON.stringify({
    title: '🔴 Noticias',
    body: `${sourceLabel} · ${decodeEntities(article.title)}`,
    tag: `news-${article.externalId}`,
    url: article.url,
    icon: '/icon-512.png',
  })

  let sentOk = 0
  let sentFail = 0

  await Promise.allSettled(
    subs.map((sub) =>
      webpush
        .sendNotification(
          { endpoint: sub.endpoint, keys: { p256dh: sub.p256dh, auth: sub.auth } },
          payload
        )
        .then(() => {
          sentOk++
        })
        .catch(async (err: { statusCode?: number }) => {
          sentFail++
          if (err.statusCode === 410 || err.statusCode === 404) {
            await deleteSubscription(sub.endpoint)
          }
        })
    )
  )

  return { recipients: subs.length, sentOk, sentFail }
}

<file path="src/infrastructure/rss/adceutaFetcher.ts">
import { ParsedArticle } from './RssFetcher'
import { downloadAndUploadImage } from '@/infrastructure/storage/imageUploader'
import { decodeEntities } from '@/lib/decodeEntities'

interface WPPost {
  id: number
  link: string
  date: string
  title: { rendered: string }
  excerpt: { rendered: string }
  _embedded?: {
    'wp:featuredmedia'?: Array<{ source_url?: string }>
  }
}

export async function fetchAdceutaOfficialNews(limit = 15): Promise<ParsedArticle[]> {
  const res = await fetch(`https://adceutafc.com/wp-json/wp/v2/posts?per_page=${limit}&_embed=1`, {
    headers: { 'User-Agent': 'FunHubCeuta/1.0' },
    next: { revalidate: 0 },
  })

  if (!res.ok) return []

  const posts: WPPost[] = await res.json()

  // Process posts: download + upload images to Supabase Storage
  const articles = await Promise.all(
    posts.map(async (post) => {
      const rawImageUrl = post._embedded?.['wp:featuredmedia']?.[0]?.source_url ?? null
      const title = decodeEntities(post.title.rendered.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim())
      const description = decodeEntities(
        post.excerpt.rendered.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim()
      ).slice(0, 300)

      // Download image and store in Supabase Storage to avoid hotlink issues
      let imageUrl: string | null = null
      if (rawImageUrl) {
        imageUrl = await downloadAndUploadImage(rawImageUrl, `adceuta-${post.id}`)
      }

      return {
        externalId: `adceuta-wp-${post.id}`,
        title,
        description: description || null,
        url: post.link,
        imageUrl,
        source: 'oficial',
        publishedAt: new Date(post.date).toISOString(),
      }
    })
  )

  return articles
}
