<file path="src/infrastructure/rss/ceutadeportivaFetcher.ts">
import type { ParsedArticle } from './RssFetcher'

const BASE = 'https://ceutadeportiva.com'
const SOURCE = 'ceutadeportiva'

const FOOTBALL_KEYWORDS = [
  'ceuta', 'bodiger', 'kone', 'koné', 'murube', 'caballa', 'fichaje', 'renovacion',
  'salida', 'jugador', 'entrenador', 'plantilla', 'mercado', 'segunda', 'laliga',
]

// Exclude articles that are clearly about other sports or non-club content
const OTHER_SPORT_KEYWORDS = [
  'waterpolo', 'water polo', 'natacion', 'natación', 'atletismo', 'baloncesto',
  'basketball', 'balonmano', 'handbal', 'tenis', 'padel', 'pádel', 'ciclismo',
  'boxeo', 'judo', 'karate', 'ajedrez', 'voleibol', 'rugby', 'hockey',
  'cn caballa', 'club natacion', 'club náutico', 'triatlon', 'triatlón',
  'alevines', 'juveniles', 'benjamin', 'benjamín', 'infantil',
  'campeonato de españa de', 'liga nacional de',
  // Non-football city/institution content
  'icd', 'instituto ceutí de deportes', 'instituto ceuti', 'leyendas del deporte',
  'exposición', 'exposicion', 'inauguración', 'inauguracion', 'escuela deportiva',
  'deporte escolar', 'deporte base', 'deporte femenino ceuta',
]

export function isFootball(title: string, url: string): boolean {
  // Only evaluate the slug part of the URL, not the domain (which always contains "ceuta")
  const slug = url.replace(/^https?:\/\/[^/]+/, '')
  const text = (title + ' ' + slug).toLowerCase()
  if (OTHER_SPORT_KEYWORDS.some((kw) => text.includes(kw))) return false
  return FOOTBALL_KEYWORDS.some((kw) => text.includes(kw))
}

function slugToId(url: string): string {
  return url.replace(/^https?:\/\/[^/]+\//, '').replace(/\/$/, '').replace(/\//g, '_')
}

export async function fetchCeutaDeportiva(limit = 15): Promise<ParsedArticle[]> {
  let html: string
  try {
    const res = await fetch(BASE, {
      headers: {
        'User-Agent':
          'Mozilla/5.0 (iPhone; CPU iPhone OS 16_0 like Mac OS X) AppleWebKit/605.1.15 (KHTML, like Gecko) Version/16.0 Mobile/15E148 Safari/604.1',
        Accept: 'text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/xml;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8',
        'Accept-Language': 'es-ES,es;q=0.9',
      },
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10000),
    })
    if (!res.ok) return []
    html = await res.text()
  } catch {
    return []
  }

  // Extract article URLs from homepage (WordPress date-based permalinks)
  const urlPattern = /href="(https:\/\/ceutadeportiva\.com\/\d{4}\/\d{2}\/[^"#?]+)"/g
  const seen = new Set<string>()
  const urls: string[] = []
  let match
  while ((match = urlPattern.exec(html)) !== null) {
    const url = match[1].replace(/\/$/, '')
    if (!seen.has(url)) {
      seen.add(url)
      urls.push(url)
    }
    if (urls.length >= limit * 2) break
  }

  // Fetch each article page to get OG meta
  const articles: ParsedArticle[] = []

  await Promise.allSettled(
    urls.slice(0, limit).map(async (url) => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(url, {
          headers: {
            'User-Agent':
              'Mozilla/5.0 (iPhone; CPU iPhone OS 16_0 like Mac OS X) AppleWebKit/605.1.15 (KHTML, like Gecko) Version/16.0 Mobile/15E148 Safari/604.1',
          },
          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000),
        })
        if (!res.ok) return
        const html = await res.text()

        const title = (html.match(/property="og:title" content="([^"]+)"/) ||
          html.match(/content="([^"]+)" property="og:title"/))?.[1]
        if (!title) return

        // Skip non-football articles
        if (!isFootball(title, url)) return

        const image = (html.match(/property="og:image" content="([^"]+)"/) ||
          html.match(/content="([^"]+)" property="og:image"/))?.[1]
        const description = (html.match(/property="og:description" content="([^"]+)"/) ||
          html.match(/content="([^"]+)" property="og:description"/))?.[1]
        const publishedAt = (html.match(/property="article:published_time" content="([^"]+)"/) ||
          html.match(/content="([^"]+)" property="article:published_time"/))?.[1]

        const externalId = slugToId(url)

        articles.push({
          externalId,
          source: SOURCE,
          title: title.replace(' - Ceuta Deportiva', '').trim(),
          description: description ?? null,
          url,
          imageUrl: image ?? null,
          publishedAt: publishedAt ?? null,
        })
      } catch {
        // skip
      }
    })
  )

  // Sort newest first
  return articles.sort((a, b) =>
    (b.publishedAt ?? '').localeCompare(a.publishedAt ?? '')
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/infrastructure/rss/RssFetcher.ts">
import { XMLParser } from 'fast-xml-parser'
import { RssSource, BASE_KEYWORDS } from './sources'
import { decodeEntities } from '@/lib/decodeEntities'

export interface ParsedArticle {
  externalId: string
  title: string
  description: string | null
  url: string
  imageUrl: string | null
  source: string
  publishedAt: string | null
}

const parser = new XMLParser({
  ignoreAttributes: false,
  attributeNamePrefix: '@_',
  cdataPropName: '__cdata',
  processEntities: true,
})


function extractMediaUrl(obj: unknown): string | null {
  if (!obj || typeof obj !== 'object') return null
  const url = (obj as Record<string, unknown>)['@_url']
  return typeof url === 'string' && url.length > 0 ? url : null
}

const EMOJI_HOSTS = ['s.w.org', 'twemoji', 'gravatar.com']
const IMG_EXT = /\.(?:jpg|jpeg|jfif|png|webp|gif|avif)(\?[^\s"<>]*)?$/i
const IMG_URL_RE = /https?:\/\/[^\s"<>]+\.(?:jpg|jpeg|jfif|png|webp|gif|avif)(?:\?[^\s"<>]*)?/gi
const DATA_IMG_URL_RE = /data-img-url=["']([^"']+)["']/gi

function isValidImage(url: string): boolean {
  return (
    !EMOJI_HOSTS.some((h) => url.includes(h)) &&
    !url.includes('youtube.com') &&
    !url.includes('youtu.be') &&
    IMG_EXT.test(url.split('?')[0])
  )
}

function firstValidImage(html: string): string | null {
  const dataUrlMatches = html.matchAll(DATA_IMG_URL_RE)
  for (const m of dataUrlMatches) {
    const url = m[1]
    if (isValidImage(url)) return url
  }

  const matches = html.matchAll(IMG_URL_RE)
  for (const m of matches) {
    const url = m[0].replace(/-\d+x\d+(\.\w+)$/, '$1')
    if (isValidImage(url)) return url
  }
  return null
}

function extractImage(item: Record<string, unknown>): string | null {
  // media:thumbnail / media:content (standard RSS media tags)
  // media:content may be an array — take the first with a valid url
  const mediaContent = item['media:content']
  const mediaItems = Array.isArray(mediaContent) ? mediaContent : mediaContent ? [mediaContent] : []
  for (const m of mediaItems) {
    const url = extractMediaUrl(m)
    if (url && isValidImage(url)) return url
  }
  const url = extractMediaUrl(item['media:thumbnail'])
  if (url && isValidImage(url)) return url
  // enclosure
  const enclosure = item['enclosure']
  if (enclosure && typeof enclosure === 'object') {
    const u = (enclosure as Record<string, unknown>)['@_url']
    if (typeof u === 'string' && isValidImage(u)) return u
  }
  // content:encoded — full HTML body (plain string or CDATA object)
  const encoded = item['content:encoded']
  const encodedStr =
    typeof encoded === 'string'
      ? encoded
      : encoded && typeof encoded === 'object'
        ? String((encoded as Record<string, unknown>)['__cdata'] ?? '')
        : ''
  if (encodedStr) {
    const found = firstValidImage(encodedStr)
    if (found) return found
  }
  // description — some feeds embed images here (plain string or CDATA object)
  const desc = item['description']
  const descStr =
    typeof desc === 'string'
      ? desc
      : desc && typeof desc === 'object'
        ? String((desc as Record<string, unknown>)['__cdata'] ?? '')
        : ''
  if (descStr) {
    const found = firstValidImage(descStr)
    if (found) return found
  }
  return null
}

// Meta tag patterns tried in order: og:image, twitter:image, schema.org image
const OG_PATTERNS = [
  /<meta[^>]+property=["']og:image["'][^>]+content=["']([^"']+)["']/i,
  /<meta[^>]+content=["']([^"']+)["'][^>]+property=["']og:image["']/i,
  /<meta[^>]+name=["']twitter:image["'][^>]+content=["']([^"']+)["']/i,
  /<meta[^>]+content=["']([^"']+)["'][^>]+name=["']twitter:image["']/i,
  /<meta[^>]+itemprop=["']image["'][^>]+content=["']([^"']+)["']/i,
  /<meta[^>]+content=["']([^"']+)["'][^>]+itemprop=["']image["']/i,
]

async function fetchOgImage(articleUrl: string): Promise<string | null> {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(articleUrl, {
      headers: { 'User-Agent': 'FunHubCeuta/1.0 RSS Reader' },
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(6000),
    })
    if (!res.ok) return null
    // Read only first 50KB — enough to find the <head> meta tags
    const reader = res.body?.getReader()
    if (!reader) return null
    const chunks: Uint8Array[] = []
    let total = 0
    while (total < 50_000) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read()
      if (done || !value) break
      chunks.push(value)
      total += value.length
    }
    reader.cancel()
    const html = new TextDecoder().decode(
      chunks.reduce((a, b) => {
        const c = new Uint8Array(a.length + b.length)
        c.set(a)
        c.set(b, a.length)
        return c
      }, new Uint8Array())
    )
    for (const re of OG_PATTERNS) {
      const m = html.match(re)
      if (m?.[1] && isValidImage(m[1])) return m[1]
    }
    return null
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

// Other Ceuta-based clubs that must never be matched
const EXCLUDE_TERMS = [
  'ceuta depor',
  'ceutadepor',
  'atletico ceuta',
  'atlético ceuta',
  'atletico de ceuta',
  'atlético de ceuta',
]

// Title patterns from press that are generic roundup/preview content, not real club news.
// These slip through keyword filters because they mention the club in passing.
export const EXCLUDE_TITLE_PATTERNS = [
  /\bjornada\s+\d+/i,          // "previa jornada 32", "resumen jornada 32"
  /previa\b.*\bjornada\b/i,    // "previa de la jornada"
  /jornada\b.*\bprevia\b/i,
  /\bclasificaci[oó]n\b.*\bjornada\b/i,
  /\bjornada\b.*\bclasificaci[oó]n\b/i,
]

function isRelevant(item: Record<string, unknown>, source: RssSource): boolean {
  // Feed already scoped to the club — accept everything
  if (source.keywords.length === 0) return true

  const text = [
    item.title,
    item.description,
    item['content:encoded'],
    item['media:keywords'],
    item['category'],
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .map((v) => String(v).replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ')) // strip HTML tags
    .join(' ')
    .toLowerCase()

  // Reject articles about other Ceuta clubs
  if (EXCLUDE_TERMS.some((t) => text.includes(t))) return false

  // Reject generic jornada preview/roundup articles from press
  const title = String(item.title ?? '').replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '')
  if (EXCLUDE_TITLE_PATTERNS.some((re) => re.test(title))) return false

  // At least one BASE_KEYWORD must be present to avoid matching unrelated city news.
  // National press sources can provide broader baseKeywords via source.baseKeywords.
  const base = source.baseKeywords ?? BASE_KEYWORDS
  const hasBase = base.some((kw) => text.includes(kw.toLowerCase()))
  if (!hasBase) return false

  return source.keywords.some((kw) => text.includes(kw.toLowerCase()))
}

function extractGuid(item: Record<string, unknown>): string {
  const guid = item.guid
  if (typeof guid === 'string') return guid
  // fast-xml-parser wraps text+attributes as { '#text': '...', '@_isPermaLink': '...' }
  if (guid && typeof guid === 'object') {
    const text = (guid as Record<string, unknown>)['#text']
    if (typeof text === 'string') return text
  }
  return String(item.link ?? '')
}

export async function fetchSource(source: RssSource): Promise<ParsedArticle[]> {
  const res = await fetch(source.url, {
    headers: { 'User-Agent': 'FunHubCeuta/1.0 RSS Reader' },
    next: { revalidate: 0 },
  })

  if (!res.ok) return []

  const xml = await res.text()
  const parsed = parser.parse(xml)
  const raw = parsed?.rss?.channel?.item ?? []
  const items: Record<string, unknown>[] = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : [raw]

  const mapped = items
    .filter((item) => isRelevant(item, source))
    .map((item) => ({
      externalId: extractGuid(item),
      title: decodeEntities(String(item.title ?? '').replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '')).trim(),
      description: item.description
        ? decodeEntities(
            String(item.description)
              .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '')
              .trim()
          ).slice(0, 300)
        : null,
      url: String(item.link ?? ''),
      imageUrl: extractImage(item),
      source: source.id,
      publishedAt: item.pubDate ? new Date(String(item.pubDate)).toISOString() : null,
    }))
    .filter((a) => a.externalId && a.url)

  // Fetch og:image in parallel for articles that have no image yet
  const withImages = await Promise.all(
    mapped.map(async (a) => {
      if (a.imageUrl) return a
      const ogImage = await fetchOgImage(a.url)
      return { ...a, imageUrl: ogImage }
    })
  )

  return withImages
}
</file>

<file path="src/lib/__tests__/decodeEntities.test.ts">
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'
import { decodeEntities } from '../decodeEntities'

describe('decodeEntities', () => {
  it('decodes &quot; to "', () => {
    expect(decodeEntities('El &quot;Ceuta&quot; gana')).toBe('El "Ceuta" gana')
  })

  it('decodes &amp; to &', () => {
    expect(decodeEntities('Ceuta &amp; Melilla')).toBe('Ceuta & Melilla')
  })

  it('decodes &apos; to \'', () => {
    expect(decodeEntities('L&apos;equip')).toBe("L'equip")
  })

  it('decodes &lt; and &gt;', () => {
    expect(decodeEntities('&lt;p&gt;texto&lt;/p&gt;')).toBe('<p>texto</p>')
  })

  it('decodes typographic quotes &#8220; &#8221;', () => {
    expect(decodeEntities('&#8220;Victoria&#8221;')).toBe('“Victoria”')
  })

  it('decodes typographic apostrophe &#8217;', () => {
    expect(decodeEntities('L&#8217;equip')).toBe('L’equip')
  })

  it('decodes em dash &#8212;', () => {
    expect(decodeEntities('Ceuta&#8212;Algeciras')).toBe('Ceuta—Algeciras')
  })

  it('decodes en dash &#8211;', () => {
    expect(decodeEntities('3&#8211;0')).toBe('3–0')
  })

  it('decodes arbitrary numeric entities like &#233; → é', () => {
    expect(decodeEntities('caf&#233;')).toBe('café')
  })

  it('removes unknown named entities', () => {
    expect(decodeEntities('texto&nbsp;texto')).toBe('textotexto')
  })

  it('leaves plain text untouched', () => {
    expect(decodeEntities('AD Ceuta FC gana 2-0')).toBe('AD Ceuta FC gana 2-0')
  })

  it('handles empty string', () => {
    expect(decodeEntities('')).toBe('')
  })

  it('handles multiple entities in one string', () => {
    expect(decodeEntities('&quot;Ceuta &amp; Melilla&quot;')).toBe('"Ceuta & Melilla"')
  })
})
</file>

<file path="src/lib/decodeEntities.ts">
export function decodeEntities(str: string): string {
  return str
    .replace(/&#8211;/g, '–')
    .replace(/&#8212;/g, '—')
    .replace(/&#8216;/g, '‘')
    .replace(/&#8217;/g, '’')
    .replace(/&#8220;/g, '“')
    .replace(/&#8221;/g, '”')
    .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
    .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
    .replace(/&apos;/g, "'")
    .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
    .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
    .replace(/&#(\d+);/g, (_, code) => String.fromCharCode(Number(code)))
    .replace(/&[a-z]+;/gi, '')
}

<file path="src/lib/serverFetches.ts">
import { supabase } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/client'
import { TeamsSupabaseRepository } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/TeamsSupabaseRepository'
import { PREVIOUS_SEASON } from '@/lib/constants'

export async function fetchCeutaForm(): Promise<string[]> {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('matches')
    .select('result')
    .eq('status', 'finished')
    .not('result', 'is', null)
    .order('date', { ascending: false })
    .limit(5)
  if (data?.length) return data.map((m) => m.result as string).reverse()

  const { data: config } = await supabase
    .from('app_config')
    .select('value')
    .eq('key', 'ceuta_form')
    .single()
  return config?.value?.split(',').filter(Boolean) ?? []
}

export async function fetchTeamLogos(): Promise<Record<string, string>> {
  const repo = new TeamsSupabaseRepository()
  const [current, previous] = await Promise.all([
    repo.getAllTeams(),
    repo.getAllTeams(PREVIOUS_SEASON),
  ])
  const map: Record<string, string> = {}
  // Previous season first so current season overrides on name collision
  for (const t of previous) if (t.logoUrl) map[t.name] = t.logoUrl
  for (const t of current) if (t.logoUrl) map[t.name] = t.logoUrl
  return map
}
</file>

<file path="src/app/admin/page.tsx">
import { supabase } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/client'
import { CURRENT_SEASON } from '@/lib/constants'
import { BarChart, LineChart } from '@/components/admin/Charts'
import { AdminActions } from '@/features/admin/AdminActions'

function parseDevice(ua: string | null): { os: string; browser: string; icon: string } {
  if (!ua) return { os: 'Desconocido', browser: '—', icon: '❓' }
  const u = ua.toLowerCase()
  const isIOS = /iphone|ipad/.test(u)
  const isAndroid = /android/.test(u)
  const isMac = /macintosh/.test(u) && !isIOS
  const isWindows = /windows/.test(u)
  const isSafari = /safari/.test(u) && !/chrome/.test(u)
  const isChrome = /chrome/.test(u) && !/edg/.test(u)
  const isFirefox = /firefox/.test(u)
  const isEdge = /edg/.test(u)

  const os = isIOS ? 'iOS' : isAndroid ? 'Android' : isMac ? 'Mac' : isWindows ? 'Windows' : 'Otro'
  const browser = isSafari
    ? 'Safari'
    : isChrome
      ? 'Chrome'
      : isFirefox
        ? 'Firefox'
        : isEdge
          ? 'Edge'
          : '—'
  const icon = isIOS ? '🍎' : isAndroid ? '🤖' : isMac ? '💻' : isWindows ? '🖥️' : '❓'

  return { os, browser, icon }
}

async function getStats() {
  const [
    { count: subscribers },
    { count: totalNews },
    { count: players },
    { data: newsBySource },
    { data: cronLogs },
    { data: notifLogs },
    { data: subsGrowth },
    { data: subsList },
  ] = await Promise.all([
    supabase.from('push_subscriptions').select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true }),
    supabase.from('news').select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true }),
    supabase
      .from('player_stats')
      .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true })
      .eq('season', CURRENT_SEASON),
    supabase
      .from('news')
      .select('source')
      .then(({ data }) => ({
        data: data
          ? Object.entries(
              data.reduce((acc: Record<string, number>, r) => {
                acc[r.source] = (acc[r.source] ?? 0) + 1
                return acc
              }, {})
            ).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
          : [],
      })),
    supabase.from('cron_logs').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(50),
    supabase
      .from('notification_logs')
      .select('*')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(30),
    supabase
      .from('cron_logs')
      .select('created_at, details')
      .eq('cron_type', 'news')
      .eq('status', 'success')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
      .limit(60),
    supabase
      .from('push_subscriptions')
      .select('endpoint, user_agent, created_at')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
  ])

  return {
    subscribers: subscribers ?? 0,
    totalNews: totalNews ?? 0,
    players: players ?? 0,
    newsBySource: (newsBySource as [string, number][]) ?? [],
    cronLogs: cronLogs ?? [],
    notifLogs: notifLogs ?? [],
    subsList: (subsList ?? []).map(
      (s: { endpoint: string; user_agent: string | null; created_at: string | null }) => ({
        endpoint: s.endpoint,
        device: parseDevice(s.user_agent),
        createdAt: s.created_at,
      })
    ),
    subsGrowth: (subsGrowth ?? []).map(
      (r: { created_at: string; details: { subscribers?: number } }) => ({
        date: r.created_at,
        subs: r.details?.subscribers ?? 0,
      })
    ),
  }
}

const SOURCE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  oficial: 'AD Ceuta FC',
  faro: 'El Faro',
  elpueblo: 'El Pueblo',
  marca: 'Marca',
  mundodeportivo: 'Mundo Dep.',
  '20minutos': '20 Min.',
  elmundo: 'El Mundo',
}

const CRON_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  news: '📰 Noticias',
  'player-stats': '📊 Stats',
  matches: '⚽ Partidos',
  live: '🔴 Live',
}

function fmt(iso: string | null) {
  if (!iso) return '—'
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleString('es-ES', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: '2-digit',
    year: '2-digit',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
    timeZone: 'Europe/Madrid',
  })
}

function fmtDuration(ms: number | null) {
  if (!ms) return '—'
  return ms < 1000 ? `${ms}ms` : `${(ms / 1000).toFixed(1)}s`
}

const C = {
  red: 'var(--red)',
  black: 'var(--black)',
  white: 'var(--white)',
  bgSoft: 'var(--bg-soft)',
  bgMid: 'var(--bg-mid)',
  muted: 'var(--muted)',
  border: 'var(--border)',
  radius: 'var(--radius)',
}

const s = {
  page: {
    fontFamily: 'system-ui, sans-serif',
    maxWidth: 520,
    margin: '0 auto',
    padding: '24px 16px',
    background: C.white,
    minHeight: '100vh',
  } as React.CSSProperties,
  eyebrow: {
    fontSize: 11,
    fontWeight: 700,
    textTransform: 'uppercase' as const,
    letterSpacing: '0.1em',
    color: C.red,
    marginBottom: 4,
  },
  title: {
    fontSize: 26,
    fontWeight: 900,
    letterSpacing: '-0.02em',
    textTransform: 'uppercase' as const,
    color: C.black,
    marginBottom: 24,
  },
  grid2: {
    display: 'grid',
    gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr',
    gap: 8,
    marginBottom: 16,
  } as React.CSSProperties,
  tileRed: { background: C.red, borderRadius: 14, padding: 16 },
  tileBlack: { background: C.black, borderRadius: 14, padding: 16 },
  tileGray: { background: C.bgSoft, borderRadius: 14, padding: 16 },
  bigNum: (color = C.white) => ({ fontSize: 48, fontWeight: 900, color, lineHeight: 1 }),
  label: (color = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.75)') => ({
    fontSize: 9,
    fontWeight: 700,
    textTransform: 'uppercase' as const,
    letterSpacing: '0.1em',
    color,
    marginTop: 8,
  }),
  sectionTitle: {
    fontSize: 9,
    fontWeight: 700,
    textTransform: 'uppercase' as const,
    letterSpacing: '0.1em',
    color: C.muted,
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  card: { background: C.bgSoft, borderRadius: 14, padding: 16, marginBottom: 12 },
  table: { width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse' as const, fontSize: 12 },
  th: {
    textAlign: 'left' as const,
    fontSize: 9,
    fontWeight: 700,
    textTransform: 'uppercase' as const,
    letterSpacing: '0.08em',
    color: C.muted,
    paddingBottom: 8,
  },
  td: {
    padding: '7px 0',
    borderTop: `1px solid ${C.border}`,
    color: C.black,
    verticalAlign: 'top' as const,
  },
  badge: (ok: boolean) => ({
    display: 'inline-block',
    padding: '2px 7px',
    borderRadius: 99,
    fontSize: 10,
    fontWeight: 700,
    background: ok ? '#dcfce7' : '#fee2e2',
    color: ok ? '#166534' : '#991b1b',
  }),
}

export default async function AdminPage() {
  const stats = await getStats()

  // Notifications total
  const totalSent = stats.notifLogs.reduce(
    (a: number, n: { sent_ok: number }) => a + (n.sent_ok ?? 0),
    0
  )
  const totalRecip = stats.notifLogs.reduce(
    (a: number, n: { recipients: number }) => a + (n.recipients ?? 0),
    0
  )
  const deliveryRate = totalRecip > 0 ? Math.round((totalSent / totalRecip) * 100) : 0

  // Last successful news cron
  const lastNewsRun = stats.cronLogs.find(
    (l: { cron_type: string; status: string }) => l.cron_type === 'news' && l.status === 'success'
  )

  // News by source for bar chart
  const sourceChartData = stats.newsBySource.slice(0, 7).map(([src, count]) => ({
    label: SOURCE_LABELS[src]?.split(' ')[0] ?? src,
    value: count,
  }))

  // Subscribers evolution from cron logs
  const subsData = stats.subsGrowth
    .map((r: { subs: number }) => r.subs)
    .filter((v: number) => v > 0)

  // Device breakdown
  const deviceCount: Record<string, number> = {}
  for (const s of stats.subsList) {
    const key = `${s.device.icon} ${s.device.os}`
    deviceCount[key] = (deviceCount[key] ?? 0) + 1
  }

  // Cron errors
  const cronErrors = stats.cronLogs.filter((l: { status: string }) => l.status === 'error')

  // Cron success rate
  const cronSuccess = stats.cronLogs.filter(
    (l: { status: string }) => l.status === 'success'
  ).length
  const cronRate =
    stats.cronLogs.length > 0 ? Math.round((cronSuccess / stats.cronLogs.length) * 100) : 100

  return (
    <div style={s.page}>
      <div style={s.eyebrow}>Panel de administración</div>
      <div style={s.title}>AD Ceuta FC</div>

      <AdminActions />

      {/* KPIs principales */}
      <div style={s.grid2}>
        <div style={s.tileRed}>
          <div style={s.bigNum()}>{stats.subscribers}</div>
          <div style={s.label()}>Usuarios activos</div>
        </div>
        <div style={s.tileBlack}>
          <div style={s.bigNum()}>{stats.notifLogs.length}</div>
          <div style={s.label()}>Notificaciones enviadas</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style={s.grid2}>
        <div style={s.tileGray}>
          <div style={s.bigNum(C.black)}>{stats.totalNews}</div>
          <div style={s.label(C.muted)}>Noticias en BD</div>
        </div>
        <div style={s.tileGray}>
          <div style={s.bigNum(C.black)}>{deliveryRate}%</div>
          <div style={s.label(C.muted)}>Tasa de entrega</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style={s.grid2}>
        <div style={s.tileGray}>
          <div style={s.bigNum(C.black)}>{cronRate}%</div>
          <div style={s.label(C.muted)}>Éxito de crons</div>
        </div>
        <div style={s.tileGray}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700, color: C.black, lineHeight: 1.3 }}>
            {fmt(lastNewsRun?.created_at ?? null)}
          </div>
          <div style={s.label(C.muted)}>Último scrape noticias</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Gráfico suscriptores */}
      <div style={s.card}>
        <div style={s.sectionTitle}>Evolución de suscriptores</div>
        <LineChart data={subsData} color={C.red} />
        {subsData.length === 0 && (
          <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: C.muted, margin: 0 }}>
            Los datos se acumularán con cada ejecución del scraper de noticias.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Errores de crons */}
      {cronErrors.length > 0 && (
        <div style={{ ...s.card, background: '#fee2e2', marginBottom: 12 }}>
          <div style={{ ...s.sectionTitle, color: '#991b1b' }}>⚠️ Errores recientes en crons</div>
          <table style={s.table}>
            <tbody>
              {cronErrors
                .slice(0, 5)
                .map(
                  (l: {
                    id: number
                    cron_type: string
                    details: { error?: string }
                    created_at: string
                  }) => (
                    <tr key={l.id}>
                      <td style={s.td}>
                        <strong>{CRON_LABELS[l.cron_type] ?? l.cron_type}</strong>
                      </td>
                      <td style={{ ...s.td, color: '#991b1b', fontSize: 11 }}>
                        {l.details?.error ?? 'Error desconocido'}
                      </td>
                      <td style={{ ...s.td, whiteSpace: 'nowrap', color: C.muted, fontSize: 11 }}>
                        {fmt(l.created_at)}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )
                )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Suscriptores */}
      <div style={s.card}>
        <div style={s.sectionTitle}>Suscriptores ({stats.subscribers})</div>
        {/* Device breakdown */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap', marginBottom: 12 }}>
          {Object.entries(deviceCount).map(([key, count]) => (
            <div
              key={key}
              style={{
                background: C.bgMid,
                borderRadius: 99,
                padding: '4px 10px',
                fontSize: 11,
                fontWeight: 700,
              }}
            >
              {key} · {count}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        {stats.subsList.length === 0 ? (
          <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: C.muted, margin: 0 }}>
            Los nuevos suscriptores mostrarán dispositivo y fecha.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <table style={s.table}>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th style={s.th}>Dispositivo</th>
                <th style={s.th}>Navegador</th>
                <th style={{ ...s.th, textAlign: 'right' }}>Suscrito el</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {stats.subsList.map(
                (
                  sub: {

                    createdAt: string | null

                    <td style={s.td}>
                      {sub.device.icon} {sub.device.os}
                    </td>
                    <td style={s.td}>{sub.device.browser}</td>

                        ...s.td,
                        textAlign: 'right',
                        color: C.muted,

                        whiteSpace: 'nowrap',

                      {fmt(sub.createdAt)}
                    </td>

        )}

      {/* Gráfico noticias por fuente */}
      <div style={s.card}>
        <div style={s.sectionTitle}>Noticias por fuente</div>
        <BarChart data={sourceChartData} color={C.red} />
        <table style={{ ...s.table, marginTop: 12 }}>
          <tbody>
            {stats.newsBySource.map(([src, count]) => (
              <tr key={src}>
                <td style={s.td}>{SOURCE_LABELS[src] ?? src}</td>
                <td style={{ ...s.td, textAlign: 'right', fontWeight: 700 }}>{count}</td>
                <td style={{ ...s.td, textAlign: 'right', color: C.muted, paddingLeft: 8 }}>
                  {Math.round((count / stats.totalNews) * 100)}%
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      {/* Historial de notificaciones */}
      <div style={s.card}>
        <div style={s.sectionTitle}>Historial de notificaciones</div>
        {stats.notifLogs.length === 0 ? (
          <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: C.muted, margin: 0 }}>
            Sin notificaciones registradas aún. Se registrarán en el próximo scrape.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <table style={s.table}>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th style={s.th}>Artículo</th>
                <th style={{ ...s.th, textAlign: 'right' }}>Env.</th>
                <th style={{ ...s.th, textAlign: 'right' }}>Fecha</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {stats.notifLogs.map(

                  id: number

                  sent_ok: number
                  recipients: number

                    <td style={s.td}>

                        {SOURCE_LABELS[n.article_source] ?? n.article_source}

                      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, lineHeight: 1.3 }}>{n.article_title}</div>

                    <td

                      {n.sent_ok}

                    <td
                      style={{
                        ...s.td,
                        textAlign: 'right',

                        color: C.muted,
                        fontSize: 11,
                      }}

                      {fmt(n.created_at)}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                )
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Historial de crons */}
      <div style={s.card}>
        <div style={s.sectionTitle}>Historial de crons (últimas 50 ejecuciones)</div>
        {stats.cronLogs.length === 0 ? (

              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {stats.cronLogs.map(
                (l: {
                  id: number
                  cron_type: string
                  status: string
                  duration_ms: number
                  details: { newArticles?: number; updated?: number; error?: string }
                  created_at: string
                }) => (
                  <tr key={l.id}>
                    <td style={s.td}>
                      <div style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>
                        {CRON_LABELS[l.cron_type] ?? l.cron_type}
                      </div>
                      {l.details?.newArticles != null && (
                        <div style={{ fontSize: 10, color: C.muted }}>
                          {l.details.newArticles} nuevas
                        </div>
                      )}
                      {l.details?.updated != null && (
                        <div style={{ fontSize: 10, color: C.muted }}>
                          {l.details.updated} jugadores
                        </div>
                      )}
                      {l.details?.error && (
                        <div style={{ fontSize: 10, color: '#991b1b' }}>{l.details.error}</div>
                      )}
                    </td>
                    <td style={s.td}>
                      <span style={s.badge(l.status === 'success')}>
                        {l.status === 'success' ? '✓ OK' : '✗ Error'}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ ...s.td, textAlign: 'right', color: C.muted }}>
                      {fmtDuration(l.duration_ms)}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ ...s.td, textAlign: 'right', color: C.muted, fontSize: 11 }}>
                      {fmt(l.created_at)}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                )
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

<file path="src/app/clasificacion/page.tsx">
import { ClasificacionPage } from '@/features/standings/ClasificacionPage'
import { StandingsService } from '@/domain/standings/StandingsService'
import { StandingsSupabaseRepository } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/StandingsSupabaseRepository'
import { STANDINGS_CURRENT_SEASON, STANDINGS_PREVIOUS_SEASON } from '@/lib/constants'
import { fetchCeutaForm, fetchTeamLogos } from '@/lib/serverFetches'

export const revalidate = 300

export default async function ClasificacionServerPage() {
  const service = new StandingsService(new StandingsSupabaseRepository())
  const [standings, previousStandings, form, logos] = await Promise.all([
    service.getStandings(STANDINGS_CURRENT_SEASON),
    service.getStandings(STANDINGS_PREVIOUS_SEASON),
    fetchCeutaForm(),
    fetchTeamLogos(),
  ])

  return (
    <ClasificacionPage
      entries={standings?.entries ?? []}
      previousEntries={previousStandings?.entries ?? []}
      form={form}
      logos={logos}
    />
  )
}

<file path="src/app/partidos/page.tsx">
import { PartidosPage } from '@/features/matches/PartidosPage'
import { supabase } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/client'
import { deduplicateMatches } from '@/app/api/matches/deduplicateMatches'
import { fetchTeamLogos } from '@/lib/serverFetches'
import { CURRENT_SEASON, PREVIOUS_SEASON } from '@/lib/constants'

export const revalidate = 300

async function fetchMatches() {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('matches')
    .select('*')
    .eq('season', CURRENT_SEASON)
    .order('date', { ascending: false })
    .limit(60)

  const deduped = deduplicateMatches(data ?? [])
  const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]

  const upcoming = deduped
    .filter((r) => r.status === 'scheduled' && String(r.date).slice(0, 10) >= today)
    .sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date))

  const results = deduped
    .filter(
      (r) =>
        r.status === 'finished' || (r.status === 'scheduled' && String(r.date).slice(0, 10) < today)
    )
    .sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date))

  return { upcoming, results }
}

async function fetchPreviousSeason() {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('matches')
    .select('*')
    .eq('season', PREVIOUS_SEASON)
    .order('date', { ascending: false })

  const deduped = deduplicateMatches(data ?? [])
  return deduped.sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date))
}

export default async function PartidosServerPage() {
  const [{ upcoming, results }, previousSeason, logos] = await Promise.all([
    fetchMatches(),
    fetchPreviousSeason(),
    fetchTeamLogos(),
  ])

  return (
    <PartidosPage
      upcoming={upcoming}
      results={results}
      previousSeason={previousSeason}
      logos={logos}
    />
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/features/club/tabs/HistoriaTab.tsx">
'use client'

import {
  CalendarDays,
  Flag,
  Heart,
  Landmark,
  MapPin,
  Shield,
  Trophy,
  Users,
  type LucideIcon,
} from 'lucide-react'

const HISTORY = [
  {

function Timeline() {
  return (
    <div className="hist-timeline">
      <div className="hist-tl-line" />
      {HISTORY.map((e, i) => {
        const Icon = e.icon as LucideIcon

        return (
          <div key={i} className="hist-tl-item" style={{ paddingBottom: i === HISTORY.length - 1 ? 0 : undefined }}>
            <div className="hist-tl-dot" style={{
              background: e.tone === 'key' ? 'var(--red)' : e.tone === 'gold' ? 'var(--gold)' : 'var(--white)',
              borderColor: e.tone === 'key' ? 'var(--red)' : e.tone === 'gold' ? 'var(--gold)' : 'var(--muted)',
            }} />
            <div className="hist-tl-kicker">
              <Icon size={14} strokeWidth={2.4} />
              <div className="hist-tl-year" style={{ color: e.tone === 'key' ? 'var(--red)' : e.tone === 'gold' ? 'var(--gold)' : 'var(--black)' }}>{e.year}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="hist-tl-title">{e.title}</div>
            <div className="hist-tl-desc">{e.desc}</div>
          </div>
        )
      })}
    </div>
  )
}

<file path="src/infrastructure/scrapers/adceutaScraper.ts">
import { ESPN_BASE as ESPN, ESPN_CEUTA_ID as CEUTA_ID } from '@/lib/constants'

export interface ScrapedMatch {
  homeTeam: string
  awayTeam: string
  date: string // YYYY-MM-DD
  time: string // HH:MM
  weekday: string
  isHome: boolean
  ticketsUrl: string | null
  espnEventId: string | null
}

export interface ScrapedResult {
  homeTeam: string
  awayTeam: string
  homeScore: number
  awayScore: number
  date: string // YYYY-MM-DD
  weekday: string
  result: 'W' | 'D' | 'L'
  isHome: boolean
  espnEventId: string | null
}

const WEEKDAYS_ES = ['Domingo', 'Lunes', 'Martes', 'Miércoles', 'Jueves', 'Viernes', 'Sábado']

function parseScore(raw: unknown): number {
  if (typeof raw === 'number') return raw
  if (typeof raw === 'string') return parseInt(raw) || 0
  if (raw && typeof raw === 'object') {
    const v =
      (raw as Record<string, unknown>).displayValue ?? (raw as Record<string, unknown>).value
    return typeof v === 'string' ? parseInt(v) || 0 : Number(v) || 0
  }
  return 0
}

async function fetchSchedule(): Promise<Record<string, unknown>[]> {
  // Try team endpoint first (faster, scoped)
  const teamRes = await fetch(`${ESPN}/teams/${CEUTA_ID}/schedule`, { next: { revalidate: 0 } })
  if (teamRes.ok) {
    const data = await teamRes.json()
    const events = (data.events ?? []) as Record<string, unknown>[]
    if (events.length > 0) return events
  }

  // Fallback: full scoreboard filtered to Ceuta (covers seasons where ESPN team endpoint lags)
  const scoreRes = await fetch(
    `https://site.api.espn.com/apis/site/v2/sports/soccer/esp.2/scoreboard?dates=20260801-20270630&limit=500`,
    { next: { revalidate: 0 } }
  )
  if (!scoreRes.ok) return []
  const scoreData = await scoreRes.json()
  const all = (scoreData.events ?? []) as Record<string, unknown>[]
  return all.filter((e) => {
    const comps = (e.competitions as Record<string, unknown>[])?.[0]
    const competitors = (comps?.competitors ?? []) as Record<string, unknown>[]
    return competitors.some((c) => (c.team as Record<string, unknown>)?.id === CEUTA_ID)
  })
}

function mapEventToMatch(e: Record<string, unknown>): ScrapedMatch {
  const comp = (e.competitions as Record<string, unknown>[])[0]
  const competitors = comp.competitors as Record<string, unknown>[]
  const home = competitors.find((c) => c.homeAway === 'home')!
  const away = competitors.find((c) => c.homeAway === 'away')!
  const dateStr = (e.date as string).slice(0, 10)
  const dateObj = new Date(e.date as string)
  return {
    homeTeam: (home.team as Record<string, unknown>).displayName as string,
    awayTeam: (away.team as Record<string, unknown>).displayName as string,
    date: dateStr,
    time: dateObj.toLocaleTimeString('es-ES', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', timeZone: 'Europe/Madrid' }),
    weekday: WEEKDAYS_ES[dateObj.getDay()],
    isHome: ((home.team as Record<string, unknown>).id as string) === CEUTA_ID,
    ticketsUrl: null,
    espnEventId: String(e.id ?? ''),
  }
}

export async function scrapeAllScheduled(): Promise<ScrapedMatch[]> {
  const events = await fetchSchedule()
  return events
    .filter((e) => {
      const comp = (e.competitions as Record<string, unknown>[])?.[0]
      const state = (comp?.status as Record<string, unknown>)?.type as Record<string, unknown>
      return (state?.state as string) === 'pre'
    })
    .map(mapEventToMatch)
}

export async function scrapeNextMatches(): Promise<ScrapedMatch[]> {
  const events = await fetchSchedule()
  const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]

  return events
    .filter((e) => {
      const comp = (e.competitions as Record<string, unknown>[])?.[0]
      const state = (comp?.status as Record<string, unknown>)?.type as Record<string, unknown>
      return (state?.state as string) === 'pre' && (e.date as string)?.slice(0, 10) >= today
    })
    .slice(0, 10)
    .map(mapEventToMatch)
}

export async function scrapeNextMatch(): Promise<ScrapedMatch | null> {
  const all = await scrapeNextMatches()
  return all[0] ?? null
}

export async function scrapeRecentResults(limit = 10): Promise<ScrapedResult[]> {
  const events = await fetchSchedule()

  return events
    .filter((e) => {
      const comp = (e.competitions as Record<string, unknown>[])?.[0]
      const state = (comp?.status as Record<string, unknown>)?.type as Record<string, unknown>
      return (state?.state as string) === 'post'
    })
    .slice(-limit)
    .reverse()
    .map((e) => {
      const comp = (e.competitions as Record<string, unknown>[])[0]
      const competitors = comp.competitors as Record<string, unknown>[]
      const home = competitors.find((c) => c.homeAway === 'home')!
      const away = competitors.find((c) => c.homeAway === 'away')!
      const homeScore = parseScore(home.score)
      const awayScore = parseScore(away.score)
      const isHome = ((home.team as Record<string, unknown>).id as string) === CEUTA_ID
      const ceutaGoals = isHome ? homeScore : awayScore
      const oppGoals = isHome ? awayScore : homeScore
      const result: 'W' | 'D' | 'L' =
        ceutaGoals > oppGoals ? 'W' : ceutaGoals === oppGoals ? 'D' : 'L'
      const dateStr = (e.date as string).slice(0, 10)
      const dateObj = new Date(e.date as string)
      return {
        homeTeam: (home.team as Record<string, unknown>).displayName as string,
        awayTeam: (away.team as Record<string, unknown>).displayName as string,
        homeScore,
        awayScore,
        date: dateStr,
        weekday: WEEKDAYS_ES[dateObj.getDay()],
        result,
        isHome,
        espnEventId: String(e.id ?? ''),
      }
    })
}

export async function scrapeRecentForm(): Promise<string[]> {
  const results = await scrapeRecentResults(5)
  return results.map((r) => r.result)
}
</file>

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { supabase } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/client'
import { CURRENT_SEASON } from '@/lib/constants'

const TEAMS: Array<{ id: string; name: string }> = [
  { id: '2737', name: 'Albacete' },
  { id: '6832', name: 'Almería' },
  { id: '12597', name: 'Burgos' },
  { id: '11487', name: 'CD Sabadell' },
  { id: '4438', name: 'Castellón' },
  { id: '5404', name: 'Ceuta' },
  { id: '3842', name: 'Cádiz' },
  { id: '8447', name: 'Córdoba' },
  { id: '3752', name: 'Eibar' },
  { id: '7320', name: 'Eldense' },
  { id: '20179', name: 'FC Andorra' },
  { id: '9812', name: 'Girona' },
  { id: '3747', name: 'Granada' },
  { id: '98', name: 'Las Palmas' },
  { id: '17534', name: 'Leganés' },
  { id: '84', name: 'Mallorca' },
  { id: '85', name: 'RC Celta Fortuna' }, // filial usa escudo del Celta de Vigo (Primera División ID 85)
  { id: '92', name: 'Real Oviedo' },
  { id: '20983', name: 'Real Sociedad II' },
  { id: '95', name: 'Real Valladolid' },
  { id: '3788', name: 'Sporting Gijón' },
  { id: '245', name: 'Tenerife' },
]

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
  if (searchParams.get('secret') !== process.env.TEST_SECRET) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })
  }

  const rows = TEAMS.map(({ id, name }) => ({
    name,
    logo_url: `https://a.espncdn.com/i/teamlogos/soccer/500/${id}.png`,
    season: CURRENT_SEASON,
  }))

  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('teams')
    .upsert(rows, { onConflict: 'name,season', ignoreDuplicates: false })

  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: error.message }, { status: 500 })

  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, total: rows.length })
}

<file path="src/app/club/page.tsx">
import { Suspense } from 'react'
import { ClubPage } from '@/features/club/ClubPage'
import { StandingsService } from '@/domain/standings/StandingsService'
import { StandingsSupabaseRepository } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/StandingsSupabaseRepository'
import { supabase } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/client'
import { CURRENT_SEASON, PREVIOUS_SEASON, STANDINGS_CURRENT_SEASON, STANDINGS_PREVIOUS_SEASON } from '@/lib/constants'
import type { LeadersMap } from '@/features/club/clubTypes'
import { fetchCeutaForm } from '@/lib/serverFetches'

export const revalidate = 300

const CATEGORIES = [
  { key: 'appearances', label: 'Partidos jugados', icon: '📋', min: 1 },
  { key: 'goals', label: 'Goles', icon: '⚽', min: 1 },
  { key: 'assists', label: 'Asistencias', icon: '🎯', min: 1 },
  { key: 'shots', label: 'Tiros totales', icon: '🔫', min: 1 },
  { key: 'shots_on_target', label: 'Tiros a puerta', icon: '🎯', min: 1 },
  { key: 'saves', label: 'Paradas', icon: '🧤', min: 1 },
  { key: 'yellow_cards', label: 'Tarjetas amarillas', icon: '🟨', min: 1 },
  { key: 'red_cards', label: 'Tarjetas rojas', icon: '🟥', min: 1 },
]

const NAME_OVERRIDES: Record<string, string> = {
  guillermo: 'vallejo',
  peri: 'pery',
}

function norm(name: string) {
  return name.toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, '').trim()
}
function sigWords(name: string): string[] {
  return norm(name)
    .split(/[\s.]+/)
    .filter((w) => w.length > 3 && /^[a-z]+$/.test(w))
}

const GROUP_ORDER = ['Porteros', 'Defensas', 'Centrocampistas', 'Delanteros']

async function fetchSquadBySeason(season: string) {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('squad')
    .select('*')
    .eq('season', season)
    .order('dorsal', { ascending: true })

  const byGroup: Record<string, typeof data> = {}
  for (const p of data ?? []) {
    const g = p.position_group ?? 'Otros'
    if (!byGroup[g]) byGroup[g] = []
    byGroup[g]!.push(p)
  }
  const grouped = GROUP_ORDER.filter((g) => byGroup[g]?.length).map((g) => ({
    group: g,
    players: byGroup[g]!,
  }))
  return { grouped, total: data?.length ?? 0 }
}

async function fetchCeuta(season: string) {
  const service = new StandingsService(new StandingsSupabaseRepository())
  const standings = await service.getStandings(season)
  return standings?.entries?.find((e) => e.teamName.toLowerCase().includes('ceuta')) ?? null
}

async function fetchLeadersBySeason(season: string): Promise<LeadersMap | null> {
  const [{ data }, { data: squadData }] = await Promise.all([
    supabase
      .from('player_stats')
      .select('name, photo_url, appearances, goals, assists, shots, shots_on_target, saves, yellow_cards, red_cards')
      .eq('season', season),
    supabase.from('squad').select('name, photo_url').eq('season', season),
  ])
  if (!data) return null

  const squadPhotos = (squadData ?? [])
    .filter((p) => p.photo_url)
    .map((p) => ({ words: sigWords(p.name), url: p.photo_url }))

  function getPhoto(espnName: string, espnPhoto: string | null): string {
    if (espnPhoto) return espnPhoto
    const override = NAME_OVERRIDES[norm(espnName)]
    if (override) {
      const found = squadData?.find((p) => norm(p.name).includes(override))
      if (found?.photo_url) return found.photo_url
    }
    const espnWords = sigWords(espnName)
    for (const sq of squadPhotos) {
      if (espnWords.some((w) => sq.words.includes(w))) return sq.url
    }
    return ''
  }

  const leaders: LeadersMap = {}
  for (const cat of CATEGORIES) {
    const sorted = data
      .filter((p) => (p[cat.key as keyof typeof p] as number) >= cat.min)
      .sort((a, b) => (b[cat.key as keyof typeof b] as number) - (a[cat.key as keyof typeof a] as number))
      .slice(0, 5)
    if (!sorted.length) continue
    leaders[cat.key] = {
      label: cat.label,
      icon: cat.icon,
      leaders: sorted.map((p) => ({
        name: p.name,
        value: p[cat.key as keyof typeof p] as number,
        photo: getPhoto(p.name, p.photo_url),
      })),
    }
  }
  return leaders
}

export default async function ClubServerPage() {
  const [
    { grouped, total },
    { grouped: previousGrouped, total: previousTotal },
    ceuta,
    ceutaPrevious,
    form,
    leaders,
    previousLeaders,
  ] = await Promise.all([
    fetchSquadBySeason(CURRENT_SEASON),
    fetchSquadBySeason(PREVIOUS_SEASON),
    fetchCeuta(STANDINGS_CURRENT_SEASON),
    fetchCeuta(STANDINGS_PREVIOUS_SEASON),
    fetchCeutaForm(),
    fetchLeadersBySeason(CURRENT_SEASON),
    fetchLeadersBySeason(PREVIOUS_SEASON),
  ])

  return (
    <Suspense>
      <ClubPage
        grouped={grouped}
        total={total}
        previousGrouped={previousGrouped}
        previousTotal={previousTotal}
        ceuta={ceuta}
        ceutaPrevious={ceutaPrevious}
        form={form}
        leaders={leaders}
        previousLeaders={previousLeaders}
      />
    </Suspense>
  )
}
</file>

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'

const SSR_NAV: NavId[] = ['mercado', 'clasificacion', 'partidos', 'club']

function AppInner() {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [modal, setModal] = useState<Modal>(null)
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false)
  const [loaderVisible, setLoaderVisible] = useState(true)

  // Safari bfcache: when restored from cache, force a full reload so React re-initialises
  useEffect(() => {
    const handlePageShow = (e: PageTransitionEvent) => {
      if (e.persisted) window.location.reload()
    }
    window.addEventListener('pageshow', handlePageShow)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('pageshow', handlePageShow)
  }, [])

  function handleNav(id: NavId) {
    if (SSR_NAV.includes(id)) router.push(`/${id}`)
  }

  return (
    <div id="app-root" className="home-shell">
      {loaderVisible && <LoadingScreen fadeOut={ready} onFadeOutDone={() => setLoaderVisible(false)} />}

      {/* Modal views — slide over the main content */}
      {modal === 'himno' && (
        <>
          <div className="topbar home-topbar-mobile" style={{ justifyContent: 'center' }}>
            {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
            <img
              src="/escudo.png"
              alt="AD Ceuta"
              style={{ width: 40, height: 40, objectFit: 'contain' }}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="scroll-area">
            <HimnoPage onBack={() => setModal(null)} />
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {/* Main app */}
      <div style={{ display: modal ? 'none' : 'contents' }}>
        <div className="topbar home-topbar-mobile" style={{ justifyContent: 'center' }}>
          {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
          <img
            src="/escudo.png"
            alt="AD Ceuta"
            style={{ width: 40, height: 40, objectFit: 'contain', cursor: 'pointer' }}
            onClick={() => router.push('/')}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="scroll-area">
          <HomePage onReady={() => setReady(true)} onOpenHimno={() => setModal('himno')} />
        </div>

        <div className="bottom-nav">
          {navItems.map((n) => (
            <button
              key={n.id}
              className={`nav-item ${n.id === 'inicio' ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => handleNav(n.id)}
            >
              {n.icon}
              <span className="nav-label">{n.label}</span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <Suspense>
      <AppInner />
    </Suspense>
  )
}

const SSR_ROUTES = ['/clasificacion', '/partidos', '/club', '/noticias', '/alineacion', '/mercado']

// Topbar rendered on SSR routes AND on home in desktop-only mode
function Topbar({ pathname, router }: { pathname: string; router: ReturnType<typeof useRouter> }) {
  return (
    <div className="topbar">
      <div className="topbar-logo" style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }} onClick={() => router.push('/')}>
        {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
        <img src="/escudo.png" alt="AD Ceuta" style={{ width: 40, height: 40, objectFit: 'contain' }} />
        <span className="topbar-title desktop-only">AD CEUTA <b>INFO</b></span>
      </div>
      <nav className="top-nav desktop-only">
        {NAV_DESKTOP.map((n) => (
          <button
            key={n.id}
            className={`top-nav-item ${pathname === n.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
            onClick={() => router.push(n.id)}
          >
            {n.icon}
            {n.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </nav>
    </div>
  )
}

export function AppShell({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const router = useRouter()

  if (pathname.startsWith('/admin')) return <>{children}</>

  const isSSRRoute = SSR_ROUTES.some((r) => pathname === r || pathname.startsWith(r + '/'))

  // Home page has its own mobile shell; on desktop we inject a topbar above it via CSS
  if (!isSSRRoute) {
    return (
      <>
        <InstallModal />
        {/* Desktop-only topbar overlay for home and other non-SSR pages */}
        <div className="desktop-topbar-overlay">
          <Topbar pathname={pathname} router={router} />
        </div>
        {children}
      </>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div id="app-root">
      <InstallModal />
      <Topbar pathname={pathname} router={router} />

      <div className="scroll-area">{children}</div>

      {/* Bottom nav: mobile only */}
      <div className="bottom-nav">
        {NAV_MOBILE.map((n) => (
          <button
            key={n.id}
            className={`nav-item ${pathname === n.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
            onClick={() => router.push(n.id)}
          >
            {n.icon}
            <span className="nav-label">{n.label}</span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

<file path="src/components/ui/LoadingScreen.tsx">
'use client'

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'

const WORDS = ['ORGULLO', 'CABALLA']

export function LoadingScreen({ fadeOut, onFadeOutDone }: { fadeOut?: boolean; onFadeOutDone?: () => void }) {
  const boxRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null)
  const word0 = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null)
  const word1 = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null)
  const words = [word0, word1]

  useEffect(() => {
    const box = boxRef.current
    if (!box) return

    let idx = 0

    function moveTo(i: number) {
      const el = words[i].current
      if (!el || !box) return
      const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect()
      const parentRect = el.offsetParent?.getBoundingClientRect() ?? rect
      box.style.width = rect.width + 8 + 'px'
      box.style.height = rect.height + 4 + 'px'
      box.style.left = rect.left - parentRect.left - 4 + 'px'
      box.style.top = rect.top - parentRect.top - 2 + 'px'
      words.forEach((w, j) => {
        if (w.current) w.current.style.filter = j === i ? 'blur(0)' : 'blur(3px)'
      })
    }

    moveTo(0)
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      idx = (idx + 1) % WORDS.length
      moveTo(idx)
    }, 1200)

    return () => clearInterval(interval)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!fadeOut) return
    const t = setTimeout(() => onFadeOutDone?.(), 600)
    return () => clearTimeout(t)
  }, [fadeOut])

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        position: 'fixed',
        inset: 0,
        background: 'var(--white)',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',
        gap: 32,
        zIndex: 999,
        transition: 'opacity 0.5s ease',
        opacity: fadeOut ? 0 : 1,
        pointerEvents: fadeOut ? 'none' : 'auto',
      }}
    >
      {/* Escudo + spinner */}
      <div style={{ position: 'relative', width: 120, height: 120 }}>
        <svg
          width="120"
          height="120"
          viewBox="0 0 120 120"
          style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, animation: 'spin 1.4s linear infinite' }}
        >
          <circle cx="60" cy="60" r="52" fill="none" stroke="var(--bg-mid)" strokeWidth="4" />
          <circle
            cx="60"
            cy="60"
            r="52"
            fill="none"
            stroke="var(--red)"
            strokeWidth="4"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeDasharray="326"
            strokeDashoffset="245"
            style={{ transformOrigin: '60px 60px', animation: 'arc 1.4s ease-in-out infinite' }}
          />
        </svg>
        <div
          style={{
            position: 'absolute',
            inset: 0,
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
          }}
        >
          {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
          <img src="/escudo.png" alt="AD Ceuta" style={{ width: 68, height: 68, objectFit: 'contain' }} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* TrueFocus words */}
      <div style={{ position: 'relative', display: 'flex', gap: 16, alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <span
          ref={boxRef}
          style={{
            position: 'absolute',
            border: '2px solid var(--red)',
            borderRadius: 6,
            boxShadow: '0 0 8px var(--red)',
            transition: 'all 0.45s cubic-bezier(0.25,0.46,0.45,0.94)',
            pointerEvents: 'none',
          }}
        />
        {WORDS.map((word, i) => (
          <span
            key={word}
            ref={words[i]}
            style={{
              fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)',
              fontSize: 22,
              fontWeight: 900,
              letterSpacing: '0.12em',
              color: 'var(--black)',
              transition: 'filter 0.45s ease',
            }}
          >
            {word}
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>

      <style>{`
        @keyframes spin {
          from { transform: rotate(-90deg); }
          to   { transform: rotate(270deg); }
        }
        @keyframes arc {
          0%   { stroke-dashoffset: 310; }
          50%  { stroke-dashoffset: 80; }
          100% { stroke-dashoffset: 310; }
        }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}

const STAFF = [
  {
    name: 'José Juan Romero',
    role: 'Entrenador',
    photo:
      'https://assets.laliga.com/squad/2025/t14705/man53081/512x512/man53081_t14705_2025_1_003_000.png',

function flag(nationality: string): string {
  for (const [key, emoji] of Object.entries(NAT_FLAGS)) {
    if (nationality.toLowerCase().includes(key.toLowerCase())) return emoji
  }
  return ''
}

function initials(name: string): string {
  return name
    .split(' ')
    .map((w) => w[0])
    .join('')
    .slice(0, 2)
    .toUpperCase()
}

function capitalize(s: string): string {
  return s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1).toLowerCase()
}

function CollapsibleStaff() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  return (
    <div className="mb-1">
      <button
        onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}
        className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between bg-ceuta-bg-soft px-4 py-3 ${open ? 'rounded-t-[10px]' : 'rounded-[10px]'}`}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <div className="h-4 w-[3px] rounded-sm bg-ceuta-red" />
          <span className="font-display text-[12px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-widest text-ceuta-black">
            Cuerpo técnico
          </span>
          <span className="font-display text-[10px] tracking-wide text-ceuta-muted">
            {STAFF.length} miembros
          </span>
        </div>
        <span
          className={`text-[14px] text-ceuta-muted transition-transform duration-200 ${open ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`}
        >
          ▼
        </span>
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-b-[10px] bg-ceuta-bg-soft">
          {STAFF.map((s, i) => (
            <div
              key={i}
              className={`flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 ${i > 0 ? 'border-t border-ceuta-border' : ''}`}
            >
              <Image
                src={s.photo}
                alt={s.name}
                width={38}
                height={38}
                sizes="38px"
                quality={70}
                className="size-[38px] shrink-0 rounded-full object-cover object-top"
              />
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <div className="font-display text-[13px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-ceuta-black">
                  {s.name}
                </div>
                <div className="text-[12px] text-ceuta-muted">{s.role}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function CollapsibleGroup({
  group,
  players,
  defaultOpen,
}: {
  group: string
  players: Player[]
  defaultOpen?: boolean
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(defaultOpen ?? false)
  return (
    <div className="mb-1">
      <button
        onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}
        className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between bg-ceuta-bg-soft px-4 py-3 ${open ? 'rounded-t-[10px]' : 'rounded-[10px]'}`}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <div className="h-4 w-[3px] rounded-sm bg-ceuta-red" />
          <span className="font-display text-[12px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-widest text-ceuta-black">
            {group}
          </span>
          <span className="font-display text-[10px] tracking-wide text-ceuta-muted">
            {players.length} jugadores
          </span>
        </div>
        <span
          className={`text-[14px] text-ceuta-muted transition-transform duration-200 ${open ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`}
        >
          ▼
        </span>
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-b-[10px] bg-ceuta-bg-soft">
          {players.map((p, i) => (
            <Link
              key={i}
              href={`/jugador/${p.id}`}
              className={`flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 active:bg-ceuta-bg-soft no-underline ${i > 0 ? 'border-t border-ceuta-border' : ''}`}
            >
              <div className="min-w-[32px] text-center font-display text-[22px] font-black text-ceuta-bg-mid">
                {p.dorsal ?? '—'}
              </div>
              {p.photo_url ? (
                <Image
                  src={p.photo_url}
                  alt={p.name}
                  width={38}
                  height={38}
                  sizes="38px"
                  quality={70}
                  className="size-[38px] shrink-0 rounded-full object-cover object-top"
                />
              ) : (
                <div className="flex size-[38px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-ceuta-bg-mid font-display text-[12px] font-extrabold text-ceuta-muted">
                  {initials(p.name)}
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <div className="font-display text-[13px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-ceuta-black">
                  {p.name}
                </div>
                <div className="text-[12px] text-ceuta-muted">{capitalize(p.position)}</div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <div className="text-[16px]">{flag(p.nationality)}</div>
                <svg
                  width="10"
                  height="10"
                  viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                  fill="none"
                  stroke="currentColor"
                  strokeWidth="2.5"
                  strokeLinecap="round"
                  strokeLinejoin="round"
                  className="text-ceuta-muted"
                >
                  <path d="M5 12h14M12 5l7 7-7 7" />
                </svg>
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export function PlantillaTab({
  grouped,
  total,
  previousGrouped = [],
  previousTotal = 0,
  loading,
}: {
  grouped: Group[]
  total: number
  previousGrouped?: Group[]
  previousTotal?: number
  loading: boolean
}) {
  const [season, setSeason] = useState<'2526' | '2627'>('2526')

  return (
    <>
      {/* ── SEASON SELECTOR ── */}
      <div className="chips chips-center mb-3 mt-1">
        <button className={`chip ${season === '2526' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setSeason('2526')}>
          Temp. 25/26
        </button>
        <button className={`chip ${season === '2627' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setSeason('2627')}>
          Temp. 26/27
        </button>
      </div>

      {season === '2627' ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 px-3 py-12">
          <span className="text-3xl">🔜</span>
          <p className="font-display text-[13px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-ceuta-muted text-center">
            Plantilla por determinar
          </p>
          <p className="font-body text-[12px] text-ceuta-muted text-center">
            La plantilla 26/27 se anunciará próximamente
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="px-3 mb-1">
            <CollapsibleStaff />
          </div>
          <p className="px-3 pb-1.5 font-display text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ceuta-muted">
            Jugadores · {total} · toca para expandir
          </p>
          {loading ? (
            <p className="py-8 text-center font-display text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ceuta-muted">
              Cargando plantilla…
            </p>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 px-3">
              {grouped.map((g, i) => (
                <CollapsibleGroup
                  key={g.group}
                  group={g.group}
                  players={g.players}
                  defaultOpen={i === 0}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
