function timeAgo(dateStr: string | null): string {
  if (!dateStr) return ''
  const diff = Date.now() - new Date(dateStr).getTime()
  const mins = Math.floor(diff / 60000)
  if (mins < 60) return `Hace ${mins} min`
  const hours = Math.floor(mins / 60)
  if (hours < 24) return `Hace ${hours}h`
  const days = Math.floor(hours / 24)
  if (days === 1) return 'Ayer'
  return `Hace ${days} días`
}

const THUMB_COLORS = [
  'bg-ceuta-red',
  'bg-ceuta-black',
  'bg-ceuta-bg-mid',
  'bg-ceuta-red',
  'bg-ceuta-black',
  'bg-ceuta-bg-mid',
]

function newsImageLoader({ src, width, quality }: ImageLoaderProps): string {
  if (!src.startsWith('/api/proxy/image')) return src

  const params = new URLSearchParams(src.split('?')[1] ?? '')
  params.set('w', String(width))
  params.set('q', String(quality ?? 70))
  return `/api/proxy/image?${params.toString()}`
}

function loaderFor(src: string) {
  return src.startsWith('/api/proxy/image') ? newsImageLoader : undefined
}

function isStoredNewsImage(src: string): boolean {
  try {
    const url = new URL(src)
    return url.hostname.endsWith('.supabase.co') && url.pathname.includes('/news-images/')
  } catch {
    return false
  }
}

function useNearViewport<T extends HTMLElement>() {
  const ref = useRef<T | null>(null)
  const [isNear, setIsNear] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isNear) return
    const element = ref.current
    if (!element) return

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => {
        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
          setIsNear(true)
          observer.disconnect()
        }
      },
      { rootMargin: '360px 0px' }
    )

    observer.observe(element)
    return () => observer.disconnect()
  }, [isNear])

  return [ref, isNear] as const
}

function SourceTag({ source }: { source: string }) {
  const s = SOURCES[source]
  if (!s)
    return (
      <span className="font-display text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ceuta-red">
        {source}
      </span>
    )
  return (
    <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-display text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ceuta-red">
      {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
      <img
        src={`/api/proxy/favicon?domain=${s.domain}`}
        alt=""
        width={12}
        height={12}
        loading="lazy"
        decoding="async"
        className="rounded-[2px]"
      />
      {s.label}
    </span>
  )
}

export function HomePage({
  onReady,
  onOpenHimno,
}: {
  onReady?: () => void
  onOpenHimno?: () => void
}) {
  const [news, setNews] = useState<NewsItem[]>([])
  const [newsFetched, setNewsFetched] = useState(false)
  const [featuredImageReady, setFeaturedImageReady] = useState(false)
  const [timedOut, setTimedOut] = useState(false)
  const [minTimePassed, setMinTimePassed] = useState(false)
  const onReadyRef = useRef(onReady)
  const imagePerfEnabledRef = useRef(false)
  const mountedAtRef = useRef(0)
  const newsFetchStartedAtRef = useRef(0)
  const newsRequestedRef = useRef(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    onReadyRef.current = onReady
  }, [onReady])

  // Minimum time to show loader so TrueFocus animation gets at least 3 full cycles
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setMinTimePassed(true), 3600)
    return () => clearTimeout(t)
  }, [])

  // Safety timeout — never stay on loading screen more than 8s
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setTimedOut(true), 8000)
    return () => clearTimeout(t)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    mountedAtRef.current = performance.now()
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search)
    const enabled =
      params.get('perfImages') === '1' || window.localStorage.getItem('perfImages') === '1'

    imagePerfEnabledRef.current = enabled
    if (enabled) console.info('[home image perf] tracking enabled')
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (newsRequestedRef.current) return
    newsRequestedRef.current = true
    newsFetchStartedAtRef.current = performance.now()
    fetch('/api/news?limit=10')
      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : null))
      .then((data) => {
        if (data?.news) {
          if (imagePerfEnabledRef.current) {
            console.table([
              {
                step: 'news fetched',
                msFromMount: Math.round(performance.now() - mountedAtRef.current),
                durationMs: Math.round(performance.now() - newsFetchStartedAtRef.current),
                items: data.news.length,
                featuredHasImage: Boolean(data.news[0]?.imageUrl),
              },
            ])
          }
          setNews(data.news)
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {})
      .finally(() => {
        setNewsFetched(true)
      })
  }, [])

  function reportFeaturedImageLoad(img: HTMLImageElement) {
    if (!imagePerfEnabledRef.current) return

    const currentSrc = img.currentSrc
    const resource = performance
      .getEntriesByType('resource')
      .filter((entry): entry is PerformanceResourceTiming => entry instanceof PerformanceResourceTiming)
      .find((entry) => entry.name === currentSrc)

    console.table([
      {
        step: 'featured image loaded',
        msFromMount: Math.round(performance.now() - mountedAtRef.current),
        naturalWidth: img.naturalWidth,
        naturalHeight: img.naturalHeight,
        transferKb: resource ? Math.round(resource.transferSize / 102.4) / 10 : 'n/a',
        encodedKb: resource ? Math.round(resource.encodedBodySize / 102.4) / 10 : 'n/a',
        requestMs: resource ? Math.round(resource.responseEnd - resource.startTime) : 'n/a',
        url: currentSrc,
      },
    ])
  }

  const featured = news[0]
  const featuredImage = proxyImage(featured?.imageUrl)

  // loading = true until min time passed AND news arrived AND (featured image loaded OR there's no image)
  const loading = !timedOut && (!minTimePassed || !newsFetched || (!!featuredImage && !featuredImageReady))

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading) onReadyRef.current?.()
  }, [loading])
  const recent = news.slice(1, 5)
  const older = news.slice(5)
  const [olderRef, olderImagesReady] = useNearViewport<HTMLDivElement>()

  return (
    <div className="page">
      {/* ── HERO ── */}
      {featured ? (
        <a
          href={featured.url}
          target="_blank"
          rel="noopener noreferrer"
          className="relative mx-3 mt-3 block aspect-[16/10] cursor-pointer overflow-hidden rounded-[14px] bg-ceuta-black"
        >
          {featuredImage && (
            <Image
              loader={loaderFor(featuredImage)}
              src={featuredImage}
              alt={featured.title}
              fill
              preload
              sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 420px"
              quality={70}
              unoptimized={isStoredNewsImage(featuredImage)}
              className="object-cover"
              style={{ objectPosition: 'center 20%' }}
              onLoad={(event) => {
                reportFeaturedImageLoad(event.currentTarget)
                setFeaturedImageReady(true)
              }}
            />
          )}
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/85 via-black/20 to-transparent" />
          <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 p-4">
            <div className="mb-2.5 flex w-fit items-center gap-1.5 bg-ceuta-red px-[9px] py-[3px]">
              {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
              <img
                src={`/api/proxy/favicon?domain=${SOURCES[featured.source]?.domain}`}
                alt=""
                width={14}
                height={14}
                loading="eager"
                decoding="async"
                className="rounded-[2px]"
              />
              <span className="font-display text-[10.5px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-white">
                {SOURCES[featured.source]?.label ?? featured.source}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="font-display text-[24px] font-black uppercase leading-[1.05] tracking-[0.01em] text-white">
              {featured.title}
            </div>
            <div className="mt-2 text-[12px] text-white/70">{timeAgo(featured.publishedAt)}</div>
          </div>
        </a>
      ) : (
        <div className="relative mx-3 mt-3 aspect-[16/10] overflow-hidden rounded-[14px] bg-ceuta-black">
          <div className="flex size-full items-center justify-center font-display text-[13px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-white/40">
            {loading ? 'CARGANDO…' : 'AD CEUTA'}
          </div>
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/85 via-black/20 to-transparent" />
          <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 p-4">
            <div className="mb-2.5 w-fit bg-ceuta-red px-[9px] py-[3px] font-display text-[10.5px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-white">
              Destacado
            </div>
            <div className="font-display text-[24px] font-black uppercase leading-[1.05] text-white">
              Bienvenido a AD Ceuta Info
            </div>
            <div className="mt-2 text-[12px] text-white/70">Noticias próximamente</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <NotificationBanner />

      {/* ── PRÓXIMO PARTIDO ── */}
      <div className="flex items-end justify-between px-4 pb-3 pt-6">
        <div>
          <div className="mb-1.5 font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ceuta-red">
            Próximo encuentro
          </div>
          <div className="font-display text-[22px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.02em] leading-none text-ceuta-black">
            Próximo partido
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <LiveMatchCard />

      {/* ── NOTICIAS ── */}
      <div className="flex items-end justify-between px-4 pb-3 pt-6">
        <div>
          <div className="mb-1.5 font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ceuta-red">
            Actualidad
          </div>
          <div className="font-display text-[22px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.02em] leading-none text-ceuta-black">
            Últimas noticias
          </div>
        </div>
        {news.length > 0 && (
          <Link
            href="/noticias"
            className="flex items-center gap-1 font-display text-[12px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-ceuta-black"
          >
            Ver todo <span className="text-[14px]">→</span>
          </Link>
        )}
      </div>

      {loading && (
        <div className="px-4 py-5 font-display text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-ceuta-muted">
          Cargando noticias…
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Lista vertical — noticias recientes */}
      {recent.map((n, i) => {
        const image = proxyImage(n.imageUrl)

        return (
          <a
            key={n.id}
            href={n.url}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3 border-b border-ceuta-border px-4 py-[14px] transition-colors active:bg-ceuta-bg-soft"
          >
            {image ? (
              <div className="relative size-[88px] shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-[8px]">
                <Image
                  loader={loaderFor(image)}
                  src={image}
                  alt=""
                  fill
                  sizes="88px"
                  quality={65}
                  loading="lazy"
                  unoptimized={isStoredNewsImage(image)}
                  className="object-cover"
                />
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div
                className={`relative size-[88px] shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-[8px] ${THUMB_COLORS[i % THUMB_COLORS.length]}`}
              />
            )}
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <SourceTag source={n.source} />
              <div className="mt-1 font-display text-[16px] font-bold uppercase leading-[1.2] tracking-[0.01em] text-ceuta-black">
                {n.title}
              </div>
              <div className="mt-[5px] text-[11.5px] text-ceuta-muted">
                {timeAgo(n.publishedAt)}
              </div>
            </div>
          </a>
        )
      })}

      {/* Carrusel horizontal — noticias anteriores */}
      {older.length > 0 && (
        <>
          <div className="flex items-end justify-between px-4 pb-3 pt-5">
            <div>
              <div className="mb-1.5 font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ceuta-red">
                Anteriores
              </div>
              <div className="font-display text-[22px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.02em] leading-none text-ceuta-black">
                También te puede interesar
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div
            ref={olderRef}
            className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-3 pb-3 [scrollbar-width:none] [scroll-snap-type:x_mandatory] [-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch]"
          >
            {older.map((n, i) => {
              const image = proxyImage(n.imageUrl)

              return (
                <a
                  key={n.id}
                  href={n.url}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="w-[220px] shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-[14px] border border-ceuta-border bg-white [scroll-snap-align:start]"
                >
                  {image ? (
                    <div className="relative h-[110px] w-full shrink-0">
                      <Image
                        loader={loaderFor(image)}
                        src={image}
                        alt=""
                        fill
                        sizes="220px"
                        quality={65}
                        loading="lazy"
                        unoptimized={isStoredNewsImage(image)}
                        className="object-cover"
                      />
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div
                      className={`h-[110px] w-full shrink-0 ${THUMB_COLORS[i % THUMB_COLORS.length]}`}
                    />
                  )}
                  <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-1 p-[8px_10px_10px]">
                    <SourceTag source={n.source} />
                    <div className="line-clamp-3 font-display text-[12px] font-bold uppercase leading-[1.35] text-ceuta-black">
                      {n.title}
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-auto text-[11px] text-ceuta-muted">
                      {timeAgo(n.publishedAt)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </a>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {!loading && news.length === 0 && (
        <div className="px-4 py-4 font-display text-[13px] uppercase tracking-[0.05em] text-ceuta-muted">
          No hay noticias aún — ejecuta el sync para cargarlas
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ── HIMNO ── */}
      <div className="px-3 pt-2">
        <button
          onClick={onOpenHimno}
          className="w-full cursor-pointer bg-transparent p-0 text-left"
        >
          <div className="relative flex items-center gap-[14px] overflow-hidden rounded-[14px] bg-ceuta-black px-[18px] py-4">
            <div
              className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0"
              style={{
                background:
                  'repeating-linear-gradient(-45deg, transparent 0 7px, rgba(255,255,255,0.03) 7px 14px)',
              }}
            />
            <div className="h-[42px] w-1 shrink-0 rounded-[2px] bg-ceuta-red" />
            <svg
              width="22"
              height="22"
              viewBox="0 0 24 24"
              fill="none"
              stroke="white"
              strokeWidth="1.8"
              strokeLinecap="round"
              strokeLinejoin="round"
              className="shrink-0"
            >
              <path d="M9 18V5l12-2v13" />
              <circle cx="6" cy="18" r="3" />
              <circle cx="18" cy="16" r="3" />
            </svg>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <div className="font-display text-[15px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.04em] text-white">
                Himno del AD Ceuta
              </div>
              <div className="mt-0.5 font-display text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-white/50">
                Ver letra completa
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="font-display text-[18px] font-bold text-ceuta-red">→</div>
          </div>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* ── EN REDES ── */}
      <div className="flex items-end justify-between px-4 pb-3 pt-6">
        <div>
          <div className="mb-1.5 font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ceuta-red">
            #adceuta
          </div>
          <div className="font-display text-[22px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.02em] leading-none text-ceuta-black">
            En redes
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <SocialLinks />

      <div className="h-2" />
    </div>
  )
}

'use client'

import React, { useState } from 'react'
import { StandingEntry } from '@/domain/standings/Standings'
import { Tile, TileValue, TileLabel } from '../components/ClubTiles'
import type { Leader, LeadersMap } from '../clubTypes'

function StatLeaderSection({
  title,
  icon,
  leaders,
  max,
}: {
  title: string
  icon: string
  leaders: Leader[]
  max: number
}) {
  return (
    <div className="mx-3 mb-2 rounded-[14px] bg-ceuta-bg-soft p-4">
      <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5">
        <span className="text-[13px]">{icon}</span>
        <span className="font-display text-[9px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-ceuta-muted">
          {title}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2.5">
        {leaders.map((l, i) => (
          <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <span
              className={`w-3.5 shrink-0 font-display text-[11px] font-black ${i === 0 ? 'text-ceuta-red' : 'text-ceuta-muted'}`}
            >
              {i + 1}
            </span>
            {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
            <img
              src={l.photo}
              alt={l.name}
              className={`size-8 shrink-0 rounded-full object-cover object-top ${i === 0 ? 'outline outline-2 outline-ceuta-red outline-offset-1' : ''}`}
            />
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="truncate font-display text-[12px] font-bold text-ceuta-black">
                {l.name}
              </div>
              <div className="mt-1 h-[3px] overflow-hidden rounded-sm bg-black/10">
                <div
                  className={`h-full rounded-sm ${i === 0 ? 'bg-ceuta-red' : 'bg-black/25'}`}
                  style={{ width: `${Math.round((l.value / max) * 100)}%` }}
                />
              </div>
            </div>
            <span
              className={`shrink-0 font-display text-[18px] font-black ${i === 0 ? 'text-ceuta-black' : 'text-ceuta-muted'}`}
            >
              {l.value}
            </span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function getZoneLabel(pos: number): string {
  if (pos <= 2) return 'Ascenso directo'
  if (pos <= 6) return 'Zona play-off'
  if (pos >= 19) return 'Zona descenso'
  return 'Zona media'
}

const STAT_TILES = [
  { key: 'played', label: 'Partidos jugados' },
  { key: 'won', label: 'Victorias' },
  { key: 'drawn', label: 'Empates' },
  { key: 'lost', label: 'Derrotas' },
  { key: 'goalsFor', label: 'Goles a favor' },
  { key: 'goalsAgainst', label: 'Goles en contra' },
  { key: 'goalDiff', label: 'Diferencia de goles' },
] as const

export function EstadisticasTab({
  ceuta,
  ceutaPrevious,
  form,
  leaders,
  previousLeaders,
}: {
  ceuta: StandingEntry | null
  ceutaPrevious?: StandingEntry | null
  form: string[]
  leaders: LeadersMap | null
  previousLeaders?: LeadersMap | null
}) {
  const [season, setSeason] = useState<'2526' | '2627'>('2526')

  const activeCeuta = season === '2526' ? ceutaPrevious : null
  const activeLeaders = season === '2526' ? leaders : null
  const activeForm = season === '2526' ? form : []

  const goalDiff = activeCeuta
    ? activeCeuta.goalDifference > 0
      ? `+${activeCeuta.goalDifference}`
      : activeCeuta.goalDifference
    : '—'

  const tileValues: Record<string, unknown> = {
    played: activeCeuta?.played,
    won: activeCeuta?.won,
    drawn: activeCeuta?.drawn,
    lost: activeCeuta?.lost,
    goalsFor: activeCeuta?.goalsFor,
    goalsAgainst: activeCeuta?.goalsAgainst,
    goalDiff,
  }

  if (season === '2627') {
    return (
      <>
        <div className="chips chips-center mb-2 mt-1 px-3">
          <button className="chip" onClick={() => setSeason('2526')}>Temp. 25/26</button>
          <button className="chip active" onClick={() => setSeason('2627')}>Temp. 26/27</button>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 px-3 py-12">
          <span className="text-3xl">🔜</span>
          <p className="font-display text-[13px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-ceuta-muted text-center">
            Sin datos por el momento
          </p>
          <p className="font-body text-[12px] text-ceuta-muted text-center">
            La temporada 26/27 aún no ha comenzado
          </p>
        </div>
      </>
    )
  }

  return (
    <>
      {/* ── SEASON SELECTOR ── */}
      <div className="chips chips-center mb-2 mt-1 px-3">
        <button className="chip active" onClick={() => setSeason('2526')}>Temp. 25/26</button>
        <button className="chip" onClick={() => setSeason('2627')}>Temp. 26/27</button>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-2 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 px-3">
        <Tile className="min-h-[110px] bg-ceuta-red">
          <TileValue className="text-[54px] text-white">
            {activeCeuta?.position ?? '—'}
            <span className="align-super text-[22px]">º</span>
          </TileValue>
          <TileLabel className="text-white/75">
            {activeCeuta ? getZoneLabel(activeCeuta.position) : 'Clasificación'}
          </TileLabel>
        </Tile>
        <Tile className="min-h-[110px] bg-ceuta-black">
          <TileValue className="text-[54px] text-white">{activeCeuta?.points ?? '—'}</TileValue>
          <TileLabel className="text-white/75">Puntos</TileLabel>
        </Tile>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-2 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 px-3">
        {STAT_TILES.map(({ key, label }) => (
          <Tile key={key}>
            <TileValue className="text-[42px] text-ceuta-black">
              {(tileValues[key] as React.ReactNode) ?? '—'}
            </TileValue>
            <TileLabel className="text-ceuta-muted">{label}</TileLabel>
          </Tile>
        ))}
        <Tile>
          {activeForm.length > 0 && (activeCeuta?.played ?? 0) > 0 ? (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
              {activeForm.slice(-5).map((f, i) => (
                <div key={i} className={`form-pill ${f}`}>{f}</div>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <TileValue className="text-[42px] text-ceuta-black">–</TileValue>
          )}
          <TileLabel className="text-ceuta-muted">Últimos 5</TileLabel>
        </Tile>
      </div>

      {activeLeaders ? (
        ['appearances', 'goals', 'assists', 'shots', 'shots_on_target', 'saves', 'yellow_cards', 'red_cards']
          .map((key) => activeLeaders[key])
          .filter(Boolean)
          .map((cat) => (
            <StatLeaderSection
              key={cat.label}
              title={cat.label}
              icon={cat.icon}
              leaders={cat.leaders}
              max={cat.leaders[0]?.value ?? 1}
            />
          ))
      ) : (
        <p className="mx-3 py-6 text-center font-display text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ceuta-muted">
          No hay estadísticas disponibles
        </p>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { StandingEntry } from '@/domain/standings/Standings'
import { EstadisticasTab } from './tabs/EstadisticasTab'
import { PlantillaTab } from './tabs/PlantillaTab'
import { HistoriaTab } from './tabs/HistoriaTab'
import type { Group, LeadersMap } from './clubTypes'

const TABS = [
  { id: 'stats', label: 'Estadísticas' },
  { id: 'plantilla', label: 'Plantilla' },
  { id: 'historia', label: 'Historia' },
]

interface Props {
  grouped?: Group[]
  total?: number
  previousGrouped?: Group[]
  previousTotal?: number
  ceuta?: StandingEntry | null
  ceutaPrevious?: StandingEntry | null
  form?: string[]
  leaders?: LeadersMap | null
  previousLeaders?: LeadersMap | null
}

export function ClubPage({
  grouped = [],
  total = 0,
  previousGrouped = [],
  previousTotal = 0,
  ceuta = null,
  ceutaPrevious = null,
  form = [],
  leaders = null,
  previousLeaders = null,
}: Props) {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams()
  const [tab, setTab] = useState(() => searchParams.get('tab') ?? 'stats')

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = searchParams.get('tab')
    if (t) setTab(t)
  }, [searchParams])

  return (
    <div className="page">
      {/* ── HERO ── */}
      <div className="page-hero-full relative mt-2 overflow-hidden bg-ceuta-black px-[18px] pb-[52px] pt-[22px] text-white">
        <div
          className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 right-[-40px] top-0 w-[180px]"
          style={{
            background: 'repeating-linear-gradient(-45deg, white 0 14px, #0A0A0A 14px 28px)',
            clipPath: 'polygon(60px 0, 100% 0, 100% 100%, 0 100%)',
          }}
        />
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-ceuta-red">
          <div className="h-0.5 w-[18px] bg-ceuta-red" />
          Temporada 2025–26
        </div>
        <div className="mt-2 font-display text-[38px] font-black uppercase leading-[0.95]">
          AD CEUTA <em className="not-italic text-ceuta-gold">FC</em>
          <br />
          <span className="text-[28px]">EL CLUB</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="chips mt-4 mb-3">
        {TABS.map((t) => (
          <button
            key={t.id}
            className={`chip ${tab === t.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
            onClick={() => setTab(t.id)}
          >
            {t.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {tab === 'stats' && <EstadisticasTab ceuta={ceuta} ceutaPrevious={ceutaPrevious} form={form} leaders={leaders} previousLeaders={previousLeaders} />}
      {tab === 'plantilla' && <PlantillaTab grouped={grouped} total={total} previousGrouped={previousGrouped} previousTotal={previousTotal} loading={false} />}
      {tab === 'historia' && <HistoriaTab />}

      <div className="h-2" />
    </div>
  )
}

<file path="src/features/home/LiveMatchCard.tsx">
'use client'

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { LiveMatch } from '@/app/api/live/route'
import { usePushNotifications } from '@/hooks/usePushNotifications'
import { findLogo } from '@/lib/findLogo'
import { ScrapedMatch } from '@/infrastructure/scrapers/adceutaScraper'
import Link from 'next/link'

const POLL_INTERVAL_LIVE = 60_000
const POLL_INTERVAL_PRE = 300_000

const EVENT_ICONS: Record<string, string> = {
  goal: '⚽',
  yellowCard: '🟨',
  redCard: '🟥',
  substitution: '🔄',
}

function GoalFlash({ show }: { show: boolean }) {
  if (!show) return null
  return (
    <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 z-[2] rounded-[14px] bg-ceuta-red/25 animate-[goalFlash_1.2s_ease-out_forwards]">
      <style>{`@keyframes goalFlash { 0%{opacity:1} 100%{opacity:0} }`}</style>
    </div>
  )
}

function LiveBadge({ clock }: { clock: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
      <span className="inline-block size-2 animate-[pulse_1.2s_ease-in-out_infinite] rounded-full bg-ceuta-red">
        <style>{`@keyframes pulse { 0%,100%{opacity:1;transform:scale(1)} 50%{opacity:0.4;transform:scale(1.3)} }`}</style>
      </span>
      <span className="font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ceuta-red">
        EN VIVO {clock && `· ${clock}`}
      </span>
    </div>
  )
}

function TeamCrest({ name, logos }: { name: string; logos: Record<string, string> }) {
  const logo = findLogo(logos, name)
  if (logo) {
    return (
      <div className="flex size-[52px] items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white">
        {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
        {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
        <img src={logo} alt={name} className="size-9 object-contain" fetchPriority="high" />
      </div>
    )
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex size-[52px] items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white font-display text-[13px] font-black text-ceuta-black">
      {name.slice(0, 3).toUpperCase()}
    </div>
  )
}

function MatchEvents({ events }: { events: LiveMatch['events'] }) {
  if (events.length === 0) return null
  const sorted = [...events].sort((a, b) => (parseInt(a.minute) || 0) - (parseInt(b.minute) || 0))
  return (
    <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-[5px] border-t border-white/[0.08] pt-2.5">
      {sorted.map((ev, i) => (
        <div key={i} className="grid grid-cols-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
            {ev.team === 'home' && (
              <>
                <span className="text-[13px]">{EVENT_ICONS[ev.type]}</span>
                <span className="font-display text-[12px] font-semibold text-white/90">
                  {ev.playerName.split(' ').pop()}
                </span>
                <span className="font-display text-[10px] text-white/40">{ev.minute}</span>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
            {ev.team === 'away' && (
              <>
                <span className="font-display text-[10px] text-white/40">{ev.minute}</span>
                <span className="font-display text-[12px] font-semibold text-white/90">
                  {ev.playerName.split(' ').pop()}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[13px]">{EVENT_ICONS[ev.type]}</span>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

function LineupButton({ eventId, match, score }: { eventId: string; match: string; score?: string }) {
  return (
    <Link
      href={`/alineacion?eventId=${eventId}&match=${encodeURIComponent(match)}${score ? `&score=${encodeURIComponent(score)}` : ''}`}
      className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-ceuta-red px-3 py-1.5"
    >
      <svg
        width="12"
        height="12"
        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
        fill="none"
        stroke="white"
        strokeWidth="2.5"
        strokeLinecap="round"
        strokeLinejoin="round"
      >
        <rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="2" />
        <path d="M3 9h18M3 15h18M9 3v18" />
      </svg>
      <span className="font-display text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-white">
        Alineación
      </span>
    </Link>
  )
}

function MatchCardShell({ children, relative }: { children: React.ReactNode; relative?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div
      className={`mx-3 overflow-hidden rounded-[14px] bg-ceuta-black px-[18px] pb-[18px] pt-5 text-white ${relative ? 'relative' : ''}`}
    >
      {/* diagonal stripes */}
      <div
        className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 right-0 top-0 w-[110px]"
        style={{
          background:
            'repeating-linear-gradient(-45deg, transparent 0 7px, rgba(255,255,255,0.04) 7px 14px)',
        }}
      />
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}

function Competition() {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-[10.5px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ceuta-red">
      <span className="inline-block size-[6px] rounded-full bg-ceuta-red" />
      Liga Hypermotion
    </div>
  )
}

export function LiveMatchCard() {
  const [match, setMatch] = useState<LiveMatch | null>(null)
  const [nextMatch, setNextMatch] = useState<ScrapedMatch | null>(null)
  const [goalFlash, setGoalFlash] = useState(false)
  const [logos, setLogos] = useState<Record<string, string>>({})
  const prevScoreRef = useRef<string>('')
  const { permission, subscribed, subscribe } = usePushNotifications()

  const fetchLive = async () => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/live', { cache: 'no-store' })
    if (!res.ok) return
    const data = await res.json()
    const m: LiveMatch | null = data.match
    if (m) {
      const scoreKey = `${m.homeScore}-${m.awayScore}`
      if (prevScoreRef.current && prevScoreRef.current !== scoreKey) {
        setGoalFlash(true)
        setTimeout(() => setGoalFlash(false), 1500)
      }
      prevScoreRef.current = scoreKey
    }
    setMatch(m)
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchLive()
    fetch('/api/matches/next')
      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : null))
      .then((d) => {
        if (d?.match) setNextMatch(d.match)
      })
      .catch(() => {})
    fetch('/api/teams')
      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : null))
      .then((d) => {
        if (d?.teams) {
          const map: Record<string, string> = {}
          for (const t of d.teams) if (t.logoUrl) map[t.name] = t.logoUrl
          setLogos(map)
          // Preload next match logos so they appear instantly
          if (nextMatch) {
            for (const teamName of [nextMatch.homeTeam, nextMatch.awayTeam]) {
              const url = map[teamName] ?? Object.entries(map).find(([k]) => k.toLowerCase().includes(teamName.toLowerCase().slice(0, 5)))?.[1]
              if (url) { const img = new Image(); img.src = url }
            }
          }
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {})

    const interval = setInterval(
      fetchLive,
      match?.state === 'in' ? POLL_INTERVAL_LIVE : POLL_INTERVAL_PRE
    )
    return () => clearInterval(interval)
  }, [match?.state])

  // No ESPN data — show next match from DB
  if (!match) {
    if (!nextMatch)
      return (
        <MatchCardShell>
          <Competition />
          <div className="relative z-[1] mt-5 grid grid-cols-[1fr_auto_1fr] items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
              <div className="size-[52px] animate-pulse rounded-full bg-white/10" />
              <div className="h-3 w-16 animate-pulse rounded bg-white/10" />
            </div>
            <div className="text-center">
              <div className="font-display text-[12px] font-black tracking-[0.16em] text-ceuta-muted">VS</div>
              <div className="mt-2 h-6 w-14 animate-pulse rounded bg-white/10 mx-auto" />
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
              <div className="size-[52px] animate-pulse rounded-full bg-white/10" />
              <div className="h-3 w-16 animate-pulse rounded bg-white/10" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </MatchCardShell>
      )
    const dateStr = `${nextMatch.date}T${nextMatch.time}:00`
    return (
      <MatchCardShell>
        <Competition />
        <div className="relative z-[1] mt-4 grid grid-cols-[1fr_auto_1fr] items-center gap-2.5">
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
            <TeamCrest name={nextMatch.homeTeam} logos={logos} />
            <div className="text-center font-display text-[15px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.04em]">
              {nextMatch.homeTeam}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="text-center">
            <div className="font-display text-[12px] font-black tracking-[0.16em] text-ceuta-muted">
              VS
            </div>
            <div className="mt-1.5 font-display text-[22px] font-black leading-none tracking-[0.04em]">
              {new Date(dateStr)
                .toLocaleDateString('es-ES', { weekday: 'short', timeZone: 'UTC' })
                .toUpperCase()
                .replace('.', '')}
              <br />
              {new Date(dateStr).toLocaleDateString('es-ES', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', timeZone: 'UTC' })}
              <br />
              <span className="text-[16px] text-white/60">Por confirmar</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
            <TeamCrest name={nextMatch.awayTeam} logos={logos} />
            <div className="text-center font-display text-[15px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.04em]">
              {nextMatch.awayTeam}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-[18px] flex items-center justify-between border-t border-white/10 pt-[14px]">
          <span className="font-display text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-white/50">
            Liga Hypermotion
          </span>
          {nextMatch.espnEventId && (
            <LineupButton
              eventId={nextMatch.espnEventId}
              match={`${nextMatch.homeTeam} vs ${nextMatch.awayTeam}`}
            />
          )}
        </div>
      </MatchCardShell>
    )
  }

  const isLive = match.state === 'in'
  const isFinished = match.state === 'post'

  return (
    <MatchCardShell relative>
      <GoalFlash show={goalFlash} />

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <Competition />
        {isLive && <LiveBadge clock={match.clock} />}
        {isFinished && (
          <span className="font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-white/50">
            FINALIZADO
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="relative z-[1] mt-4 grid grid-cols-[1fr_auto_1fr] items-center gap-2.5">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
          <TeamCrest name={match.homeTeam} logos={logos} />
          <div className="text-center font-display text-[15px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.04em]">
            {match.homeTeam}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="text-center">
          {isLive || isFinished ? (
            <div className="font-display text-[38px] font-black leading-none tracking-[-0.02em] text-white">
              {match.homeScore} – {match.awayScore}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="mb-1.5 font-display text-[12px] font-black tracking-[0.16em] text-ceuta-muted">
                VS
              </div>
              {(() => {
                // Prefer DB date (nextMatch) over ESPN startDate which can be corrupt
                const dateStr = nextMatch
                  ? `${nextMatch.date}T${nextMatch.time}:00`
                  : match.startDate
                const d = new Date(dateStr)
                return (
                  <div className="font-display text-[22px] font-black leading-none tracking-[0.04em]">
                    {d.toLocaleDateString('es-ES', { weekday: 'short', timeZone: 'UTC' }).toUpperCase().replace('.', '')}
                    <br />
                    {d.toLocaleDateString('es-ES', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', timeZone: 'UTC' })}
                    <br />
                    <span className="text-[16px] text-white/60">
                      Por confirmar
                    </span>
                  </div>
                )
              })()}
            </>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
          <TeamCrest name={match.awayTeam} logos={logos} />
          <div className="text-center font-display text-[15px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.04em]">
            {match.awayTeam}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <MatchEvents events={match.events} />

      <div className="mt-[18px] flex items-center justify-between border-t border-white/10 pt-[14px]">
        <span className="font-display text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-white/50">
          {match.venue || 'Liga Hypermotion'}
        </span>
        {match.espnId && (() => {
          const kickoff = nextMatch
            ? new Date(`${nextMatch.date}T${nextMatch.time}:00`)
            : new Date(match.startDate)
          const minutesUntil = (kickoff.getTime() - Date.now()) / 60_000
          const showLineup = isLive || isFinished || minutesUntil <= 20
          return showLineup ? (
            <LineupButton
              eventId={match.espnId}
              match={`${match.homeTeam} vs ${match.awayTeam}`}
              score={(isLive || isFinished) ? `${match.homeScore}-${match.awayScore}` : undefined}
            />
          ) : null
        })()}
      </div>
    </MatchCardShell>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/features/standings/ClasificacionPage.tsx">
'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { StandingEntry } from '@/domain/standings/Standings'
import { findLogo as findLogoUtil } from '@/lib/findLogo'
import { FormPill } from '@/components/ui/FormPill'

function getZone(pos: number, total: number): 'promo' | 'playoff' | 'relegation' | 'none' {
  if (pos <= 2) return 'promo'
  if (pos <= 6) return 'playoff'
  if (pos >= total - 3) return 'relegation'
  return 'none'
}

function getZoneLabel(pos: number, total: number): string {
  if (pos <= 2) return 'Ascenso directo'
  if (pos <= 6) return 'Zona play-off'
  if (pos >= total - 3) return 'Zona descenso'
  return 'Zona media'
}

function isCeuta(entry: StandingEntry): boolean {
  return entry.teamName.toLowerCase().includes('ceuta')
}

const ZONE_BAR: Record<string, string> = {
  promo: 'bg-ceuta-gold',
  playoff: 'bg-ceuta-red',
  relegation: 'bg-ceuta-black',
  none: 'bg-transparent',
}

const ZONE_POS: Record<string, string> = {
  promo: 'text-ceuta-black',
  playoff: 'text-ceuta-black',
  relegation: 'text-ceuta-muted',
  none: 'text-ceuta-muted',
}

function SkeletonRow({ i }: { i: number }) {
  return (
    <div
      className="grid items-center gap-1 border-b border-ceuta-border px-[14px] py-[11px]"
      style={{ gridTemplateColumns: '32px 1fr repeat(4, 28px) 34px', opacity: 1 - i * 0.03 }}
    >
      <div className="mx-auto h-[14px] w-4 animate-pulse rounded-[3px] bg-ceuta-bg-mid" />
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <div className="size-[22px] shrink-0 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-ceuta-bg-mid" />
        <div
          className="h-[13px] animate-pulse rounded-[3px] bg-ceuta-bg-mid"
          style={{ width: 90 + (i % 3) * 20 }}
        />
      </div>
      {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((j) => (
        <div key={j} className="mx-auto h-[13px] w-5 animate-pulse rounded-[3px] bg-ceuta-bg-mid" />
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

interface Props {
  entries?: StandingEntry[]
  previousEntries?: StandingEntry[]
  form?: string[]
  logos?: Record<string, string>
}

function StandingsTable({ entries, logos }: { entries: StandingEntry[]; logos: Record<string, string> }) {
  const total = entries.length
  function findLogo(name: string): string | null {
    return findLogoUtil(logos, name)
  }
  return (
    <div className="mt-2 bg-white">
      <div
        className="grid items-center gap-1 border-b-2 border-ceuta-black px-[14px] pb-2"
        style={{ gridTemplateColumns: '32px 1fr repeat(4, 28px) 34px' }}
      >
        {['#', 'Equipo', 'PJ', 'DG', 'GF', 'GC', 'PTS'].map((h, i) => (
          <div
            key={h}
            className={`font-display text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] ${i === 0 || i > 1 ? 'text-center' : 'text-left'} ${h === 'PTS' ? 'text-ceuta-black' : 'text-ceuta-muted'}`}
          >
            {h}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      {entries.map((t) => {
        const zone = getZone(t.position, total)
        const mine = isCeuta(t)
        const logo = findLogo(t.teamName)
        return (
          <div
            key={t.id ?? t.externalId}
            className={`relative grid items-center gap-1 border-b border-ceuta-border px-[14px] py-[11px] active:bg-ceuta-bg-soft ${mine ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-ceuta-red-soft to-transparent' : ''}`}
            style={{ gridTemplateColumns: '32px 1fr repeat(4, 28px) 34px' }}
          >
            {zone !== 'none' && (
              <div className={`absolute left-0 top-1/2 h-[22px] w-[3px] -translate-y-1/2 rounded-r-sm ${mine ? 'w-[4px] bg-ceuta-red' : ZONE_BAR[zone]}`} />
            )}
            <div className={`text-center font-display text-[16px] font-black ${mine ? 'text-ceuta-red' : ZONE_POS[zone]}`}>
              {t.position}
            </div>
            <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
              {logo ? (
                // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                <img src={logo} alt={t.teamName} className="size-[22px] shrink-0 object-contain" />
              ) : (
                <div className={`flex size-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full font-display text-[8px] font-black ${mine ? 'bg-ceuta-red text-white' : 'bg-ceuta-bg-soft text-ceuta-muted'}`}>
                  {t.teamName.slice(0, 3).toUpperCase()}
                </div>
              )}
              <div className={`truncate font-display text-[14px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.02em] ${mine ? 'text-ceuta-red-dark' : 'text-ceuta-black'}`}>
                {t.teamName}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="text-center font-display text-[13px] font-semibold text-ceuta-black">{t.played}</div>
            <div className="text-center font-display text-[13px] font-semibold text-ceuta-black">{t.goalDifference > 0 ? `+${t.goalDifference}` : t.goalDifference}</div>
            <div className="text-center font-display text-[13px] font-semibold text-ceuta-black">{t.goalsFor}</div>
            <div className="text-center font-display text-[13px] font-semibold text-ceuta-black">{t.goalsAgainst}</div>
            <div className={`text-center font-display text-[16px] font-black ${mine ? 'text-ceuta-red' : 'text-ceuta-black'}`}>{t.points}</div>
          </div>
        )
      })}
    </div>
  )
}

function CeutaCard({ entry, form, total }: { entry: StandingEntry; form?: string[]; total: number }) {
  const empty = entry.played === 0
  return (
    <div className="relative z-[2] -mt-7 mx-3 rounded-[14px] border-2 border-ceuta-black bg-white p-[18px] shadow-[6px_6px_0_#D62027]">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="font-display text-[56px] font-black leading-[0.85] tracking-[-0.02em] text-ceuta-black">
          {empty ? '–' : entry.position}
          {!empty && <sup className="text-[18px] text-ceuta-red ml-0.5 align-super">º</sup>}
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1">
          <div className="font-display text-[18px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.02em] text-ceuta-black">
            AD Ceuta FC
          </div>
          <div className="mt-0.5 font-display text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-ceuta-muted">
            {empty ? 'Temporada no iniciada' : getZoneLabel(entry.position, total)}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="text-right">
          <div className="font-display text-[32px] font-black leading-[0.9] text-ceuta-red">
            {empty ? '–' : entry.points}
          </div>
          <div className="mt-0.5 font-display text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ceuta-muted">
            Puntos
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-[14px] grid grid-cols-5 gap-1 border-t border-dashed border-ceuta-border pt-[14px]">
        {[
          { v: entry.played, l: 'PJ' },
          { v: entry.won, l: 'PG' },
          { v: entry.drawn, l: 'PE' },
          { v: entry.lost, l: 'PP' },
          { v: entry.goalDifference > 0 ? `+${entry.goalDifference}` : entry.goalDifference, l: 'DG' },
        ].map(({ v, l }) => {
          const empty = entry.played === 0
          return (
          <div key={l} className="flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5">
            <div className="font-display text-[16px] font-black text-ceuta-black">{empty ? '–' : v}</div>
            <div className="font-display text-[9.5px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-ceuta-muted">{l}</div>
          </div>
        )
        })}
      </div>
      {form && form.length > 0 && entry.played > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="font-display text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ceuta-muted">
            Últimos 5
          </span>
          {form.slice(-5).map((f, i) => (
            <FormPill key={i} result={f} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export function ClasificacionPage({ entries = [], previousEntries = [], form = [], logos = {} }: Props) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('actual')
  const me = entries.find(isCeuta)
  const prevMe = previousEntries.find(isCeuta)
  const total = entries.length
  const prevTotal = previousEntries.length

  function findLogo(name: string): string | null {
    return findLogoUtil(logos, name)
  }

  return (
    <div className="page">
      {/* ── HERO ── */}
      <div className="page-hero-full relative mt-2 overflow-hidden bg-ceuta-black px-[18px] pb-[52px] pt-[22px] text-white">
        {/* diagonal stripes */}
        <div
          className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 right-[-40px] top-0 w-[180px]"
          style={{
            background: 'repeating-linear-gradient(-45deg, white 0 14px, #0A0A0A 14px 28px)',
            clipPath: 'polygon(60px 0, 100% 0, 100% 100%, 0 100%)',
          }}
        />
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-ceuta-red">
          <div className="h-0.5 w-[18px] bg-ceuta-red" />
          {tab === 'actual' ? `Temporada 2026–27 · Jornada ${me?.played ?? '—'}` : 'Temporada 2025–26 · Final'}
        </div>
        <div className="mt-2 font-display text-[38px] font-black uppercase leading-[0.95]">
          CLASIFI<em className="not-italic text-ceuta-gold">CACIÓN</em>
          <br />
          LIGA HYPERMOTION
        </div>
      </div>

      {tab === 'anterior' && (
        <>
          {previousEntries.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="px-4 py-5 font-display text-[13px] uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-ceuta-muted">
              No hay datos de la temporada anterior
            </p>
          ) : (
            <>
              {prevMe && <CeutaCard entry={prevMe} total={prevTotal} />}
              {/* ── TABS ── */}
              <div className="chips mt-4 mb-2">
                {[
                  { id: 'actual', l: 'Temporada actual' },
                  { id: 'anterior', l: 'Temp. 25/26' },
                ].map((t) => (
                  <button
                    key={t.id}
                    className={`chip ${tab === t.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
                    onClick={() => setTab(t.id)}
                  >
                    {t.l}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
              <div className="px-3 pb-1 pt-3">
                <div className="font-display text-[18px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.04em] text-ceuta-black">
                  Tabla completa
                </div>
              </div>
              <StandingsTable entries={previousEntries} logos={logos} />
              <div className="mt-4 bg-ceuta-bg-soft p-4">
                <div className="mb-2.5 font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ceuta-muted">
                  Zonas de la tabla
                </div>
                {[
                  { bar: 'bg-ceuta-gold', text: 'Ascenso directo · 1.º–2.º' },
                  { bar: 'bg-ceuta-red', text: 'Play-off ascenso · 3.º–6.º' },
                  { bar: 'bg-ceuta-black', text: 'Descenso · 19.º–22.º' },
                ].map(({ bar, text }) => (
                  <div key={text} className="flex items-center gap-2.5 py-1">
                    <div className={`h-4 w-1 shrink-0 rounded-sm ${bar}`} />
                    <div className="font-display text-[12px] font-semibold text-ceuta-black">{text}</div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
              <div className="h-4" />
            </>
          )}
        </>
      )}

      {tab === 'actual' && (
        <>
          {me && <CeutaCard entry={me} form={form} total={total} />}

          {/* ── TABS ── */}
          <div className="chips mt-4 mb-2">
            {[
              { id: 'actual', l: 'Temporada actual' },
              { id: 'anterior', l: 'Temp. 25/26' },
            ].map((t) => (
              <button
                key={t.id}
                className={`chip ${tab === t.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => setTab(t.id)}
              >
                {t.l}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* ── SECTION HEADER ── */}
          <div className="px-3 pb-1 pt-3">
            <div className="font-display text-[18px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.04em] text-ceuta-black">
              Tabla completa
            </div>
          </div>

      {/* ── TABLE ── */}
      <StandingsTable entries={entries} logos={logos} />

      {/* ── LEGEND ── */}
      <div className="mt-4 bg-ceuta-bg-soft p-4">
        <div className="mb-2.5 font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ceuta-muted">
          Zonas de la tabla
        </div>
        {[
          { bar: 'bg-ceuta-gold', text: 'Ascenso directo · 1.º–2.º' },
          { bar: 'bg-ceuta-red', text: 'Play-off ascenso · 3.º–6.º' },
          { bar: 'bg-ceuta-black', text: 'Descenso · 19.º–22.º' },
        ].map(({ bar, text }) => (
          <div key={text} className="flex items-center gap-2.5 py-1">
            <div className={`h-4 w-1 shrink-0 rounded-sm ${bar}`} />
            <div className="font-display text-[12px] font-semibold text-ceuta-black">{text}</div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="h-4" />
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

<file path="src/app/mercado/MercadoClient.tsx">
'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import Image from 'next/image'
import type { Transfer } from '@/infrastructure/supabase/TransfersRepository'

const POSITIONS: Record<string, string> = {
  POR: 'Portero', DEF: 'Defensa', MED: 'Centrocampista', DEL: 'Delantero',
}

type Tab = 'in' | 'out' | 'renew' | 'rumor'

const TABS: { id: Tab; label: string }[] = [
  { id: 'renew', label: 'Renovados' },
  { id: 'in',    label: 'Fichados'  },
  { id: 'out',   label: 'Salidas'   },
  { id: 'rumor', label: 'Rumores'   },
]

const BADGE_LABEL: Record<Tab, string> = {
  renew: 'Renovado',
  in:    'Oficial',
  out:   'Salida',
  rumor: 'Rumor',
}

const REL_TEXT: Record<number, string> = { 3: 'Muy fiable', 2: 'Posible', 1: 'Especulación' }

const ROUTE_LABEL: Record<Tab, string> = {
  in:    'Procede de',
  out:   'Destino',
  renew: 'Renueva hasta',
  rumor: 'Suena desde',
}

function isExitRumor(t: Transfer): boolean {
  return t.clubFrom === 'AD Ceuta FC'
}

function ArrowIn() {
  return <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/><polyline points="12 5 19 12 12 19"/></svg>
}
function ArrowOut() {
  return <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"><line x1="19" y1="12" x2="5" y2="12"/><polyline points="12 19 5 12 12 5"/></svg>
}
function RenewIcon() {
  return <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"><polyline points="23 4 23 10 17 10"/><polyline points="1 20 1 14 7 14"/><path d="M3.51 9a9 9 0 0114.85-3.36L23 10M1 14l4.64 4.36A9 9 0 0020.49 15"/></svg>
}
function CheckIcon() {
  return <svg width="11" height="11" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor"><path d="M9 16.17L4.83 12l-1.42 1.41L9 19 21 7l-1.41-1.41z"/></svg>
}
function FlameIcon({ on }: { on: boolean }) {
  return (
    <svg width="17" height="17" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" className={on ? 'text-ceuta-red' : 'text-ceuta-border'}>
      <path d="M12 2c0 4-4 5-4 9a4 4 0 008 0c0-1.2-.5-2-1-2.8.3 1.4-.3 2.3-1 2.3.6-2.3-1-3.5-1-5.5 0-1.5 0-3-1-5z"/>
    </svg>
  )
}

function PlayerPhoto({ photoUrl, name, type }: { photoUrl: string | null; name: string; type: Tab }) {
  const [error, setError] = useState(false)
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false)
  const initials = name.split(' ').map(w => w[0]).join('').slice(0, 2).toUpperCase()
  const ringCls =
    type === 'in'    ? 'ring-2 ring-ceuta-red'    :
    type === 'renew' ? 'ring-2 ring-[#1E7F47]'    :
                       'ring-2 ring-ceuta-border'

  return (
    <div className={`relative size-[58px] shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-ceuta-bg-soft ${ringCls}`}>
      {photoUrl && !error ? (
        <>
          {!loaded && (
            <div className="absolute inset-0 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-ceuta-bg-mid" />
          )}
          <Image
            src={photoUrl.includes('supabase.co') ? photoUrl : `/api/proxy/image?url=${encodeURIComponent(photoUrl)}&w=120`}
            alt={name} fill sizes="58px"
            className="object-cover object-top"
            onError={() => setError(true)}
            onLoad={() => setLoaded(true)}
            unoptimized
          />
        </>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex size-full items-center justify-center bg-ceuta-black">
          <span className="font-display text-[20px] font-black text-white/30">{initials}</span>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function TransferCard({ t, type, idx }: { t: Transfer; type: Tab; idx: number }) {
  const exitRumor = type === 'rumor' && isExitRumor(t)

  const badgeCls = exitRumor ? 'bg-ceuta-bg-mid text-ceuta-muted' :
    type === 'renew' ? 'bg-[#E4F3EA] text-[#1E7F47]'  :
    type === 'in'    ? 'bg-ceuta-black text-white'      :
    type === 'out'   ? 'bg-ceuta-red-soft text-ceuta-red' :
                       'bg-ceuta-bg-mid text-ceuta-muted'
  const badgeLabel = exitRumor ? 'Posible salida' : BADGE_LABEL[type]

  const routeClub = type === 'in'
    ? t.clubFrom
    : type === 'out'
      ? t.clubTo
      : type === 'renew'
        ? 'AD Ceuta FC'
        : exitRumor ? (t.clubTo ?? 'Por determinar') : (t.clubFrom ?? null)

  const routeLabel = exitRumor ? 'Posible destino' : ROUTE_LABEL[type]
  const showRoute  = !!(routeClub || type === 'renew')

  return (
    <div
      className={`relative overflow-hidden rounded-[14px] bg-white ${type === 'rumor' ? 'border-[1.5px] border-dashed border-ceuta-border' : 'border border-ceuta-border'}`}
      style={{ animationDelay: `${idx * 0.04}s` }}
    >
      {/* left colour bar */}
      <div className={`absolute bottom-0 left-0 top-0 w-1 ${
        type === 'renew' ? 'bg-[#1E7F47]'  :
        type === 'in'    ? 'bg-ceuta-red'   :
        type === 'out'   ? 'bg-ceuta-black' :
                           'bg-ceuta-gold'
      }`} />

      <div className="pb-[13px] pl-[19px] pr-[15px] pt-[14px]">
        {/* top row */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <PlayerPhoto photoUrl={t.photoUrl} name={t.name} type={type} />

          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <div className="truncate font-display text-[19px] font-extrabold uppercase leading-none tracking-[0.01em] text-ceuta-black">
              {t.name}
            </div>
            <div className="mt-[7px] flex flex-wrap items-center gap-[7px]">
              <span className={`rounded-[4px] px-2 py-[3px] font-display text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] ${
                type === 'renew' ? 'bg-[#1E7F47] text-white'  :
                type === 'in'    ? 'bg-ceuta-red text-white'   :
                type === 'out'   ? 'bg-ceuta-black text-white' :
                                   'bg-ceuta-gold text-black'
              }`}>
                {POSITIONS[t.position] ?? t.position}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <span className={`flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 self-start rounded-[5px] px-[9px] py-[5px] font-display text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] ${badgeCls}`}>
            {type === 'in'    && <CheckIcon />}
            {type === 'renew' && <RenewIcon />}
            {badgeLabel}
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* route box */}
        {showRoute && (
          <div className="mt-[13px] flex items-center gap-[10px] rounded-[8px] bg-ceuta-bg-soft px-3 py-[10px]">
            <div className={`flex size-[26px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full ${
              type === 'renew' ? 'bg-[#1E7F47] text-white'  :
              type === 'in'    ? 'bg-ceuta-red text-white'   :
              type === 'out'   ? 'bg-ceuta-black text-white' :
                                 'bg-ceuta-gold text-black'
            }`}>
              {(type === 'out' || exitRumor) ? <ArrowOut /> : type === 'renew' ? <RenewIcon /> : <ArrowIn />}
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="font-display text-[9.5px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.13em] text-ceuta-muted">
                {routeLabel}
              </div>
              <div className="mt-[1px] truncate font-display text-[15px] font-bold uppercase leading-[1.05] tracking-[0.02em] text-ceuta-black">
                {type === 'renew' ? (t.clubTo ?? 'AD Ceuta FC') : routeClub}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* reliability (rumors) */}
        {type === 'rumor' && (
          <div className="mt-[13px] flex items-center gap-[9px] border-t border-dashed border-ceuta-border pt-3">
            <span className="font-display text-[9.5px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.13em] text-ceuta-muted">
              Fiabilidad
            </span>
            <div className="flex gap-1">
              {[1, 2, 3].map(i => <FlameIcon key={i} on={i <= t.reliability} />)}
            </div>
            <span className={`ml-auto font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.06em] ${
              t.reliability === 3 ? 'text-ceuta-red'   :
              t.reliability === 2 ? 'text-[#c9a200]'   :
                                    'text-ceuta-muted'
            }`}>
              {REL_TEXT[t.reliability] ?? ''}
            </span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* notes */}
        {t.notes && (
          <p className="mt-[10px] font-body text-[12px] leading-[1.45] text-ceuta-muted">
            {t.notes}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function RumorDivider({ label }: { label: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-[10px] pb-[10px] pt-[18px]">
      <div className="h-0.5 w-[18px] shrink-0 bg-ceuta-red" />
      <span className="font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-ceuta-red">
        {label}
      </span>
      <div className="h-px flex-1 bg-ceuta-border" />
    </div>
  )
}

function RumorList({ transfers }: { transfers: Transfer[] }) {
  const arrivals = transfers.filter(t => !isExitRumor(t))
  const exits    = transfers.filter(t => isExitRumor(t))
  return (
    <div className="pb-6">
      {arrivals.length > 0 && (
        <>
          <RumorDivider label="En el radar" />
          <div className="grid gap-[10px] [grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(320px,1fr))]">
            {arrivals.map((t, i) => <TransferCard key={t.id} t={t} type="rumor" idx={i} />)}
          </div>
        </>
      )}
      {exits.length > 0 && (
        <>
          <RumorDivider label="Posibles salidas" />
          <div className="grid gap-[10px] [grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(320px,1fr))]">
            {exits.map((t, i) => <TransferCard key={t.id} t={t} type="rumor" idx={i} />)}
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function EmptyState({ tab }: { tab: Tab }) {
  const msgs: Record<Tab, string> = {
    in:    'Sin fichajes confirmados todavía',
    renew: 'Sin renovaciones confirmadas todavía',
    out:   'Sin salidas confirmadas todavía',
    rumor: 'Sin rumores por el momento',
  }
  return (
    <div className="py-12 text-center font-display text-[13px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-ceuta-muted">
      {msgs[tab]}
    </div>
  )
}

export default function MercadoClient({ transfers }: { transfers: Transfer[] }) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('renew')

  const counts: Record<Tab, number> = {
    renew: transfers.filter(t => t.type === 'renew').length,
    in:    transfers.filter(t => t.type === 'in').length,
    out:   transfers.filter(t => t.type === 'out').length,
    rumor: transfers.filter(t => t.type === 'rumor').length,
  }

  const filtered = transfers.filter(t => t.type === tab)
  const headTitle: Record<Tab, string> = {
    renew: 'Renovaciones', in: 'Altas confirmadas',
    out:   'Bajas y salidas', rumor: 'En el radar',
  }

  return (
    <div className="page min-h-screen bg-ceuta-bg">
      {/* Hero */}
      <div className="page-hero-full relative mt-2 overflow-hidden bg-ceuta-black px-[18px] pb-[52px] pt-[22px] text-white">
        <div
          className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 right-[-40px] top-0 w-[180px]"
          style={{
            background: 'repeating-linear-gradient(-45deg, white 0 14px, #0A0A0A 14px 28px)',
            clipPath: 'polygon(60px 0, 100% 0, 100% 100%, 0 100%)',
          }}
        />
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-ceuta-red">
          <div className="h-0.5 w-[18px] bg-ceuta-red" />
          Temporada 2026–27
        </div>
        <div className="mt-2 font-display text-[38px] font-black uppercase leading-[0.95]">
          MERCADO DE<br />
          <em className="not-italic text-ceuta-gold">FICHAJES</em>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Counter pills overlapping hero */}
      <div className="relative z-[3] -mt-11 grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5 px-3">
        {TABS.map(t => {
          const active = tab === t.id
          const COLOR: Record<string, string> = {
            renew: '#1E7F47',
            in:    'var(--red)',
            out:   'var(--black)',
            rumor: 'var(--gold)',
          }
          const color = COLOR[t.id]
          const isLight = t.id === 'rumor'

          return (
            <button
              key={t.id}
              onClick={() => setTab(t.id)}
              className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-[14px] border-[1.5px] px-1.5 pb-[11px] pt-[13px] text-center shadow-[0_6px_18px_rgba(0,0,0,0.08)] transition-colors"
              style={active
                ? { backgroundColor: color, borderColor: color }
                : { backgroundColor: '#fff', borderColor: 'var(--border)' }
              }
            >
              {!active && (
                <div className="absolute left-0 right-0 top-0 h-1" style={{ backgroundColor: color }} />
              )}
              <div
                className="font-display text-[30px] font-black leading-[0.9]"
                style={{ color: active ? (isLight ? '#000' : '#fff') : 'var(--black)' }}
              >
                {counts[t.id]}
              </div>
              <div
                className="mt-[5px] font-display text-[9px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.08em]"
                style={{ color: active ? (isLight ? 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)') : 'var(--muted)' }}
              >
                {t.label}
              </div>
            </button>
          )
        })}
      </div>

      {/* List */}
      <div className="px-3">
        {filtered.length > 0 && (
          <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between px-1 pb-2 pt-[14px]">
            <span className="font-display text-[16px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-[0.02em] text-ceuta-black">
              {headTitle[tab]}
            </span>
            <span className="font-body text-[12px] text-ceuta-muted">
              {filtered.length} movimientos
            </span>
          </div>
        )}

        {filtered.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState tab={tab} />
        ) : tab === 'rumor' ? (
          <RumorList transfers={filtered} />
        ) : (
          <div className="grid gap-[10px] pb-6 [grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(320px,1fr))]">
            {filtered.map((t, i) => (
              <TransferCard key={t.id} t={t} type={tab} idx={i} />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

<file path="src/features/matches/PartidosPage.tsx">
'use client'

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { findLogo as findLogoUtil } from '@/lib/findLogo'
import { formatMatchDate, shortTeamName, resultLabel } from './matchUtils'
import Link from 'next/link'

interface MatchRow {
  home_team: string
  away_team: string
  date: string
  time: string
  weekday: string
  is_home: boolean
  home_score: number | null
  away_score: number | null
  result: string | null
  tickets_url: string | null
  espn_event_id: string | null
}

function resultBadge(r: string | null) {
  const item = resultLabel(r)
  if (!item) return null
  return <span className={`result-badge ${item.cls}`}>{item.label}</span>
}

interface Props {
  upcoming?: MatchRow[]
  results?: MatchRow[]
  previousSeason?: MatchRow[]
  logos?: Record<string, string>
}

function ResultCard({ r, logos }: { r: MatchRow; logos: Record<string, string> }) {
  function findLogo(name: string): string | null {
    return findLogoUtil(logos, name)
  }
  const { day, mon } = formatMatchDate(r.date)
  const ceutaHome = r.home_team.toLowerCase().includes('ceuta')
  const cls = r.result === 'W' ? 'win' : r.result === 'L' ? 'lose' : 'draw'
  const homeScoreCls = ceutaHome
    ? r.result === 'W' ? 'win' : r.result === 'L' ? 'lose' : ''
    : r.result === 'L' ? 'win' : r.result === 'W' ? 'lose' : ''
  const awayScoreCls = !ceutaHome
    ? r.result === 'W' ? 'win' : r.result === 'L' ? 'lose' : ''
    : r.result === 'L' ? 'win' : r.result === 'W' ? 'lose' : ''

  return (
    <div className={`result-card ${cls}`}>
      <div className="result-card-row">
        <div className="result-date-col">
          <div className="result-day">{day}</div>
          <div className="result-mon">{mon}</div>
        </div>
        <div className="result-teams-col">
          <div className="result-team-row">
            {findLogo(r.home_team) ? (
              // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
              <img src={findLogo(r.home_team)!} alt="" className="size-[18px] shrink-0 object-contain" />
            ) : (
              <div className="size-[18px] shrink-0" />
            )}
            <div className={`result-team-name ${ceutaHome ? 'bold' : ''}`}>
              {shortTeamName(r.home_team)}
            </div>
            <div className={`result-score ${homeScoreCls}`}>{r.home_score}</div>
          </div>
          <div className="result-team-row">
            {findLogo(r.away_team) ? (
              // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
              <img src={findLogo(r.away_team)!} alt="" className="size-[18px] shrink-0 object-contain" />
            ) : (
              <div className="size-[18px] shrink-0" />
            )}
            <div className={`result-team-name ${!ceutaHome ? 'bold' : ''}`}>
              {shortTeamName(r.away_team)}
            </div>
            <div className={`result-score ${awayScoreCls}`}>{r.away_score}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1.5">
          {resultBadge(r.result)}
          {r.espn_event_id && (
            <Link
              href={`/alineacion?eventId=${r.espn_event_id}&match=${encodeURIComponent(`${shortTeamName(r.home_team)} vs ${shortTeamName(r.away_team)}`)}&score=${r.home_score}-${r.away_score}`}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-[6px] border-[1.5px] border-ceuta-black bg-ceuta-black px-2 py-1 no-underline"
            >
              <svg width="10" height="10" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="white" strokeWidth="2.2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
                <rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="2" />
                <path d="M3 9h18M3 15h18M9 3v18" />
              </svg>
              <span className="whitespace-nowrap font-display text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-white">
                Alineación
              </span>
            </Link>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function PartidosPage({ upcoming = [], results = [], previousSeason = [], logos = {} }: Props) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('proximos')

  useEffect(() => {
    const stored = sessionStorage.getItem('partidosTab')
    if (stored) setTab(stored)
  }, [])

  function changePartidosTab(t: string) {
    sessionStorage.setItem('partidosTab', t)
    setTab(t)
  }

  function findLogo(name: string): string | null {
    return findLogoUtil(logos, name)
  }

  // Group previousSeason matches by month label
  const prevByMonth: { label: string; matches: MatchRow[] }[] = []
  for (const m of previousSeason) {
    const label = new Date(m.date)
      .toLocaleString('es-ES', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' })
      .replace(/^\w/, (c) => c.toUpperCase())
    const existing = prevByMonth.find((g) => g.label === label)
    if (existing) existing.matches.push(m)
    else prevByMonth.push({ label, matches: [m] })
  }

  return (
    <div className="page">
      {/* ── HERO ── */}
      <div className="page-hero-full relative mt-2 overflow-hidden bg-ceuta-black px-[18px] pb-[52px] pt-[22px] text-white">
        <div
          className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 right-[-40px] top-0 w-[180px]"
          style={{
            background: 'repeating-linear-gradient(-45deg, white 0 14px, #0A0A0A 14px 28px)',
            clipPath: 'polygon(60px 0, 100% 0, 100% 100%, 0 100%)',
          }}
        />
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-ceuta-red">
          <div className="h-0.5 w-[18px] bg-ceuta-red" />
          Temporada 2026–27 · Liga Hypermotion
        </div>
        <div className="mt-2 font-display text-[38px] font-black uppercase leading-[0.95]">
          PARTIDOS <em className="not-italic text-ceuta-gold">Y</em>
          <br />
          <em className="not-italic text-ceuta-gold">RESULTADOS</em>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="chips mt-4 mb-2">
        {[
          { id: 'proximos', l: 'Próximos' },
          { id: 'resultados', l: 'Resultados' },
          { id: 'temporada-anterior', l: 'Temp. 25/26' },
        ].map((t) => (
          <button
            key={t.id}
            className={`chip ${tab === t.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
            onClick={() => changePartidosTab(t.id)}
          >
            {t.l}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {tab === 'proximos' && (
        <>
          <div className="month-label">
            {upcoming[0]
              ? new Date(upcoming[0].date)
                  .toLocaleString('es-ES', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' })
                  .replace(/^\w/, (c) => c.toUpperCase())
              : 'Próximos partidos'}
          </div>
          {upcoming.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="px-4 py-5 font-display text-[13px] uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-ceuta-muted">
              No hay próximos partidos
            </p>
          ) : (
            upcoming.map((m, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="fixt-card">
                <div className="fixt-head">
                  <div className="fixt-comp">Liga Hypermotion</div>
                  <div className="fixt-date">
                    {m.weekday} · Por confirmar
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="fixt-teams items-center">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    {findLogo(m.home_team) ? (
                      // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                      <img src={findLogo(m.home_team)!} alt="" className="size-7 object-contain" />
                    ) : (
                      <div className="size-7 rounded-full bg-ceuta-bg-mid" />
                    )}
                    <div className={`fixt-tname ${m.is_home ? 'mine' : ''}`}>
                      {shortTeamName(m.home_team)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="fixt-vs">VS</div>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
                    <div className={`fixt-tname away ${!m.is_home ? 'mine' : ''}`}>
                      {shortTeamName(m.away_team)}
                    </div>
                    {findLogo(m.away_team) ? (
                      // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                      <img src={findLogo(m.away_team)!} alt="" className="size-7 object-contain" />
                    ) : (
                      <div className="size-7 rounded-full bg-ceuta-bg-mid" />
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="mt-1.5 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="font-display text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-ceuta-muted">
                    {new Date(m.date).toLocaleDateString('es-ES', {
                      day: 'numeric',
                      month: 'long',
                      timeZone: 'UTC',
                    })}
                    {m.is_home ? ' · A. Murube' : ''}
                  </span>
                  {m.tickets_url && (
                    <a
                      href={m.tickets_url}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noopener noreferrer"
                      className="match-cta px-[10px] py-1 text-[10px]"
                    >
                      Entradas
                    </a>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </>
      )}

      {tab === 'resultados' && (
        <>
          <div className="month-label">Últimos partidos</div>
          {results.map((r, i) => (
            <ResultCard key={i} r={r} logos={logos} />
          ))}
        </>
      )}

      {tab === 'temporada-anterior' && (
        <>
          <div className="px-4 py-3 font-display text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ceuta-muted">
            Temporada 2025–26 · Liga Hypermotion
          </div>
          {previousSeason.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="px-4 py-5 font-display text-[13px] uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-ceuta-muted">
              No hay datos de la temporada anterior
            </p>
          ) : (
            prevByMonth.map((group) => (
              <div key={group.label}>
                <div className="month-label">{group.label}</div>
                {group.matches.map((r, i) => (
                  <ResultCard key={i} r={r} logos={logos} />
                ))}
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
